Agentic AI Kerr实验室：用于磁性Skyrmions的Kerr显微镜实验的LLM代理#

原题：The Agentic AI Kerr Lab: LLM Agents for Kerr Microscopy Experiments with Magnetic Skyrmions
作者：Kilian Leutner、Yuean Zhou、Aditya Kumar、Thomas B. Winkler、Robert Frömter、Hans Fangohr 等 7 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-03
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：磁性斯格明子是拓扑自旋纹理，可以通过磁显微镜直接观察并由场和电流驱动，这使得它们成为基础物理的模型系统和自旋电子器件的候选者。磁光克尔显微镜是一种实时成像的方法；然而，大多数实验使用连续成像、分析和激发的低效循环，其中每一步都需要操作设置的实验人员的判断。在这里，我们介绍了 Agentic AI Kerr 实验室 (AgentKerr)，其中基于大型语言模型的代理在 Kerr 显微镜上自动高效地运行此循环。在 Kerr 图像上训练的卷积…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：skyrmion。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

磁性斯格明子是拓扑自旋纹理，可以通过磁显微镜直接观察并由场和电流驱动，这使得它们成为基础物理的模型系统和自旋电子器件的候选者。磁光克尔显微镜是一种实时成像的方法；然而，大多数实验使用连续成像、分析和激发的低效循环，其中每一步都需要操作设置的实验人员的判断。在这里，我们介绍了 Agentic AI Kerr 实验室 (AgentKerr)，其中基于大型语言模型的代理在 Kerr 显微镜上自动高效地运行此循环。在 Kerr 图像上训练的卷积神经网络不是依赖于语言模型的模式检测，而是将实时图像转换为斯格明子可观测值，例如位置、大小和形状，代理以此为基础做出决策。该架构允许代理安全地操作仪器，因为每个硬件操作都会根据操作策略进行确定性检查，而与发出该操作的代理无关。在没有给出程序的情况下，代理自行决定如何创建、调整和移动斯格明子，并编写自己的分析。该方法可以转移到其他自旋纹理，并指向磁成像实验，在磁成像实验中，研究人员提出问题，代理找到实现产生答案的实验的方法。

English Abstract

Magnetic skyrmions are topological spin textures that can be observed directly by magnetic microscopy and driven by fields and currents, which makes them a model system for fundamental physics and candidates for spintronic devices. Magneto-optical Kerr microscopy is a method to image them in real time; however, most experiments use an inefficient cycle of consecutive imaging, analysis and excitation in which every step requires the judgement of the experimentalist operating the setup. Here we present the Agentic AI Kerr Lab (AgentKerr), in which an agent based on a large language model operates this cycle automatically and efficiently on a Kerr microscope. Rather than relying on pattern detection by a language model, a convolutional neural network trained on Kerr images turns live images into skyrmion observables such as position, size and shape, on which the agent bases its decisions. The architecture lets agents operate the instruments safely, as every hardware action is checked deterministically against an action policy, independently of the agent that issues it. With no procedure given, the agent determines by itself how to create, tune and move skyrmions, and writes its own analyses. The approach can be transferred to other spin textures and points towards magnetic imaging experiments in which the researcher poses the question and the agent finds the way to realize the experiments that yield the answer.

杯盖拓扑神经网络#

原题：Cup and Cap Topological Neural Network
作者：Marta Niedostatek、Ferran Hernandez Caralt、Runyue Wang、Federica Baccini、Lorenzo Giambagli、Pietro Lió 等 7 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-06

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：拓扑深度学习 (TDL) 旨在学习不仅与节点相关的特征，还可以学习与边、三角形和高维单纯形相关的特征。然而，大多数基于边界算子和霍奇拉普拉斯算子的 TDL 方法都存在局限性，即特征和信号不能在每层超过一个维度上提升或降低。为了克服这个限制，在这项工作中，我们建议采用杯子和盖子产品。具体来说，我们制定了 Cup and Cap 拓扑神经网络（CCNN），这是一种拓扑深度学习架构，旨在通过考虑数据中存在的多体交互（例如三角形）来学习基于节点…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

拓扑深度学习 (TDL) 旨在学习不仅与节点相关的特征，还可以学习与边、三角形和高维单纯形相关的特征。然而，大多数基于边界算子和霍奇拉普拉斯算子的 TDL 方法都存在局限性，即特征和信号不能在每层超过一个维度上提升或降低。为了克服这个限制，在这项工作中，我们建议采用杯子和盖子产品。具体来说，我们制定了 Cup and Cap 拓扑神经网络（CCNN），这是一种拓扑深度学习架构，旨在通过考虑数据中存在的多体交互（例如三角形）来学习基于节点的变量（或 0-cochains）。我们通过评估 CCNN 在 TopoBench 数据集上的性能来验证 CCNN，证明其相对于其他简单复杂神经网络的竞争性能。

English Abstract

Topological Deep Learning (TDL) is designed to learn features associated not only with nodes but also with edges, triangles and higher dimensional simplices. However, most TDL approaches, being based on boundary operators and Hodge Laplacians, have the limitation that features and signals cannot be lifted or lowered across more than one dimension per layer. To overcome this limitation, in this work, we propose the adoption of the cup and cap products. Specifically, we formulate the Cup and Cap Topological Neural Network (CCNN), a topological deep learning architecture designed to learn node-based variables (or 0-cochains) by taking into account their many-body interactions (e.g. triangles) present in the data. We validate CCNN by assessing its performance on the TopoBench datasets, demonstrating its competitive performance with respect to other simplicial complex neural networks.

03

具有激子极化波导的神经网络#

原题：Neural Networks with Exciton-Polariton Waveguides
作者：Huawen Xu、Ruiqi Bao、Lingyu Tian、Yusong Gan、Junfeng Gao、Michal Matuszewski 等 8 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-05
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配6

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：我们提出并数值研究了一种使用激子极化波导网格的神经网络方案。这些使得任意可编程层间耦合适合于受限玻尔兹曼机器架构，其中极化子相位充当随机网络变量。我们表明，可以使用无监督的赫布和反赫布更新来训练这样的网络。这允许实现联想记忆，网络从不完美的输入中恢复学习的模式。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出并数值研究了一种使用激子极化波导网格的神经网络方案。这些使得任意可编程层间耦合适合于受限玻尔兹曼机器架构，其中极化子相位充当随机网络变量。我们表明，可以使用无监督的赫布和反赫布更新来训练这样的网络。这允许实现联想记忆，网络从不完美的输入中恢复学习的模式。

English Abstract

We propose and numerically study a scheme for neural networks using exciton-polariton waveguide meshes. These enable arbitrary programmable interlayer couplings suited to a restricted Boltzmann machine architecture where polariton phases act as stochastic network variables. We show that such a network can be trained using unsupervised Hebbian and anti-Hebbian updates. This allows the implementation of an associative memory, where the network recovers learned patterns from imperfect inputs.

以证据为基础的大语言模型指导钙钛矿太阳能电池的设计和优化#

原题：Evidence-grounded large language models guide perovskite solar cell design and optimization
作者：YanKai Chen、Zhi Wan、ZiHao Li、Tao Jing。arXiv · cs.AI，2026-11-03

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

使用大型语言模型来解释实验证据、设计判别性实验并制定有充分支持的机械解释仍然是材料研究中的核心挑战。在这里，我们建立了 PSM-Bench，这是一个由钙钛矿研究人员开发的包含 800 个问题的基准，用于评估钙钛矿光伏发电的科学推理。我们还介绍了 OpenPerov，这是一个专家知情的语言模型框架，可以从 4,532 篇文章重建的专家校准证据记录中学习；这些记录将实验策略、控制和观察与机械解释联系起来。结构化证据适应优于传统问答训练，OpenPerov Flash 在 13 个闭卷系统中排名第一。 OpenPerov Pro 通过使用检索到的证据修改选定的段落，进一步提高了科学掌握程度，从 87.6 提高到 89.1。对于后续研究得出的问题，这两个模型的得分也超过了专家撰写的答案。在自组装单层设计的实验测试中，OpenPerov 选择了 K 类似物，其性能优于专家首选的 Na 类似物。采用所选触点的器件在 85 ^circC 下暗热老化 1,000 小时后，功率转换效率达到 27.05%，并保留了 96.74% 的稳态参考效率。这些结果表明，重建的科学推理为构建解释实验和为材料设计提供信息的领域模型提供了有效的基础。

English Abstract

Using large language models to interpret experimental evidence, design discriminating experiments and formulate well-supported mechanistic explanations remains a central challenge in materials research. Here we establish PSM-Bench, an 800-question benchmark developed by perovskite researchers to evaluate scientific reasoning in perovskite photovoltaics. We also introduce OpenPerov, an expert-informed language-model framework that learns from expert-calibrated evidence records reconstructed from 4,532 articles; these records link experimental strategies, controls and observations to mechanistic interpretations. Structured-evidence adaptation outperformed conventional question-answer training, and OpenPerov Flash ranked first among 13 closed-book systems. OpenPerov Pro raised Scientific Mastery further, from 87.6 to 89.1, by revising selected passages with retrieved evidence. On questions derived from subsequent studies, both models also outscored expert-authored answers. In an experimental test of self-assembled monolayer design, OpenPerov selected a K analogue that outperformed the Na analogue preferred by experts. Devices incorporating the selected contact reached a power conversion efficiency of 27.05% and retained 96.74% of their steady-state reference efficiency after 1,000 h of dark thermal ageing at 85 ^circC. These results show that reconstructed scientific reasoning provides an effective basis for building domain models that interpret experiments and inform materials design.

机器学习量子数据加速量子蒙特卡罗#

原题：Machine learning quantum data to accelerate quantum Monte Carlo
作者：Tong Jiang、Andrew Zhao、Shashwat Kumar、Jigyasa Nigam、Eliott Rosenberg、Ryan Babbush 等 9 人。arXiv · physics，2026-11-06
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配5

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：模拟分子和材料中的强相关电子仍然是量子计算的核心目标。一种有前途的策略是使用量子处理器来准备引导经典量子蒙特卡罗（QMC）的试验波函数。然而，重复处理量子测量的巨大计算成本严重限制了这种方法的实际可扩展性。在这里，我们通过将量子测量数据压缩到机器学习模型中来克服这一瓶颈，该模型有效地提供 QMC 所需的波函数重叠。这将量子经典 QMC 加速了几个数量级，从而能够对多达 40 个量子位进行化学精确模拟，在具有相当精度的情况下，将之前硬件演…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

模拟分子和材料中的强相关电子仍然是量子计算的核心目标。一种有前途的策略是使用量子处理器来准备引导经典量子蒙特卡罗（QMC）的试验波函数。然而，重复处理量子测量的巨大计算成本严重限制了这种方法的实际可扩展性。在这里，我们通过将量子测量数据压缩到机器学习模型中来克服这一瓶颈，该模型有效地提供 QMC 所需的波函数重叠。这将量子经典 QMC 加速了几个数量级，从而能够对多达 40 个量子位进行化学精确模拟，在具有相当精度的情况下，将之前硬件演示的规模扩大了一倍多。我们使用 Google Willow 处理器的测量结果展示了我们对氢链和强相关铁硫簇的方法。通过在经典协处理器上实现量子态信息的高效重用，我们的框架解决了混合量子算法中的一个基本瓶颈，该瓶颈甚至会持续到容错时代。

English Abstract

Simulating strongly correlated electrons in molecules and materials remains a central goal for quantum computing. A promising strategy uses quantum processors to prepare trial wavefunctions that guide classical quantum Monte Carlo (QMC). However, the enormous computational cost of repeatedly processing quantum measurements has severely limited the practical scalability of this approach. Here, we overcome this bottleneck by compressing quantum measurement data into machine-learning models that efficiently provide the wavefunction overlaps needed by QMC. This accelerates quantum-classical QMC by orders of magnitude, enabling chemically accurate simulations on up to 40 qubits, more than doubling the scale of previous hardware demonstrations with comparable accuracy. We demonstrate our approach on hydrogen chains and strongly correlated iron-sulfur clusters using measurements from Google's Willow processor. By enabling efficient reuse of quantum-state information on the classical coprocessor, our framework addresses a fundamental bottleneck in hybrid quantum algorithms that will persist even into the fault-tolerant era.

06

基于化学成分和结构的磁交换小数据物理信息机器学习模型#

原题：Small-data physics-informed ML model for magnetic exchange based on chemical composition and structure
作者：Brice Kengni Zanguim、Thibaut Véry、Marie-Bernadette Lepetit。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-04
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配3

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：在本文中，我们提出了一种基于密集神经网络群的集成模型，能够以小于 1% 的误差预测海森堡交换相互作用。该模型使用从头算薛定谔方程的输入，即化合物结构和化学成分。在第一项工作中，我们对六方锰酸盐家族的几种化合物训练了模型，并能够以类似的精度推断出随机选择几何形状的同一家族其他化合物的 J 值，从而表明我们准确地了解了化学成分和结构对有效磁积分 J 的影响。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在本文中，我们提出了一种基于密集神经网络群的集成模型，能够以小于 1% 的误差预测海森堡交换相互作用。该模型使用从头算薛定谔方程的输入，即化合物结构和化学成分。在第一项工作中，我们对六方锰酸盐家族的几种化合物训练了模型，并能够以类似的精度推断出随机选择几何形状的同一家族其他化合物的 J 值，从而表明我们准确地了解了化学成分和结构对有效磁积分 J 的影响。

English Abstract

In this paper we propose an ensemble model based on a swarm of dense neural networks, able to predict Heisenberg exchange interactions within less than 1% error. The model uses the inputs of the ab-initio Schrödinger equation, i.e. the compound structure and chemical composition. In this first work, we trained the model on a few compounds of the hexagonal manganite family and were able to infer, with similar accuracy, the J values for other compounds of the same family at randomly chosen geometries, thus showing that we accurately learned the effect of the chemical composition and structure on effective magnetic integrals J.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

训练后量化 (PTQ) 降低了大型语言模型 (LLM) 的计算成本，但它可能会引入传统指标（例如准确性和复杂性）无法捕获的内部结构和行为变化。我们提出了 TopoQuant，一个拓扑框架，它使用拓扑数据分析（TDA）来量化自注意力权重的结构漂移，并将其与量化法学硕士的行为变化联系起来。在四个 LLM 系列和多个量化级别中，我们发现拓扑漂移在中等压缩级别下保持稳定，但在 4 位精度以下急剧增加，密切跟踪注意力一致性的下降。我们的结果揭示了拓扑漂移和注意力一致性之间的密切关系，表明模型参数的结构变化密切反映了量化下的行为退化。我们进一步证明，拓扑特征可以使用轻量级机器学习模型准确预测注意力一致性，实现高达 0.85 的 R2 分数。预测注意力一致性可以有效评估量化 LLM 中的行为一致性，从而无需运行昂贵的下游任务即可检测量化引起的退化。结果表明，拓扑漂移为量化引起的退化提供了有效的预警信号，并且可以帮助识别安全压缩限制，而无需进行广泛的下游评估。

English Abstract

Post-training quantization (PTQ) reduces the com putational cost of large language models (LLMs), but it can introduce internal structural and behavioral changes that are not captured by traditional metrics such as accuracy and per plexity. We present TopoQuant, a topological framework that uses topological data analysis (TDA) to quantify structural drift in self-attention weights and relate it to behavioral changes in quantized LLMs. Across four LLM families and multiple quantization levels, we show that topological drift remains stable at moderate compression levels but increases sharply below 4 bit precision, closely tracking declines in attention agreement. Our results reveal a strong relationship between topological drift and attention agreement, indicating that structural changes in model parameters closely reflect behavioral degradation under quantization. We further demonstrate that topological features can accurately predict attention consistency using lightweight machine learning models, achieving R2 scores of up to 0.85. Predicting attention agreement enables efficient assessment of be havioral consistency in quantized LLMs, allowing quantization induced degradation to be detected without running expensive downstream tasks. The results indicate that topological drift pro vides an efficient early-warning signal for quantization-induced degradation and can help identify safe compression limits without extensive downstream evaluation.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

等摩尔的NaCa(BH₄)₃的理论氢容量为11.24wt.%，分解热预计介于NaBH₄和Ca(BH₄)₃之间，但它从未被制备出来，也没有晶体结构的报道。迄今为止，对其的预测是在其较重的同系物采用的钙钛矿家族中建立的。这里这个假设被删除了。六个候选框架由三个独立来源组装而成——实验确定的 ABX₃ 硼氢化物，数据驱动结构预测的扭曲钙钛矿，以及对结构数据库的无偏见搜索，其中所有 2238 个生成的候选框架都是松弛的，没有排除任何一个——在固定的电荷中性组成下，每个框架的阳离子排列被详尽地枚举，420 个装饰减少到 118 个对称不等价构型，用机器学习潜力并从第一原则解决。最稳定的结构不是钙钛矿。它是空间群Cm的单斜骨架，仅通过无限制搜索才能达到，在-4.185640 eV/原子，比事先可用的最佳骨架低7.67 meV/原子；两个化学上不相关的供体原型在其上收敛至 0.076 meV/原子，其有序的阳离子排列是其自身系列的基态。其声子谱在超晶胞精确解析的任何波矢量上均不带有虚模，并且其松弛离子弹性张量是正定的，而正交钙钛矿候选物在-2.401 THz 范围内不稳定。这种对比为所报道的未能获得钙钛矿形式的组合物提供了物理原因，并且此处报道的模拟衍射图通过母相和竞争框架均不产生的七次反射来识别预测的框架。

English Abstract

Equimolar NaCa(BH₄)₃ offers a theoretical hydrogen capacity of 11.24 wt.% and a decomposition enthalpy expected to fall between those of NaBH₄ and Ca(BH₄)₂, but it has never been prepared and no crystal structure has been reported. Predictions for it have so far been built within the perovskite family that its heavier homologues adopt. Here that assumption is removed. Six candidate frameworks were assembled from three independent sources --- experimentally determined ABX₃ borohydrides, a distorted perovskite from data-driven structure prediction, and an unbiased search over a structural database in which all 2238 generated candidates were relaxed with none excluded --- and at fixed charge-neutral composition the cation arrangement of every framework was enumerated exhaustively, 420 decorations reducing to 118 symmetry-inequivalent configurations, screened with a machine-learning potential and settled from first principles. The most stable structure is not a perovskite. It is a monoclinic framework of space group Cm, reached only by the unrestricted search, at -4.185640 eV/atom, 7.67 meV/atom below the best framework available beforehand; two chemically unrelated donor prototypes converge on it to 0.076 meV/atom, and its ordered cation arrangement is the ground state of its own series. Its phonon spectrum carries no imaginary mode at any wavevector the supercell resolves exactly and its relaxed-ion elastic tensor is positive definite, whereas the orthorhombic perovskite candidate is unstable to -2.401 THz. That contrast supplies a physical reason for the reported failure to obtain this composition in perovskite form, and the simulated diffraction pattern reported here identifies the predicted framework by seven reflections that neither parent phase nor the competing framework produces.

用于电子结构哈密顿预测的直接积空间中的等变量深度学习#

原题：Equivariant Deep Learning in Direct-Product Space for Electronic-Structure Hamiltonian Prediction
作者：Shi Yin、Zujian Dai、Xinyang Pan、Lixin He。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-06

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：大多数现有的汉密尔顿预测等变量架构在不可约球面张量的直和空间中传播特征，而目标汉密尔顿变量自然是局部轨道和自旋基的直接乘积表示中的算子块。这种表示不匹配分割了相邻的哈密顿块，模糊了轨道耦合的直接操作，并将特征组织成不规则的角动量组件，这些组件需要许多变换，并且与有效的硬件执行和内存管理不协调。为了解决表示不匹配和计算瓶颈，我们提出了DP-Learner ，这是一个等变学习框架，直接在直接乘积空间中的类汉密尔顿算子块上执行消息传递。从理论…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

大多数现有的汉密尔顿预测等变量架构在不可约球面张量的直和空间中传播特征，而目标汉密尔顿变量自然是局部轨道和自旋基的直接乘积表示中的算子块。这种表示不匹配分割了相邻的哈密顿块，模糊了轨道耦合的直接操作，并将特征组织成不规则的角动量组件，这些组件需要许多变换，并且与有效的硬件执行和内存管理不协调。为了解决表示不匹配和计算瓶颈，我们提出了DP-Learner ，这是一个等变学习框架，直接在直接乘积空间中的类汉密尔顿算子块上执行消息传递。从理论上讲，我们提出了基于Lie-generator的定向嵌入和不变加权左-右积和收缩，这些积和收缩保留了与SO （ 3 ） ， SU （ 2 ）和空间反演的等方差，而没有显式的Clebsch-Gordan分解或反重建。在方法论上，我们将此原理实例化为变换器架构，其中类似哈密顿的特征矩阵或张量通过直积空间中的等变量运算符演化，而非线性不变标量路由控制矩阵单元、方向传播、注意力和环境嵌入式上下文交互的结构化门控。我们进一步构建MP-HAM-110K ，这是一个大规模的汉密尔顿数据集，由超过110,000个材料结构组成，涵盖68个元素，同时包含汉密尔顿和波函数数据。在MP-HAM-110K和Materials-HAM-SOC上的实验表明，与最先进的方法相比， DP-Learner显著提高了哈密顿预测的准确性和推理效率。

English Abstract

Most existing equivariant architectures for Hamiltonian prediction propagate features in direct-sum spaces of irreducible spherical tensors, whereas the target Hamiltonian is naturally an operator block in a direct-product representation over local orbital and spin bases. This representation mismatch fragments contiguous Hamiltonian blocks, obscures direct manipulation of orbital couplings, and organizes features into irregular angular-momentum components that require numerous transformations and are poorly aligned with efficient hardware execution and memory management. To address both the representation mismatch and computational bottleneck, we propose DP-Learner, an equivariant learning framework that performs message passing directly on Hamiltonian-like operator blocks in the direct-product space. Theoretically, we propose Lie-generator-based directional embedding and invariantly weighted left--right products and contractions that preserve equivariance to SO(3), SU(2), and spatial inversions, without explicit Clebsch--Gordan decomposition or inverse reconstruction. Methodologically, we instantiate this principle as a Transformer architecture, in which Hamiltonian-like feature matrices or tensors evolve through equivariant operators in the direct-product space, while non-linear invariant scalar routing controls structured gating over matrix units, directional propagation, attention, and environment-embedded context interactions. We further construct MP-HAM-110K, a large-scale Hamiltonian dataset comprising more than 110,000 material structures covering 68 elements with both Hamiltonian and wavefunction data. Experiments on MP-HAM-110K and Materials-HAM-SOC demonstrate that DP-Learner dramatically improves Hamiltonian prediction accuracy and inference efficiency over the state-of-the-art method.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

自旋 1/2 kagome 海森堡反铁磁体是量子自旋液体研究的核心模型，但其基态特性仍未得到解决。我们使用神经网络量子态 (NQS) 和 kagome 几何感知消息传递卷积在包含多达 300 个自旋的二维 tori 上执行大规模变分蒙特卡洛计算，并为 kagome 模型实现极低的变分能量。自旋和二聚体结构因子没有显示出磁性或价键长程有序的证据。此外，主要的对称解析自旋和二聚体相关性表现出与狄拉克自旋液体的新兴 SO(6) 单极多重态一致的常见缩放行为。最后，随着系统规模的增长，具有 Gutzwiller 投影平均场状态的优化 NQS 的保真度越来越有利于狄拉克状态，而不是竞争的 Z2 候选状态。总之，这些互补的结果为 kagome 海森堡反铁磁体中的 U(1) 狄拉克自旋液体基态提供了证据。

English Abstract

The spin-1/2 kagome Heisenberg antiferromagnet is a central model in the study of quantum spin liquids, yet its ground-state properties remain unresolved. We perform large-scale variational Monte-Carlo calculations using neural-network quantum states (NQSs) with kagome-geometry-aware message-passing convolution on two-dimensional tori containing up to 300 spins, and achieve exceptionally low variational energies for the kagome model. The spin and dimer structure factors show no evidence of magnetic or valence-bond long-range order. Moreover, the dominant symmetry-resolved spin and dimer correlations exhibit a common scaling behavior consistent with the emergent SO(6) monopole multiplet of the Dirac spin liquid. Finally, the fidelity of the optimized NQSs with Gutzwiller-projected mean-field states increasingly favors the Dirac state over the competing Z₂ candidates as the system size grows. Together, these complementary results provide evidence for a U(1) Dirac spin-liquid ground state in the kagome Heisenberg antiferromagnet.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们引入了 UnIPH，一种等变图神经网络，旨在联合预测原子结构的原子间势和紧束缚 (TB) 哈密顿量。 UnIPH 使用密度泛函理论 (DFT) 计算进行训练，然后使用 Wannier 投影来预测能量和力作为节点级目标，并使用 Wannier 投影 TB 哈密顿量作为边缘级目标。这使得能够以接近 DFT 水平的精度对耦合机械电子响应进行物理一致的建模。在分别针对双层石墨烯和单层 MoS2 的 DFT 数据分别训练两个 UnIPH 模型后，我们发现这些模型捕获了关键的形变电子效应：在扭曲的双层石墨烯中，经过训练的 UnIPH 揭示了层间间距、面内应变和面外波纹如何驱动孤立的平带形成，并进一步表明，调制基底相互作用强度甚至可以在远离魔角（大约 1.1 度）的情况下生成平带。对于单层 MoS2，UnIPH 准确地再现了声子色散和应变相关的带隙演化，并进一步预测非均匀基底如何创建局部应变袋，从而在空间上调制导带和价带边缘。

English Abstract

We introduce UnIPH, an equivariant graph neural network designed to jointly predict both interatomic potentials and tight-binding (TB) Hamiltonians for an atomic structure. UnIPH is trained using density functional theory (DFT) calculations followed by Wannier projection to predict energies and forces as node-level targets and Wannier-projected TB Hamiltonians as edge-level targets. This enables physically consistent modeling of coupled mechanical-electronic responses with near-DFT-level accuracy. After separately training two UnIPH models on DFT data for bilayer graphene and monolayer MoS2, respectively, we find that these models capture key deformation-electronic effects: in twisted bilayer graphene, the trained UnIPH reveals how interlayer spacing, in-plane strain, and out-of-plane corrugation drive isolated flat-band formation, and further shows that modulating substrate interaction strength can generate flat bands even away from the magic angle (approximately 1.1 degrees). For monolayer MoS2, UnIPH accurately reproduces phonon dispersions and strain-dependent band-gap evolution, and further predicts how a non-uniform substrate creates localized strain pockets that spatially modulate the conduction- and valence-band edges.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

硼烯是一种单层硼，由于其独特的性质（包括多态性以及在量子技术和能量转换和存储方面的潜在应用）而在二维材料中脱颖而出。然而，其应用受到硼烯与金属基底的强结合的阻碍，这阻碍了独立硼烯的实现。因此，非常需要与硼烯结合可调节的替代底物。由于在金属上合成硼烯期间会产生硼化物层，因此硼化物为硼烯合成提供了潜在的替代底物。在这项工作中，我们使用密度泛函理论 (DFT) 计算和机器学习 (ML) 研究了两个硼烯相（v1/6 和 v1/9）的硼烯和 1356 硼化物表面之间的结合能和相互作用。重要的是，将 ML 预测的电荷转移作为输入特征纳入后续 ML 模型中，可以实现结合能的物理信息预测。对四个代表性案例进行了额外的 DFT 计算，以验证 ML 预测。 SHAP 分析表明，大多数关键描述符与离域电子相关，突出了电子特性在控制结合强度中的作用。结合 DFT 和 ML 筛选，分别确定了 v1/6 和 v1/9 相的 65 个和 73 个有前景的硼化物表面。铝基和银基硼化物，包括 AlB2 和 AgB2，成为这两个相的主要候选材料。计算预测的候选底物和推导的设计指南为底物选择提供了一种基于电荷转移的机器学习策略，并代表了实验实现独立式硼烯的有希望的步骤。

English Abstract

Borophene, a monolayer of boron, is distinctive among two-dimensional materials owing to its unique properties, including polymorphism and potential applications in quantum technologies and energy conversion and storage. Nevertheless, its applications are hindered by the strong binding of borophene to metal substrates, which impedes the realization of freestanding borophene. Alternative substrates with tunable binding to borophene are therefore highly desirable. Since boride layers arise during borophene synthesis on metals, borides provide potential alternative substrates for borophene synthesis. In this work, we investigate the binding energies and interactions between borophene and 1356 boride surfaces for two borophene phases (v1/6 and v1/9), using density functional theory (DFT) calculations and machine learning (ML). Importantly, incorporating ML-predicted charge transfer as an input feature to a subsequent ML model enables physics-informed predictions of binding energies. Additional DFT calculations on four representative cases were performed to validate the ML predictions. SHAP analysis reveals that the majority of key descriptors are associated with delocalized electrons, highlighting the role of electronic properties in governing binding strength. Combined DFT and ML screening identifies 65 and 73 promising boride surfaces for the v1/6 and v1/9 phases, respectively. Al- and Ag-based borides, including AlB2 and AgB2, emerge as predominant candidates for both phases. The computationally predicted candidate substrates and the deduced design guidelines provide a charge-transfer-informed ML strategy for substrate selection and represent promising steps toward the experimental realization of freestanding borophene.

中文摘要

基于磁性随机存取存储器 (MRAM) 的自旋电子突触设备是模拟神经形态架构中快速发展的一类。然而，在缩放 MRAM 中获得提高神经网络推理精度所需的多权重或模拟行为仍然是一个挑战。在这项研究中，我们展示并调整了由自旋轨道扭矩驱动的 300 毫米晶圆级磁性隧道结人工突触中多达八个不同的电阻水平。通过改变所施加的面内场，通过磁畴壁成核和钉扎出现多重量电阻状态。这些多权重状态随机出现，并且可以通过迭代编程算法来解决，以对特定电阻进行编程。电磁模拟表明，多种状态是由面内场和施加电压介导的畴壁成核造成的。如果所有设备提供相同数量的多权重状态，则使用 MNIST 数据集在神经网络中实现测量的多权重设备可产生 96.1% 的推理精度。当包括实验性设备间差异时，准确度为 48.6%。总的来说，这项工作展示了一条通往多权重人工突触 MRAM 的道路，建立了一条通往基于自旋电子学的可扩展神经形态系统的道路。

English Abstract

Spintronic synaptic devices based on magnetic random access memory (MRAM) are a rapidly evolving class within the analog neuromorphic architecture. However, obtaining the multi-weight or analog behavior desired for improved neural network inference accuracy in scaled MRAM remains a challenge. In this study, we demonstrate and tune up to eight distinct resistance levels in 300 mm wafer-scale magnetic tunnel junction artificial synapses driven by spin orbit torque. By varying the applied in-plane field, multi-weight resistance states emerge through domain wall nucleation and pinning. These multi-weight states appear stochastically and can be addressed with an iterative programming algorithm to program a specific resistance. Electromagnetic simulations indicate the multiple states are due to domain wall nucleation mediated by in-plane field and applied voltage. Implementation of the measured multi-weight devices in a neural network yields a 96.1% inference accuracy using the MNIST dataset if all devices provide the same number of multi-weight states. When experimental device-to-device variation is included, the accuracy is 48.6%. Overall, this work demonstrates a route towards multi-weight artificial synapse MRAM, establishing a path towards scalable neuromorphic systems based on spintronics.

中文摘要

晶体生成模型可以探索一组固定晶体原型之外的成分和原子排列，但它们对特定材料族的有用性取决于生成的结构是否保留定义的拓扑结构以及它们的预测特性在整个采样化学过程中是否保持可靠。在这里，使用预训练的扩散模型来生成候选 ABX₃ 钙钛矿并检查其结构、化学和电子特性分布。基于连通性的滤波器将三维角共享钙钛矿与共享相同标称 ABX₃ 化学计量的结构区分开来。可接受的集合包含大量 B 位点多重性，包括此处使用的双原型替代参考无法访问的有序双钙钛矿配置。相对于材料项目，生成的集合富含氯化物、溴化物和碘化物成分，而所有生成的 B 位元素已在该数据库中表示。在下游 MP 训练的 ALIGNN 尺度上，预测的带隙分布向 1.4 eV 移动，但目标窗口分数 (8.2%) 接近 MP 参考分数 (7.3%)；单独的 MatterGen 和 ALIGNN 标签源阻止这种比较建立目标条件富集。 ALIGNN 模型在公式分组的随机测试分割上表现良好，但在故意严格的 B 站点不相交测试上，其表现并不优于恒定基线。对六个生成结构的第一原理计算表明，松弛可以改变结构分类，并且预测和计算的间隙可能存在明显差异。这些结果确定了晶体扩散提供的化学空间覆盖范围，以及在其输出被视为候选材料之前所需的结构和预测检查。

English Abstract

Crystal-generation models can explore compositions and atomic arrangements beyond a fixed set of crystal prototypes, but their usefulness for a specific materials family depends on whether the generated structures retain the defining topology and whether their predicted properties remain reliable across the sampled chemistry. Here, a pretrained diffusion model is used to generate candidate ABX₃ perovskites and to examine their structural, chemical and electronic-property distributions. A connectivity-based filter distinguishes three-dimensional corner-sharing perovskites from structures that share the same nominal ABX₃ stoichiometry. The accepted set contains substantial B-site multiplicity, including ordered double-perovskite configurations that are inaccessible to the two-prototype substitution reference used here. The generated set is enriched in chloride-, bromide- and iodide-containing compositions relative to the Materials Project, while all generated B-site elements are already represented in that database. On the downstream MP-trained ALIGNN scale, the predicted bandgap distribution is shifted towards 1.4 eV, but the target-window fraction (8.2%) is close to the MP reference fraction (7.3%); the separate MatterGen and ALIGNN label sources prevent this comparison from establishing target-condition enrichment. An ALIGNN model performs well on a formula-grouped random test split but does not outperform a constant baseline on a deliberately severe B-site-disjoint test. First-principles calculations on six generated structures show that relaxation can alter structural classification and that predicted and calculated gaps can differ appreciably. These results identify both the chemical-space coverage offered by crystal diffusion and the structural and predictive checks needed before its outputs can be treated as materials candidates.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习的原子间势极大地扩展了分子动力学模拟的范围，但复杂的化学环境通常需要对远程电子响应进行明确的处理，包括电荷转移和极化。基于物理的柔性电荷模型捕获了这些效应，但需要在每个时间步骤进行全局非线性电荷平衡以获得玻恩-奥本海默能量和力。这个过程的计算成本很高，并且可能导致不稳定，除非紧密收敛。在这里，我们介绍了具有任意局部非线性和远程多极静电的灵活电荷模型的影子分子动力学框架。我们将精确的玻恩-奥本海默势替换为附近的影子势，该影子势是通过精确平衡部分线性化的电子能量泛函而获得的。这提供了保守力并将全局非线性平衡简化为可以以低成本直接求解的准对角线性问题。通过扩展拉格朗日动力学传播的辅助动态电子自由度捕获长程电子响应并定义能量泛函线性化的参考状态。对超过 100,000 个原子进行的 GPU 加速模拟表明，阴影势能紧密遵循紧密收敛的玻恩-奥本海默表面，同时以接近固定电荷模型的计算成本保持长期稳定性。这些进展为机器学习的原子间势建立了一个有效且广泛适用的框架，该框架明确地结合了长程静电相互作用和依赖于环境的电子响应。

English Abstract

Machine-learned interatomic potentials have greatly expanded the reach of molecular dynamics simulations, but complex chemical environments often require explicit treatment of long-range electronic response, including charge transfer and polarization. Physics-based flexible-charge models capture these effects but require global nonlinear charge equilibration at every time step to obtain Born-Oppenheimer energies and forces. This process is computationally expensive and can lead to instabilities unless tightly converged. Here, we introduce a shadow molecular dynamics framework for flexible-charge models with arbitrary local nonlinearities and long-range multipolar electrostatics. We replace the exact Born-Oppenheimer potential with a nearby shadow potential obtained by exactly equilibrating a partially linearized electronic energy functional. This provides conservative forces and reduces the global nonlinear equilibration to a quasi-diagonal linear problem that can be solved directly at low cost. Auxiliary dynamical electronic degrees of freedom, propagated through an extended-Lagrangian dynamics, capture the long-range electronic response and define the reference state for the linearization of the energy functional. GPU-accelerated simulations with over 100,000 atoms demonstrate that the shadow potential closely follows the tightly converged Born-Oppenheimer surface, while maintaining long-term stability at a computational cost approaching that of a fixed-charge model. These advances establish an efficient and broadly applicable framework for machine-learned interatomic potentials that explicitly incorporate long-range electrostatic interactions and environment-dependent electronic response.

中文摘要

金属具有吸引人的特性，但将其稳定在单层相中会带来合成挑战。最近的一项实验表明，范德华挤压法可以稳定MoS2夹层中的某些金属。这项开创性的工作激发了系统研究，但这些研究在实验上是不切实际的，而第一原理建模仍然令人望而却步。在这里，我们使用通用机器学习原子间电位，构建了1620个金属夹层异质结构，包含6个不同的夹层和45种金属。我们进行了声子计算，揭示了1208个动态稳定的结构，并在结构滤波后将其缩小到331个。我们发现硒基过渡金属二硫族化合物能有效地稳定金属。具体而言，六边形和屈曲六边形晶格表现出最大的稳定性。我们进一步使用密度泛函理论分子动力学模拟在室温下评估了选定异质结构的热稳定性。通过揭示控制金属稳定性的物理和化学因素，我们的研究结果提供了系统的见解，以指导和加速未来应用的合成。

English Abstract

Metallenes have appealing properties, but stabilizing them in a monolayer phase poses synthesis challenges. A recent experiment showed that the van der Waals squeezing method can stabilize certain metallenes in a MoS2 sandwich. This pioneering work motivates systematic studies, but such studies are experimentally impractical, while first-principles modeling remains prohibitive. Here, using universal machine-learning interatomic potentials, we constructed 1620 metallene sandwich heterostructures containing 6 different sandwich layers and 45 metals. We performed phonon calculations, which revealed 1208 dynamically stable structures and narrowed them to 331 after structural filtering. We found that Se-based transition-metal dichalcogenides stabilize metallenes effectively. Specifically, hexagonal and buckled hexagonal crystal lattices exhibit the greatest stability. We further evaluated the thermal stability of selected heterostructures with density-functional theory molecular dynamics simulations at room temperature. By uncovering the physical and chemical factors that govern metallenes stabilization, our results provide systematic insights to guide and accelerate synthesis for future applications.

原题：Machine learning surrogates for multi-geometry EDGE2D-EIRENE studies of MAST Upgrade

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

二维边缘等离子体传输模拟提供了偏滤器等离子体行为的详细描述，但其计算成本限制了大参数扫描和迭代建模。我们提供了一个包含 23,379 个用于 MAST 升级 (MAST-U) 的融合 EDGE2D-EIRENE 模拟的数据库，涵盖三种偏滤器磁性配置、分界线密度和输入功率的变化、氘燃料位置以及氮注入速率和位置。独立的多层感知器代理接受十二个标量输出的训练，包括偏滤器前端位置、目标电子温度和核心边缘边界量。在保留测试集上，代理达到 0.9462 ≤ R² ≤ 0.9994，大大优于线性回归基线。密集替代扫描量化了碳辐射前沿运动对分隔壁密度、偏滤器几何形状和加料位置的依赖性。氮播种扫描揭示了对播种位置的强烈依赖性，高场侧播种迅速降低了核心边界电子温度，而偏滤器播种几乎没有产生响应。在 GPU 上对 786,432 个操作点的所有 12 个输出进行批量推理需要 1.76 秒，而使用四个 CPU 内核进行单独的 EDGE2D-EIRENE 模拟的中位时间为 2.61 小时。这些模型能够快速比较几何形状和源位置对采样模拟域内偏滤器和堆芯边界条件的影响。

English Abstract

Two-dimensional edge plasma transport simulations provide a detailed description of divertor plasma behaviour, but their computational cost limits large parameter scans and iterative modelling. We present a database of 23,379 converged EDGE2D-EIRENE simulations for MAST Upgrade (MAST-U), spanning three divertor magnetic configurations, variations in separatrix density and input power, deuterium fuelling locations, and nitrogen seeding rates and locations. Independent multilayer perceptron surrogates are trained for twelve scalar outputs, including divertor front positions, target electron temperature and core-edge boundary quantities. On a held-out test set, the surrogates achieve 0.9462 ≤ R² ≤ 0.9994, substantially outperforming linear regression baselines. Dense surrogate scans quantify the dependence of carbon radiation front motion on separatrix density, divertor geometry and fuelling location. Nitrogen seeding scans reveal a strong dependence on seeding location, with high-field-side seeding rapidly reducing core-boundary electron temperature, while divertor seeding produces little response. Batch inference for all twelve outputs at 786,432 operating points takes 1.76 s on GPU, compared with a median wall time of 2.61 hours using four CPU cores for separate EDGE2D-EIRENE simulations. These models enable rapid comparison of geometry and source-location effects on divertor and core-boundary conditions within the sampled simulation domain.

利用可转移深量子蒙特卡罗从头开始模拟激发态势能面#

原题：Ab initio simulation of excited-state potential energy surfaces with transferable deep quantum Monte Carlo
作者：Zeno Schätzle、P. Bernát Szabó、Alice Cuzzocrea、Matěj Mezera、Frank Noé。Nature Computational Science，2026-11-06

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：介绍了分子激发态的可转移深度学习量子蒙特卡罗。 ansatz 共享参数，代表分子几何形状和状态的波函数，从而降低了势能表面建模的成本。

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

介绍了分子激发态的可转移深度学习量子蒙特卡罗。 ansatz 共享参数，代表分子几何形状和状态的波函数，从而降低了势能表面建模的成本。

English Abstract

The accurate quantum chemical calculation of excited states is a challenging task, often requiring computationally demanding methods. When entire ground and excited potential energy surfaces (PESs) are desired, for instance, to predict the interaction of light excitation and structural changes, one is often forced to use cheaper computational methods at the cost of reduced accuracy. Here we introduce a method for the geometrically transferable optimization of neural network wavefunctions that leverages weight sharing and a dynamical ordering of electronic states. Our method enables the efficient prediction of ground- and excited-state PESs and their intersections at the highest accuracy, demonstrating up to two orders of magnitude cost reduction compared with single-point calculations. We validate our approach on four challenging excited-state PESs, namely, ethylene, the carbon dimer, the methylenimmonium cation and a rubredoxin active site model containing 96 electrons, illustrating the potential of transferable deep-learning quantum Monte Carlo as a practical framework for studying electronic excitations in molecules.

Quantum 3xplorer：用于下一代量子材料建模的 AI 原生引擎#

原题：Quantum 3xplorer: AI-Native Engine for Next-Generation Quantum Materials Modeling
作者：Vsevolod Biryukov、Sergey V. Barabash、Pranab Kumar Das、Jack Deslippe、Mauro Del Ben、Felipe H. da Jornada 等 15 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-02

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：密度泛函理论 (DFT) 等量子力学方法是现代计算材料科学的基础，也是人工智能驱动发现的主要数据来源。然而，当今已建立的电子结构代码代表了几十年前的软件架构，使得新的物理模型在新兴硬件上实施、验证和部署速度缓慢且成本高昂。我们推出了 Quantum 3xplorer（Q3，发音为“Quantum Explorer”），这是一种 AI 原生量子模拟引擎，由 AI 编码代理在领域科学家的指导和监督下从头开始编写。作为第一个要求严格的验证案例…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

密度泛函理论 (DFT) 等量子力学方法是现代计算材料科学的基础，也是人工智能驱动发现的主要数据来源。然而，当今已建立的电子结构代码代表了几十年前的软件架构，使得新的物理模型在新兴硬件上实施、验证和部署速度缓慢且成本高昂。我们推出了 Quantum 3xplorer（Q3，发音为“Quantum Explorer”），这是一种 AI 原生量子模拟引擎，由 AI 编码代理在领域科学家的指导和监督下从头开始编写。作为第一个要求严格的验证案例，我们实现了端到端的平面波赝势 DFT：范数守恒、超软和投影增强波形式、自旋极化、DFT+U 和色散校正，以及力、应力、结构松弛和电子结构分析。 Q3 重现了已建立的参考实现，在代表性基准测试中在单核上运行速度更快，并且可以跨 GPU 加速的超级计算机进行扩展。 It was developed in weeks rather than years.其分层的、物理优先的架构将抽象的量子力学概念与其数值表示分离，使其框架可以扩展到平面波之外的其他基础集和理论层次。其输入和输出遵循结构化、模式验证的数据标准，使每个结果都适合人工智能。 Q3 在 Apache 2.0 下开源，旨在供研究社区重用、扩展和改进。 Q3 指出了计算量子科学如何进行的范式转变。科学家定义物理学、制定模型并设定验证标准； AI agents write, test, and optimize the code.因此，从新理论到值得信赖的高性能实施的长达数十年的道路可以缩短到几天，从而显着加速科学发现。

English Abstract

Quantum-mechanical approaches, such as density functional theory (DFT), underpin much of modern computational materials science and are the primary source of data for AI-driven discovery. Yet the established electronic-structure codes today represent decades-old software architectures, making new physical models slow and costly to implement, validate, and deploy on emerging hardware. We present Quantum 3xplorer (Q3, pronounced "Quantum Explorer"), an AI-native quantum simulation engine written from scratch by AI coding agents under the direction and supervision of domain scientists. As a first and demanding validation case, we implement plane-wave pseudopotential DFT end to end: norm-conserving, ultrasoft, and projector-augmented-wave formalisms, spin polarization, DFT+U, and dispersion corrections, together with forces, stresses, structural relaxation, and electronic-structure analysis. Q3 reproduces established reference implementations, runs faster on a single core in representative benchmarks, and scales across GPU-accelerated supercomputers. It was developed in weeks rather than years. Its layered, physics-first architecture separates abstract quantum-mechanical concepts from their numerical representations, making its framework extendable beyond plane waves to other basis sets and levels of theory. Its inputs and outputs follow structured, schema-validated data standards, making every result AI-ready. Q3 is open source under Apache 2.0 and built to be reused, extended, and improved by the research community. Q3 points to a paradigm shift in how computational quantum science is done. Scientists define the physics, formulate the models, and set the criteria for validation; AI agents write, test, and optimize the code. The decades-long path from a new theory to a trusted, high-performance implementation can thus collapse to days, significantly accelerating scientific discovery.

OsB2 中 I 型超导性的实验证据#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

自从发现 MgB2 超导性以来，金属二硼化物引起了人们的广泛关注。其中，二硼化锇（OsB2）结晶为具有褶皱硼层的斜方结构（空间群Pmmn），被归类为低κII型超导体（T_c约2.1~K）。在这里，我们报告了实验证据，证明 OsB2 实际上是一种罕见的非元素 I 型超导体。我们的鉴定得到了三个主要实验标志的支持：（i）在静态直流磁场下的交流磁化率（χₐc）测量中存在明显的微分顺磁效应（DPE）峰值，表明中间状态； (ii) 场诱导交叉到比热 (C/T) 发散的一级相变； (iii) 磁场斜坡转变中存在明显的磁滞现象，表明过冷。

English Abstract

Since the discovery of superconductivity in MgB₂, metal diborides have attracted significant attention. Among these, osmium diboride (OsB₂), crystallizing in an orthorhombic structure (space group Pmmn) with puckered boron layers, has been classified as a low-κ Type-II superconductor (T_c approx 2.1~K). Here, we report experimental evidence establishing that OsB₂ is in fact a rare nonelemental Type-I superconductor. Our identification is supported by three primary experimental hallmarks: (i) a pronounced Differential Paramagnetic Effect (DPE) peak in AC susceptibility (χₐc) measurements under static DC magnetic fields, signaling the intermediate state; (ii) a field-induced crossover to a divergent first-order phase transition in specific heat (C/T); and (iii) pronounced magnetic hysteresis in field-ramped transitions indicative of supercooling.

光子雪崩量子过程的光磁场门控#

原题：Optical Magnetic-Field Gating of Photon Avalanching Quantum Processes
作者：Benoît Reynier、Filippo Calavaro、Natalie Fardian-Melamed、Eric Charron、Bleuenn Belz、Maria Sanz-Paz 等 11 人。arXiv · physics，2026-11-03

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

高度非线性现象，即宏观变化是由微观扰动引发的，在从流行病传播到神经网络的复杂系统中普遍存在。在光学中，光子雪崩代表了这种高度非线性行为的主要表现，其特征是雪崩阈值之后的陡峭非线性响应。然而，这种非线性通常是由材料成分决定的，这使得这种行为对于自适应传感、成像和神经形态应用来说很难调整。在这里，我们展示了镧系元素掺杂纳米粒子的巨大光磁场门控阈值和非线性。我们利用雪崩过程的独特量子选择规则：虽然激发态吸收和交叉弛豫的能量循环周期主要是电驱动的，但种子激发、基态吸收是允许磁偶极子相互作用的混合跃迁。通过将发射器放置在光学驻波内以在空间上划分电磁场，我们调整局部磁场与电场的平衡，以选择性地调节种子和放大概率。这使我们能够展示对功率阈值和非线性的前所未有的动态控制范围，将阶数从 s = 15 调整到 s = 62。此外，我们利用紧密聚焦的圆柱形矢量光束来利用电场和磁场的空间分离来局部塑造单个纳米团簇的非线性，实现亚波长超分辨率成像，大大超过高斯照明下的传统光子雪崩成像。这些发现将光的磁性成分确立为一个实用的旋钮，可以在纳米尺度上主动编程非线性光学动力学、亚衍射纳米学和临界量子动力学。

English Abstract

Highly nonlinear phenomena, where macroscopic changes are triggered by microscopic perturbations, are ubiquitous in complex systems ranging from epidemic spreading to neural networks. In optics, photon avalanching represents a prime manifestation of such highly nonlinear behavior, characterized by a steep nonlinear response (s) following an avalanche threshold. However, this nonlinearity is typically determined by material composition, making this behavior challenging to tune for adaptive sensing, imaging, and neuromorphic applications. Here, we demonstrate giant optical magnetic field gated threshold and nonlinearity in lanthanide doped nanoparticles. We exploit the distinct quantum selection rules of the avalanche process: while the energy looping cycles of excited state absorption and cross relaxation are primarily electrically driven, the seed excitation, ground state absorption, is a mixed transition permitting magnetic dipole interaction. By positioning the emitters within an optical standing wave to spatially partition the electromagnetic field, we tune the local magnetic to electric field balance to selectively regulate the seeding and amplification probabilities. This allows us to demonstrate an unprecedented dynamic range of control over the power threshold and nonlinearity, tuning the order from s = 15 to s = 62. Furthermore, we harness this spatial separation of electric and magnetic fields using tightly focused cylindrical vector beams to locally sculpt the nonlinearity of a single nanocluster, achieving sub wavelength super resolution imaging that substantially surpasses conventional photon avalanche imaging under Gaussian illumination. These findings establish the magnetic component of light as a practical knob to actively program nonlinear optical dynamics, sub diffraction nanoscopy, and critical quantum dynamics at the nanoscale.

地球磁尾重联区能通量密度结构：桥接模拟与观测#

作者：Cara L. Waters、Jonathan P. Eastwood、Harry C. Lewis、Naïs Fargette、Marit Øieroset、Tai D. Phan 等 8 人。arXiv · physics，2026-11-03

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

磁重联在空间和实验室环境中都很重要，因为它改变了磁场的拓扑结构并将磁能转换为粒子能量。确定这种能量转换发生的位置对于理解重新连接如何分配能量至关重要。使用基于模拟的新颖机器学习方法，首次明确识别分界线区域，通过对原位观察到的磁尾重联事件的统计研究来评估磁重联位点内的能通量密度的划分。发现分离体具有最大的电子能通量密度，大约是排气中的两倍，坡印廷通量密度大约是流入中的四倍，而典型的离子焓通量密度在排气中最大。因此，分离面是强烈电子和电磁能传输的场所，尽管它们的厚度较小意味着废气仍可能携带大部分总能量通量。

English Abstract

Magnetic reconnection is important in both space and laboratory contexts because it changes the topology of magnetic fields and converts magnetic energy into particle energies. Establishing where this energy conversion occurs is critical for understanding how reconnection partitions energy. Using a novel machine learning approach based on simulations that explicitly identifies the separatrix regions for the first time, the partition of energy flux densities within the magnetic reconnection site is evaluated through statistical study of magnetotail reconnection events observed in situ. The separatrices are found to have the largest electron energy flux densities, roughly twice those in the exhaust, and Poynting flux densities about four times those in the inflows, whereas typical ion enthalpy flux densities are largest in the exhaust. The separatrices are therefore sites of intense electron and electromagnetic energy transport, although their small thickness means that the exhaust may still carry most of the total energy flux.

LaQX：用于晶格量子多体模型神经量子态模拟的 JAX 工具包#

原题：LaQX: A JAX Toolkit for Neural Quantum State Simulations of Lattice Quantum Many-Body Models
作者：Yuntian Gu、Chaohui Fan、Ruichen Li、Haoxiang Chen、Weizhong Fu、Yixiao Chen 等 12 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-04
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：神经量子态为相互作用的晶格费米子和自旋提供了准确的变分表示。然而，将新的 ansatz 转变为可重复的计算，需要哈密顿量、采样、优化、可观测值和加速器并行性的协调实现。我们提出了 LaQX（基于 jaX 的晶格量子模拟），这是一个基于 JAX 的开源神经量子状态库，它通过通用功能接口组织这些组件。除了基态变分蒙特卡罗之外，LaQX 还支持激发态计算、格林函数蒙特卡罗细化、绝热演化以及依赖时间的变分蒙特卡罗的实时演化。它整合了最近在哈伯德…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

神经量子态为相互作用的晶格费米子和自旋提供了准确的变分表示。然而，将新的 ansatz 转变为可重复的计算，需要哈密顿量、采样、优化、可观测值和加速器并行性的协调实现。我们提出了 LaQX（基于 jaX 的晶格量子模拟），这是一个基于 JAX 的开源神经量子状态库，它通过通用功能接口组织这些组件。除了基态变分蒙特卡罗之外，LaQX 还支持激发态计算、格林函数蒙特卡罗细化、绝热演化以及依赖时间的变分蒙特卡罗的实时演化。它整合了最近在哈伯德模型和受抑磁体的高精度神经量子态研究中开发的方法，并且该软件包已用于研究三角晶格 Hofstadter-Hubbard 模型。我们通过哈伯德、J₁-J2 海森堡、交流哈伯德和相互作用的霍夫施塔特模型的可重复计算来记录这些功能。面向功能的模块将科学组件分开，并限制人类开发人员和大型语言模型扩展所需的源上下文。源代码、文档和可执行示例可在 https://github.com/bytedance/laqx 上获取。

English Abstract

Neural quantum states provide accurate variational representations for interacting lattice fermions and spins. Turning a new ansatz into a reproducible calculation, however, requires coordinated implementations of the Hamiltonian, sampling, optimization, observables, and accelerator parallelism. We present LaQX (Lattice Quantum simulation based on jaX), an open-source neural quantum state library based on JAX that organizes these components through common functional interfaces. Beyond ground-state variational Monte Carlo, LaQX supports excited-state calculations, Green's Function Monte Carlo refinement, adiabatic evolution, and real-time evolution with time-dependent variational Monte Carlo. It consolidates methods developed in recent high-accuracy neural quantum state studies of the Hubbard model and frustrated magnets, and the package has been used to investigate the triangular-lattice Hofstadter-Hubbard model. We document these capabilities with reproducible calculations for Hubbard, J₁-J₂ Heisenberg, altermagnetic Hubbard, and interacting Hofstadter models. Function-oriented modules separate the scientific components and limit the source context needed for extensions by human developers and large language models. Source code, documentation, and executable examples are available at https://github.com/bytedance/laqx.

磁强子-声子耦合系统中无反射状态的磁控和固有模式的特殊点#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

无反射状态（ RL ）和异常点（ EP ）是非埃尔米特系统的标志性奇异响应，在传感、波控和能量操纵方面具有潜在的应用。然而，在紧凑的集成平台中实现和独立调节这些状态仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们演示了基于FeGaB-32度-YX-LiNbO3异质结构的片上倍频声子系统中RL和EP的二维磁控。磁场幅度调谐磁振子失调，驱动实频轴上的低损耗反射零，并产生两个具有近乎消失反射的场可调谐RL。实验测量的复合体S11围绕反射零奇点的闭合轮廓累积了-2pi相位绕组。独立地，旋转磁场调谐磁声耦合强度，驱动固有磁声声本征模式光谱穿过一个特殊点，通过实部和虚部特征值分裂之间的交换来识别。统一的单端口输入输出模型将测量的复杂S11响应连接到反射零点的场驱动动力学和角度调谐的固有磁声本征模态。这些结果为集成波控制和紧凑矢量磁传感建立了一个磁可编程的非埃尔米特磁声子平台。

English Abstract

Reflectionless states (RLs) and exceptional points (EPs) are hallmark singular responses of non-Hermitian systems, with potential applications in sensing, wave control, and energy manipulation. Yet, realizing and independently tuning these states in a compact integrated platform remains challenging. Here, we demonstrate two-dimensional magnetic control of RLs and EPs in an on-chip magnon-phonon system based on a FeGaB-32 degree-YX-LiNbO3 heterostructure. The magnetic-field magnitude tunes the magnon-phonon detuning, driving a low-loss reflection zero across the real-frequency axis and producing two field-tunable RLs with near-vanishing reflection. A closed contour of the experimentally measured complex S11 around the reflection-zero singularity accumulates a -2pi phase winding. Independently, rotating the magnetic field tunes the magnon-phonon coupling strength, driving the intrinsic magnon-phonon eigenmode spectrum across an exceptional point, identified by the exchange between real-part and imaginary-part eigenvalue splitting. A unified one-port input-output model connects the measured complex S11 response to the field-driven dynamics of the reflection zeros and the angle-tuned intrinsic magnon-phonon eigenmodes. These results establish a magnetically programmable non-Hermitian magnon-phonon platform for integrated wave control and compact vector magnetic sensing.

中文摘要

随着基于第一原理和人工智能的方法能够探索更大、化学更复杂的材料空间，快速估计超导临界温度 Tc 的需求变得越来越迫切。在这种背景下，基于化学键的量已成为预测超导性的有希望的描述符。迄今为止，这些方法主要是针对氢化物超导体开发的，特别是通过网络值（一种基于电子局域化函数的描述符），其拓扑特性已被发现与 Tc 相关。从这个角度来看，我们回顾并巩固了该方法的理论，并讨论了如何在真实空间视图中实现 Tc 的估计。最后，我们提出了一个框架，可以根据易于计算的中间物理量对广泛的传统超导体进行 Tc 预测。

English Abstract

The need for rapid estimates of superconducting critical temperatures, Tc s, is becoming increasingly urgent as first-principles and artificial intelligence based methods enable the exploration of ever-larger and more chemically complex materials spaces. In this context, chemical bonding based quantities have emerged as promising descriptors for predicting superconductivity. To date, these approaches have mainly been developed for hydride superconductors, notably through the networking value, a descriptor based upon the electron localization function, whose topological properties have been found to correlate with Tc . In this perspective, we review and consolidate the method's theory, and we discuss how the estimate of the Tc is achievable within a real-space view. We conclude by proposing a framework that enables Tc prediction for the broad family of conventional superconductors from easily computable intermediate physical quantities.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出了一种费米子规范理论，用于解禁 Shastry-Sutherland 晶格上的量子临界点，并揭示了其与方晶格共享的低能场论结构。从 SU(2) π 通量母态开始，我们构建了耦合到 SU(2) 规范场和伴随希格斯场的狄拉克自旋子的连续介质理论，其凝聚驱动转变为交错通量 U(1) 自旋液体和无间隙 Z2 狄拉克自旋液体。虽然与方晶格相比，Shastry-Sutherland 晶格允许额外的对称性费米子双线性，但量子场论在附加不相关项之前是相同的。因此，希格斯势结构和领先的低能理论与量子临界点的方晶格情况一致。 SO(5) 临界点有望实现共形去限制临界点：我们在大风味展开中对其进行分析，计算其临界指数，并将费米子和希格斯场之间的汤川耦合识别为使其不稳定的相关扰动，这与最近蒙特卡罗研究中观察到的伪临界行为一致。我们表明，新兴的 SO(5) 有序参数在临界点获得了大的反常尺寸，导致 Néel 和 VBS 敏感性大大增强——这是与数值研究一致的费米子解限域量子临界性的标志。我们的研究结果在受控场论框架内提供了 Shastry-Sutherland 晶格上无间隙 Z2 狄拉克自旋液体的最新数值证据，并证明了方形晶格上的费米子解除限制临界性（包括临界指数和稳定性）扩展到对称性降低的受抑晶格。

English Abstract

We present a fermionic gauge theory for deconfined quantum criticality on the Shastry-Sutherland lattice and reveal its shared low-energy field-theoretic structure with the square lattice. Starting from an SU(2) π-flux parent state, we construct a continuum theory of Dirac spinons coupled to an SU(2) gauge field and adjoint Higgs fields whose condensates drive transitions to a staggered-flux U(1) spin liquid and a gapless Z₂ Dirac spin liquid. While the Shastry-Sutherland lattice permits additional symmetry-allowed fermion bilinears compared to the square lattice, the quantum field theories are identical up to additional irrelevant terms. Consequently, the Higgs potential structure and the leading low-energy theory coincide with the square-lattice case at the quantum critical point. The SO(5) critical point is expected to realize conformal deconfined criticality: we analyze it in a large-flavor expansion, calculate its critical exponents, and identify the Yukawa coupling between the fermions and Higgs fields as the relevant perturbation that destabilizes it, consistent with pseudocritical behavior observed in recent Monte Carlo studies. We show that the emergent SO(5) order parameter acquires a large anomalous dimension at the critical point, leading to strongly enhanced Néel and VBS susceptibilities-a hallmark of fermionic deconfined quantum criticality consistent with numerical studies. Our results place recent numerical evidence for a gapless Z₂ Dirac spin liquid on the Shastry-Sutherland lattice within a controlled field-theoretic framework and demonstrate that fermionic deconfined criticality on the square lattice-including critical exponents and stability-extends to frustrated lattices with reduced symmetry.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

量子化涡旋是超流性的标志。事实证明，它们是从超流氦到 II 型超导体等系统中不可或缺的探测器。在超冷二维 (2D) 费米气体中，相涨落增强，Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) 机制控制超流体转变，但他们的观察仍然难以捉摸。在这里，我们报告了 BEC-BCS 交叉中均匀二维气体中量子化涡流的受控创建。通过连续调整两个移动障碍物的速度，我们观察到绝对涡流电荷的量化步骤。物质波干涉测量揭示了以 2π 为单位量化的特征相绕组。对 2D 和 3D 气体的测量显示出显着的差异：只有在 2D 中，涡旋数才随温度增加，反映了 BKT 涡旋对的热激活。我们的方法提供了二维费米子超流性的证据，并提供了一种研究 BKT 物理、涡流控制输运和阻力以及检测自旋不平衡二维气体中非常规超流性的方法。

English Abstract

Quantized vortices are a hallmark of superfluidity. They have proven to be an indispensable probe for systems ranging from superfluid helium to type-II superconductors. In ultracold two-dimensional (2D) Fermi gases, where phase fluctuations are enhanced and the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) mechanism governs the superfluid transition, their observation has remained elusive. Here, we report on the controlled creation of quantized vortices in a homogeneous 2D gas in the BEC--BCS crossover. By continuously tuning the velocity of two moving obstacles, we observe quantized steps in the absolute vortex charge. Matter-wave interferometry reveals the characteristic phase winding, quantized in units of 2π. Measurements on 2D and 3D gases show a striking difference: only in 2D does the vortex number grow with temperature, reflecting thermal activation of BKT vortex pairs. Our approach provides evidence of fermionic superfluidity in 2D and offers a means to study BKT physics, vortex-controlled transport and resistance, as well as to detect unconventional superfluidity in spin-imbalanced 2D gases.

中文摘要

三维磁流体动力 (MHD) 湍流、天体物理和聚变等离子体的流动的模拟成本很高。据我们所知，我们提出了三维电阻性 MHD 湍流的第一个学习替代物，并比较了该角色的三个标准骨干网：卷积 UNet、移位窗口变换器和傅立叶神经算子。它们被训练为衰减 MHD 湍流的自回归模拟器，分辨率为 128³，雷诺数为 1000、3000 和 6000，每个雷诺数进行 500 次模拟，每个模拟都有一个两阶段程序，单步骤，然后短时间推出，并由一条规则停止。在大约一个大涡周转时间的窗口内，UNet和变压器达到0.018至0.053的相对速度误差，并保持模拟的电流片、熵、电流密度和能谱；两者中哪一个更准确取决于雷诺数，UNet 领先于 3000 和 6000，Transformer 领先于 1000。对两个公共基准应用相同的骨干网和训练程序，模型达到了 FactFormer 各向同性湍流集迄今为止报告的最低误差，比最佳已发布模型低 39%。在 The Well 的可压缩 MHD 集上，它们的推出误差比 The Well 的最佳基线低三分之一到二分之一。他们还在其推出窗口中匹配了 1.3 B 参数预训练基础模型，参数减少了 20-62 倍。代码、每个模型的配置、评估脚本和带有每个模拟种子的数据生成器将在发布时发布。

English Abstract

Three-dimensional magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) turbulence, the flow of astrophysical and fusion plasmas, is costly to simulate. We present, to our knowledge, the first learned surrogates of three-dimensional resistive MHD turbulence, and compare three standard backbones in that role: a convolutional UNet, a shifted-window transformer and a Fourier neural operator. They are trained as autoregressive emulators of decaying MHD turbulence at a resolution of 128³ and Reynolds numbers of 1000, 3000 and 6000, on 500 simulations per Reynolds number, each with one two-stage procedure, single steps and then short rollouts, and stopped by one rule. Over a window of about one large-eddy turnover time, the UNet and the transformer reach relative velocity errors of 0.018 to 0.053 and keep the current sheets, the enstrophy, the current density and the energy spectra of the simulation; which of the two is the more accurate depends on the Reynolds number, the UNet leading at 3000 and 6000 and the transformer at 1000. Applied with the same backbones and training procedure to two public benchmarks, the models reach the lowest error reported to date on the isotropic turbulence set of FactFormer, 39% below the best published model. On the compressible MHD set of The Well, their rollout errors are a third to a half below those of the best of The Well's baselines. They also match a 1.3 B-parameter pretrained foundation model in its rollout windows, with 20-62 times fewer parameters. The code, the configuration of every model, the evaluation scripts and the data generator with the seed of every simulation will be released upon publication.

中文摘要

介电超表面的逆向设计功能强大，但劳动密集型：每个新目标都需要选择参数化和品质因数、管理计算资源，并严格评估可能在数值上不收敛、对制造误差敏感或陷入局部最优的结果。在这里，我们提出了一个开源管道，其中人工智能（AI）编码代理将简单语言规范转换为超表面优化运行，执行它们，使用固定清单分析每次运行并提出下一次迭代。通过可微分三维严格耦合波分析 (RCWA) 优化设计，包括制造公差的鲁棒性，并且每个报告的设计都通过有限差分时域 (FDTD) 仿真进行独立验证。我们在自由曲面硅元光栅、组装成光束偏转超级晶胞的偏振独立元原子库以及从同一库构建的超透镜上演示了 1550 nm 的工作流程。在元光栅案例中，独立的 FDTD 验证显示，优化模型的效率高估了 5%，这是传统收敛测试未检测到的，而鲁棒设计在 ±10 nm 制造误差下保留了大部分效率。在其他情况下，所获得的 RCWA 结果通过全波模拟得到证实。

English Abstract

Inverse design of dielectric metasurfaces is powerful but labour-intensive: each new target requires choosing a parameterization and a figure of merit, managing compute resources, and critically assessing results that may be numerically unconverged, sensitive to fabrication errors or trapped in local optima. Here we present an open-source pipeline in which an artificial intelligence (AI) coding agent turns a plain-language specification into metasurface optimization runs, executes them, analyses every run with a fixed checklist and proposes the next iteration. Designs are optimized with differentiable three-dimensional rigorous coupled-wave analysis (RCWA), including robustness to fabrication tolerances, and every reported design is independently verified with finite-difference time-domain (FDTD) simulations. We demonstrate the workflow at 1550 nm on a freeform silicon metagrating, on a polarization-independent meta-atom library assembled into beam-deflecting supercells, and on metalenses built from the same library. In the metagrating case, the independent FDTD verification revealed a 5% overestimate of the efficiency by the optimization model that a conventional convergence test had not detected, and the robust design retained most of its efficiency under ±10~nm fabrication errors. In the other cases, instead, the obtained RCWA results are confirmed by full-wave simulations.

霍尔效应推进器等离子体的二维物理信息神经网络模型：自洽静电学和物理一致性诊断#

原题：A Two-Dimensional Physics-Informed Neural Network Model of a Hall Effect Thruster Plasma: Self-Consistent Electrostatics and Physical-Consistency Diagnostics

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出了 P5 霍尔推进器放电通道在 300 V 和 5.4 A 下的二维轴对称物理信息神经网络 (PINN)，它求解了 11 个耦合等离子体场，并吸收了根据 Haas 和 Gallimore 的数据重建的每个场的 145 个内部探针测量值。电势是从电荷分离形式的泊松方程中获得的，nₑ = nᵢ(1+eta)，其中 eta 由结构限制，这使得刚性静电约束保持活跃且可训练。参考解决方案与电势、电子温度和离子密度的相对 L2 误差分别为 0.017、0.073 和 0.205 的训练曲线一致，并且保留测试表明，对于撤回测量线，电势比离子密度更加稳健。然后，我们根据数据拟合未揭示的要求测试解决方案：全局粒子和电流平衡、动量方程隐含的电子电流、场对准电子力平衡和轴向离子动量预算。这些暴露了测量流量六倍的中性流入、电势表示的界限以及动量隐含电子电流，超过 99% 场对准，超过放电电流几个数量级。使用适合测量数据的无发散磁场、不确定性加权数据、电子压力梯度以及逐步施加的场对准和电流约束来校正这些问题，产生的解决方案将粒子保留在 3% 以内，将电流保持在 5.1 至 5.4 A 之间，并将 97% 的电势和 88% 的温度测量置于不确定性范围内。然而，在测试的单速流体配方中，离子密度、电势和离子速度无法匹配在一起，并且场对准的电子平衡仍然是电子自洽的主要障碍。

English Abstract

We present a two-dimensional axisymmetric physics-informed neural network (PINN) of the discharge channel of the P5 Hall thruster at 300 V and 5.4 A, which solves eleven coupled plasma fields and assimilates 145 internal probe measurements per field reconstructed from the data of Haas and Gallimore. The electric potential is obtained from Poisson's equation in charge-separation form, nₑ = nᵢ(1+eta), with eta bounded by construction, which keeps the stiff electrostatic constraint active and trainable. The reference solution agrees with the training profiles to relative L2 errors of 0.017, 0.073 and 0.205 for the potential, electron temperature and ion density, and held-out tests show the potential to be far more robust than the ion density to the withdrawal of measurement lines. We then test the solution against requirements the data fit does not reveal: global particle and current balances, the electron current implied by the momentum equation, the field-aligned electron force balance and the axial ion momentum budget. These expose a neutral inflow six times the measured flow, a bound in the potential representation, and a momentum-implied electron current, more than 99% field-aligned, exceeding the discharge current by orders of magnitude. Correcting these, with a divergence-free magnetic field fitted to measured data, uncertainty-weighted data, the electron pressure gradient and progressively imposed field-aligned and current constraints, yields a solution that conserves particles to within 3%, holds the current between 5.1 and 5.4 A and places 97% of the potential and 88% of the temperature measurements within their uncertainties. Within the tested single-velocity fluid formulation, however, the ion density, potential and ion velocity could not be matched together, and the field-aligned electron balance remains the main obstacle to electron self-consistency.

Identifying the Shear Piezoelectric Coefficient using Physics-Informed Neural Networks under Exact Boundary and Initial Constraints#

作者：Suhas Suresh Bharadwaj。arXiv · physics，2026-11-03

📄 摘要：The shear piezoelectric coefficient e₁₅ governs the shear-mode electromechanical coupling in piezoelectric micromachined ultrasonic transducers (PMUTs) and surface acoustic wave (SAW) devices, but measuring it typically…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

The shear piezoelectric coefficient e₁₅ governs the shear-mode electromechanical coupling in piezoelectric micromachined ultrasonic transducers (PMUTs) and surface acoustic wave (SAW) devices, but measuring it typically requires destructive resonance testing on dedicated specimens. On simulated data, e₁₅ can instead be recovered non-destructively from sparse, noisy subsurface wavefield observations using a constrained physics-informed neural network (PINN). By building exact spatial distance functions and temporal multipliers directly into the network output, the clamped, grounded boundaries and resting initial state hold automatically, leaving the loss with only the interior equations of coupled electro-elastodynamics and the data misfit. Non-dimensional scaling brings all governing coefficients within one decade of O(1), and the setup removes the boundary and initial-condition penalty weights that soft-constraint methods must tune. In a six-way comparison (plane-strain PZT-5H, manufactured wavefield, 30 dB noise), a soft-penalty baseline with adaptive weights converges to an erroneous e₁₅ in four of the five runs (51.7% mean error), and fixed penalties reach an error of 0.85% only after a second-order optimization stage. The constrained formulations reach mean e₁₅ errors between 0.15% and 0.51% with held-out displacement errors below 1%. Within these variants, the spatial multiplier gives rapid first-order convergence, and the quadratic temporal multiplier lowers the early-time electrostatic residual by a factor of 6.8 versus a linear one. On finite-element data along one subsurface line, the full formulation recovers e₁₅ with 1.19% mean error at 30 dB and keeps it between 0.65% and 2.71% across a 40 to 0 dB noise sweep. Finally, training on displacements alone still recovers e₁₅ with mean errors of 0.45% on finite-element and 1.64% on manufactured data.

Message-passing graph persistence#

作者：Jorge Luiz Franco、Arthur Queiroz Moura、Moacir Ponti、Amauri Souza。arXiv · stat.ML，2026-11-05
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配6

📄 摘要：Message-passing graph neural networks (GNNs) are limited in expressive power by the one-dimensional Weisfeiler-Leman test. Persistent homology (PH) provides topological descriptors that can complement GNN representations…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural networks。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Message-passing graph neural networks (GNNs) are limited in expressive power by the one-dimensional Weisfeiler-Leman test. Persistent homology (PH) provides topological descriptors that can complement GNN representations, but existing approaches typically compute persistence separately from the representation learning process. In this paper, we introduce Message-Passing Graph Persistence (MGP), a family of topological descriptors that incorporates message passing into graph filtrations. At each filtration step, node embeddings are updated using the current subgraph and associated with persistence events. These embeddings also determine the representatives of connected components during persistence computation. We prove that MGP is invariant under graph isomorphism and that it is strictly more expressive than the PH-based descriptors it extends. We evaluate MGP on an expressivity benchmark and on several graph classification and regression datasets. Experiments show that MGP outperforms other PH-based descriptors on most of these datasets.

The Tangled Web They Weave: Exploring Neural Representations with Directed Simplicial Complexes#

作者：Michael Bleher。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-11-03
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配5

📄 摘要：Understanding how artificial neural networks (ANNs) represent and combine features internally is a central challenge in mechanistic interpretability. Existing neural circuit-discovery methods represent neuron interaction…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：artificial neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Understanding how artificial neural networks (ANNs) represent and combine features internally is a central challenge in mechanistic interpretability. Existing neural circuit-discovery methods represent neuron interactions as pairwise edges in a graph. But distributed neural representations consist of large groups of neurons that are inherently linked by higher-order causal dependencies: suppressing a subgroup of neurons in concert may shift the activation of other neurons in the feature representation. We propose a method to construct filtered directed simplicial complexes (DSCs) that give a topological representation of such dependencies, built from the activation profiles of an ANN on a given set of inputs. Our approach treats ANNs as structural causal models and uses counterfactual ablations to test for causal links between groups of internal neurons within specific input contexts. As a proof of concept, we apply the approach to a pair of small multilayer perceptron (MLP) architectures in a synthetic two-dimensional quadrant classification task. In these examples, DSCs recover the designed feature pathways and coordinated class transitions, and the complexes of trained networks are higher-dimensional than those of their handcrafted references. As validation, we compare our method with graph-based circuit-discovery baselines and find that their 1-skeletons agree within the relations that a weight graph can represent. These results support DSCs as context-dependent descriptions of multi-neuron computation and may thus provide a novel, orthogonal framework to study the phenomenon of distributed feature representations and polysemanticity.

具有拓扑空洞的 PINN 和 PDE 的 De Rham 上同调架构#

原题：De Rham cohomological architectures for PINNs and PDEs with topological holes

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们尝试通过引入 de Rham 上同调增强（或 Hodge-de Rham PINN）来减轻经典物理信息神经网络（PINN）在与穿孔、孔、极点和空隙相对应的拓扑病理情况下的故障模式。由于不连续界面、非零闭路径积分、路径依赖、通过外导数与精确形式的对应等，普通 PINN 在基于孔的设置中经常遇到陷阱，因此，我们通过具有边界的流形的 Hodge-Morrey-Friedrichs (HMF) 分解参数化 1-形式来消除传统的失败，并通过 HMF 同构定理与 de Rham 上同调建立联系。 1-形式可以通过积分算子映射充当标量值 PDE 解的表示。我们建议通过相关的偏微分方程在参数化 1-形式上执行基于 PINN 的损失，这随后解决了基于势的 PINN 问题，并且还通过 Fréchet 微分互连下的 Sobolev 正则算子弥补了普通 PINN 的失败。算子关系通常具有非平凡的封闭形式，并且通常需要积分或具体情况分析，我们在实验中彻底解决了这个问题。当我们的损失不满足可积性条件时，我们可以配备穷举函数来训练网络。我们展示了我们关于阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆效应的方法，据我们了解，PINN 文献中基本上不存在这种效应。我们为我们的方法提供了理论保证，并展示了我们对广泛选择的拓扑不利问题的病态保障，包括不精确形式的场景以及非平凡谐波和共精确项的场景。

English Abstract

We attempt to alleviate failure modes of classical physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) in scenarios of topological pathologies corresponding to punctures, holes, poles, and voids by introducing de Rham cohomological augmentations, or Hodge-de Rham PINNs. Vanilla PINNs often encounter pitfalls in hole-based settings due to discontinuous interfaces, nonzero closed path integration, path dependence, correspondence to exact forms via the exterior derivative, etc., therefore we absolve traditional failures via parameterizing a 1-form by the Hodge-Morrey-Friedrichs (HMF) decomposition for manifolds with boundary, drawing a connection to de Rham cohomology such as through the HMF isomorphism theorem. 1-forms can act as representations of scalar-valued PDE solutions through integral operator mappings. We propose performing a PINN-based loss on the parameterized 1-form through an associated PDE, which subsequently solves the potential-based PINN problem and moreover remedies the failures of vanilla PINNs via the Sobolev-regular operator under a Fréchet differentiation interconnection. The operator relationship often has a nontrivial closed-form and often requires integration or a case-by-case analysis, which we address thoroughly in our experiments. When an integrability condition is not met on our losses, we can equip exhaustion functions to train the networks. We demonstrate our methods on the Aharonov-Bohm effect, which, to our understanding, is largely nonexistent in PINN literature. We develop theoretical guarantees of our methods and demonstrate our pathological safeguards on a broad selection of topologically-adverse problems, including scenarios of inexact forms, and those of nontrivial harmonics and coexact terms.

用于结构-性质关系发现的可解释空间描述符的进化设计#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

结构特性预测是机器学习中的一项经典任务，范围从分子活性和材料特性到显微镜中测量的局部功能响应。对于基于图像的回归，有两种选择定义了预测器可用的信息：图像邻域的大小和其中保留的空间分辨率。小邻域可以省略相关的周围结构，而大的、精细采样的邻域可以引入冗余或不相关的信息。在这里，我们开发了一个构建空间描述符的框架，用于预测局部光谱和磁滞回线。我们对回归模型进行基准测试，使用 SHAP 图检查其空间属性，并探索补丁大小和压缩的影响，以建立预测准确性和描述符维度之间的关系。然后，我们使用遗传算法来优化空间分区以及均值、最小和最大池化操作的分配。每个保留区域都贡献了图像的显式测量，允许搜索平衡预测误差与描述符值的数量。笛卡尔和极坐标结构提供了组织这些信息的补充方式。由此产生的描述符使得可以检查哪里需要空间细节、哪里区域摘要就足够了，以及这些选择如何依赖于预测变量和测量的响应。一旦建立了分子和固体的遗传算法规则，相同的方法就可以用于分子和固体。

English Abstract

Structure property prediction is a classical task in machine learning, ranging from molecular activity and materials properties to local functional responses measured in microscopy. For image-based regression, two choices define the information available to the predictor: the size of the image neighborhood and the spatial resolution retained within it. A small neighborhood can omit relevant surrounding structure, whereas a large, finely sampled neighborhood can introduce redundant or irrelevant information. Here, we develop a framework for constructing spatial descriptors for prediction of local spectra and hysteresis loops. We benchmark regression models, use SHAP maps to examine their spatial attribution, and explore the effect of the patch size and compression to establish the relationship between predictive accuracy and descriptor dimensionality. We then use a genetic algorithm to optimize spatial partitions and the assignment of mean, minimum, and maximum pooling operations. Each retained region contributes an explicit measurement of the image, allowing the search to balance prediction error against the number of descriptor values. Cartesian and polar constructions provide complementary ways to organize this information. The resulting descriptors make it possible to examine where spatial detail is needed, where regional summaries are sufficient, and how these choices depend on the predictor and the measured response. The same approach can be used for molecules and solids once the genetic algorithm rules for these are established.

Is Your AI Fast Enough to Run a Fusion Reactor?#

作者：Nathaniel Chen、Cheolsik Byun、Sangkyeun Kim、Andrew Rothstein、Ricardo Shousha、Hiro Josep Farre Kaga 等 9 人。arXiv · physics，2026-11-02
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配3

📄 摘要：In magnetic confinement fusion, compact machine learning algorithms are often integrated into the control loop on CPU-based custom hardware stacks. Deterministic implementations of these algorithms target instabilities w…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

In magnetic confinement fusion, compact machine learning algorithms are often integrated into the control loop on CPU-based custom hardware stacks. Deterministic implementations of these algorithms target instabilities which can evolve over microseconds to milliseconds. Staying within this framework may therefore limit the pace of control-system development to the inference time available. With the expansion of hardware acceleration and large models, we aim to bring ML models for fusion control to modern inference engines and compare, based on the physics reactor, the suitability of each engine per model. This study implements a consistent real-time machine learning algorithm for GPUs and optimized CPU packages, including OpenVINO, XLA, and TVM, at DIII-D National Fusion Facility. For the smallest model, one CPU core responds within 3.6 μs in 99% of control cycles and an NVIDIA A100 within 28 μs. We also evaluate models running simultaneously, as they would run in a typical experiment, and find best use cases per framework.

Forecasting the Ambient Solar Wind With a Physics-Informed Learned Inner Boundary: Part I-Differentiable Inverse Reconstruction From Solar Images#

作者：Enrico Camporeale、Andong Hu、Prateek Mayank、Thomas E. Berger。arXiv · physics，2026-11-03
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配3

📄 摘要：The solar-wind speed at the inner boundary of heliospheric models is not observed; near-Earth monitors record the wind only after nonlinear propagation. We pose the reconstruction of an effective boundary speed at 21.5,R…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

The solar-wind speed at the inner boundary of heliospheric models is not observed; near-Earth monitors record the wind only after nonlinear propagation. We pose the reconstruction of an effective boundary speed at 21.5,R_odot as an inverse problem and train a map from solar observations to that boundary by differentiating through a reduced heliospheric transport solver from 21.5,R_odot to measurement locations near the L1 Lagrangian point. Screening 42 candidate features from SDO/AIA 193~Å{} images and SDO/HMI magnetograms identifies coronal-hole area, its proximity to the ecliptic and its darkness as the most informative; the reference model adds their recent history, the expansion factor of GONG-based potential-field models and the speed observed one rotation earlier. In five-fold chronological cross-validation over solar cycle 24, the reconstruction outperforms 27-day recurrence and a linear regression on the screened features at a fixed lead (ROC area for wind above 600~km~s⁻¹: 0.879 versus 0.802 and 0.850) with a predicted-to-observed standard-deviation ratio of 0.81, at the cost of a +26~km~s⁻¹ bias from its tail-weighted loss. A factorial comparison shows that the skill comes from training through a speed-dependent propagation: a fixed lag degrades the score by 8.6~km~s⁻¹, stream interaction adds little, and a linear map, or a model with one coefficient per physical quantity, matches the neural network. About 57% of the variance of the hourly speed is not determined by these inputs, and the remaining error is common to all model classes: better reconstructions need more informative inputs rather than more flexible models. Forecasting and probabilistic prediction are addressed in Parts II and III.

Learning Almost Complex and Complex Structures#

作者：Edward Hirst、Tancredi Schettini Gherardini、Tomás S. R. Silva、Jakob Stein。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-11-04
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配3

📄 摘要：We study the numerical constructions of almost complex and complex structures on spheres and products of spheres up to real dimension six. Our general set-up parametrises almost complex structures via a global matrix fie…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

We study the numerical constructions of almost complex and complex structures on spheres and products of spheres up to real dimension six. Our general set-up parametrises almost complex structures via a global matrix field in an ambient Euclidean space, compressed by the tangent projector. The projector's algebraic residual measures the almost complex condition, while its Nijenhuis tensor is evaluated in the intrinsic norm of the round product metric. Polynomial features and neural networks are trained without prescribed targets to perform general searches for these structures. The almost complex search passes numerical admissibility checks on all nine examples that admit such structures and rejects S⁴, where a topological obstruction is known. A matrix correction then enforces the almost complex identity, subject to a novel uniform admissibility condition. For integrability, a free polynomial representation with Levenberg-Marquardt optimisation learns the complex structures on S²× S², S³× S¹, S³× S³ and S⁵× S¹, with the largest Nijenhuis norms found by adversarial search all below 10⁻⁹. These general searches were unsuccessful on S⁶, S⁴× S² and S⁴× T². Therefore, for these three cases direct numerical reconstructions were built in torus-fibred coordinates. They are integrable by construction, satisfy their compatibility conditions to better than 10⁻¹¹, and corroborate the recent constructions on S⁶ and S⁴× S².

二维 RP² 模型中序参数相关性的衰减速度快于幂律衰减：vibe 证明的严格结果#

原题：Faster-than-power-law decay of order parameter correlations in the two-dimensional RP² model: a rigorous result by vibe-proving
作者：Masaki Oshikawa。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-02
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：我们考虑具有最近邻相互作用的二维方格上的 RP² 模型。它是通过用指向矢代替标准经典海森堡模型中的自旋而获得的，它不区分两个相反的方向。与标准经典海森堡模型或 O(3) 非线性 sigma 模型不同，RP²（及其同类 RP³）模型允许在二维中按 π₁(RP²)= Z2 分类点涡流缺陷。人们很容易认为 Z2 涡流会引发类似于二维 O(2) 模型（XY 模型）中成熟的 Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless 相变的相…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们考虑具有最近邻相互作用的二维方格上的 RP² 模型。它是通过用指向矢代替标准经典海森堡模型中的自旋而获得的，它不区分两个相反的方向。与标准经典海森堡模型或 O(3) 非线性 sigma 模型不同，RP²（及其同类 RP³）模型允许在二维中按 π₁(RP²)= Z2 分类点涡流缺陷。人们很容易认为 Z2 涡流会引发类似于二维 O(2) 模型（XY 模型）中成熟的 Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless 相变的相变。事实上，文献中已经讨论了 RP² 和 RP³ 模型中阶次参数的幂律相关性的临界相或临界点的可能性。然而，到目前为止，该问题的挑战性性质阻碍了相图的明确结论。在本文中，我们报告了人工智能（ChatGPT 6 Astra）的严格数学证明，对于任何有限反温度 βge0，对于任何有限反温度 βge0，在二维方晶格上的 RP² 和 RP³ 模型中，距离 r 处的序参数的两点相关函数的绝对值由 C_β exp(-c_β[log(2+r)]²) 上界，其中正常数 C_β 和 c_β 仅取决于 β。即，相关函数的衰减速度比 r 的任何幂衰减得都快。证明在精益中被形式化和验证；正式化可在 doi:10.5281/zenodo.23548847 上公开获取。基于远距离单参数有效理论 ansatz 的启发式论证与严格相关界相结合表明（但未证明）Z2 涡旋在任何非零温度下始终不受约束。

English Abstract

We consider the RP² model on the two-dimensional square lattice with nearest-neighbor interactions. It is obtained by replacing the spin in the standard classical Heisenberg model with a director, which does not distinguish two opposite directions. Unlike the standard classical Heisenberg model or the O(3) nonlinear sigma model, the RP² (and its cousin RP³) model allows point vortex defects classified by π₁(RP²)= Z₂ in two dimensions. It is tempting to expect the Z₂ vortices to induce a phase transition analogous to the well-established Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transition in the O(2) model (XY model) in two dimensions. Indeed, the possibilities of a critical phase or a critical point with power-law correlations of the order parameter in the RP² and RP³ models have been discussed in the literature. However, the challenging nature of the problem has prevented a definitive conclusion on the phase diagram so far. In this paper, we report a rigorous mathematical proof by Artificial Intelligence (ChatGPT 6 Astra) that the absolute value of the two-point correlation function of order parameters at distance r in the RP² and RP³ models on the two-dimensional square lattice is bounded above by C_β exp(-c_β[log(2+r)]²) for any finite inverse temperature βge0, with positive constants C_β and c_β depending only on β. Namely, the correlation function decays faster than any power of r. The proof is formalized and verified in Lean; the formalization is publicly available at doi:10.5281/zenodo.23548847 . A heuristic argument based on a one-parameter effective theory ansatz at large distances combined with the rigorous correlation bound suggests (but does not prove) that the Z₂ vortices are always unbound at any nonzero temperature.

磁化强度和材料参数的物理信息 X 射线断层扫描#

原题：Physics-informed X-ray tomography of magnetization and material parameters
作者：Alexander Setescak、Polly Mitchell、Luke J. R. Higgins、Burkhard Kaulich、Luke Turnbull、Rikako Yamamoto 等 15 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-03
