关联：与你方向的关键词重合：dzyaloshinskii-moriya interaction。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

三维自旋纹理通过其主体材料内的微观相互作用来稳定。 X 射线矢量断层扫描可以对这些纳米级结构进行成像，但弱磁对比度和有限的视角范围常常导致部分磁化强度难以确定。在这里，我们展示了受微磁能约束的重建从这些数据中恢复了三维磁化强度，并获得了对潜在材料相互作用的敏感性。应用于手性磁体 Co8Zn₉Mn₃，该方法甚至可以从成功断层扫描重建通常所需的双轴投影的一小部分或仅从单个倾斜轴解析三维螺旋纹理。通过测试重建中哪种交换与 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用的比率能够最好地再现测量的预测，我们获得了一个与螺旋缠绕周期得出的文献值相差约 10% 的样本值。总而言之，我们的研究结果提出了一种减少矢量断层扫描所需时间并将其扩展到确定材料中基本磁相互作用的方法。

English Abstract

Three-dimensional spin textures are stabilized by microscopic interactions within their host materials. X-ray vector tomography can image these nanoscale structures, but weak magnetic contrast and a limited range of viewing angles often leave parts of the magnetization poorly determined. Here we show that a reconstruction constrained by the micromagnetic energy recovers the three-dimensional magnetization from such data and gains sensitivity to the underlying material interactions. Applied to the chiral magnet Co₈Zn₉Mn₃, the method resolves the three-dimensional helical texture even from a small fraction of the dual-axis projections typically required for a successful tomographic reconstruction, or from a single tilt axis alone. By testing which ratio of exchange to Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction in the reconstruction best reproduces the measured projections, we obtain a value for the sample that differs by about 10 % from the literature value derived from the helical winding period. Taken together, our findings suggest a way to reduce the time needed for vector tomography and to extend it to determining fundamental magnetic interactions in a material.

Co₃Sn2S2 中拓扑平带磁性的证据#

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

相关性和拓扑是新兴电子相研究的核心支柱。费米能级附近的拓扑平带将这些概念结合起来，为相关拓扑现象和非常规磁性提供了一条途径。尽管有理论预测，费米能级拓扑平带的动量分辨观测仍然难以实现。使用角分辨光电子能谱，我们在磁性 Kagome Weyl 半金属 Co₃Sn2S2 的拓扑非平凡电子结构中观察到费米能级的平带。我们进一步发现了强烈的温度依赖性，其中平带在费米能级上移动，并伴随着费米表面的重建。引人注目的是，这种演化发生在与先前报道的磁响应异常相同的温度范围内。总之，这些结果为 Co₃Sn2S2 中的拓扑平带磁性提供了证据，建立了拓扑、电子相关性和磁性相互交织的微观环境。

English Abstract

Correlations and topology are central pillars in the study of emergent electronic phases. Topological flat bands near the Fermi level unite these concepts, providing a route towards correlated topological phenomena and unconventional magnetism. Despite theoretical predictions, momentum-resolved observation of a topological flat band at the Fermi level has remained elusive. Using angle resolved photoemission spectroscopy, we observe a flat band at the Fermi level within the topologically non-trivial electronic structure of the magnetic kagome Weyl semimetal Co₃Sn₂S₂. We further uncover a strong temperature dependence in which the flat band shifts across the Fermi level, accompanied by a reconstruction of the Fermi surface. Strikingly, this evolution occurs on the same temperature scale as previously reported anomalies in the magnetic response. Together, these results provide evidence for topological flat band magnetism in Co₃Sn₂S₂, establishing a microscopic setting in which topology, electronic correlations, and magnetism become intertwined.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：ferroelectric、first-principles calculations、electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

铁电基板提供了一种通过局部键合和电子结构中极化相关的变化来控制低维系统中磁相互作用的途径。在这里，我们利用第一性原理计算，研究了 3d 过渡金属原子和二聚体在单层 α-In2Se3 上的吸附，并将 Cr 确定为两个极性表面上的稳定二聚体构型。反转铁电极化会导致其磁耦合发生显着变化：一种极化有利于 AFM 状态，而相反的极化则使 FM 和 AFM 配置几乎退化。对自旋密度、投影电子态和万尼尔哈密顿量的分析揭示了直接 Cr-Cr 跳跃以及与桥接 Se 原子相互作用的伴随变化。特别是，增强的 Cr-Se 杂化和 Se 自旋极化，以及直接两中心 Cr-Cr 跳跃的强烈减少，表明直接和底物介导的交换贡献之间存在极化依赖的平衡。这些结果表明铁电极化如何调节原子定义的二维混合系统中的磁相互作用。

English Abstract

Ferroelectric substrates offer a route to controlling magnetic interactions in low-dimensional systems through polarization-dependent changes in local bonding and electronic structure. Here, using first-principles calculations, we investigate the adsorption of 3d transition-metal atoms and dimers on monolayer α-In₂Se₃ and identify Cr as a stable dimer configuration on both polar surfaces. Reversing the ferroelectric polarization drives a pronounced change in its magnetic coupling: the AFM state is favored for one polarization, whereas the FM and AFM configurations become nearly degenerate for the opposite one. Analysis of the spin density, projected electronic states, and Wannier Hamiltonians reveals concomitant changes in the direct Cr-Cr hopping and in the interaction with the bridging Se atom. In particular, enhanced Cr-Se hybridization and Se spin polarization, together with a strong reduction of the direct two-center Cr-Cr hopping, point to a polarization-dependent balance between direct and substrate-mediated exchange contributions. These results show how ferroelectric polarization can tune magnetic interactions in an atomically defined 2D hybrid system.

轨道交换在 CoO 磁性中的关键作用#

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、effective hamiltonian。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

众所周知，CoO 反铁磁相中的 Co 离子拥有相当大的轨道矩，使这种材料有望用于轨道电子学应用。然而，涉及这些轨道自由度的位点间交换相互作用仍然很少被量化，并且它们在 CoO 磁性中的作用尚不清楚。在这里，我们使用基于 DFT+动态平均场理论的从头算力定理方法推导了 CoO 的完整低能有效哈密顿量，包括轨道交换项。我们发现了相当大的轨道相互作用，其中四极轨道交换成为主要的最近邻项。从有效哈密顿量获得的共线反铁磁序特征是磁矩远离赝立方方向倾斜，轨道贡献约为总矩的三分之一，与实验估计一致。我们表明，力矩倾斜源于轨道和自旋轨道交换相互作用的综合效应。相同的相互作用也会产生最低激发带的显着分裂。通过直接模拟非弹性中子散射（INS）强度，我们进一步表明，先前在 CoO INS 中观察到的特征柱状结构提供了轨道交换的清晰指纹。我们还讨论了我们的发现对复杂集体激发领域、其输运特性以及在轨道电子学领域的应用的进一步进展可能产生的影响。

English Abstract

Co ions in the antiferromagnetic phase of CoO are known to possess a substantial orbital moment, making this material promising for orbitronic applications. However, intersite exchange interactions involving these orbital degrees of freedom remain poorly quantified, and their role in CoO magnetism is unclear. Here, we derive a full low-energy effective Hamiltonian for CoO, including orbital exchange terms, using an ab initio force-theorem approach based on DFT+dynamical mean-field theory. We find sizable orbital interactions, with quadrupolar orbital exchange emerging as the dominant nearest-neighbor term. The collinear antiferromagnetic order obtained from the effective Hamiltonian features magnetic moments tilted away from the pseudocubic <001> direction and an orbital contribution amounting to about one-third of the total moment, in agreement with experimental estimates. We show that the moment tilting originates from the combined effect of orbital and spin-orbital exchange interactions. The same interactions also produce a prominent splitting of the lowest excitation band. By directly modeling the inelastic neutron scattering (INS) intensity, we further show that the characteristic column-like structures previously observed in CoO INS provide a clear fingerprint of orbital exchange. We also discuss possible impact of our findings for further advances in the field of complex collective excitations, their transport properties and applications in the realm of orbitronics.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetic materials、spin hamiltonian、magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在凝聚态物理的磁性领域中，磁系统由自旋哈密顿量在微观上描述。其参数化可通过从头计算和拟合实验数据来实现。通常，这是通过多阶段计算来实现的，这些计算要么将参数与可观察的材料特性相关联，要么将 Kohn-Sham 哈密顿量映射到有效的自旋模型上。在这个领域，准确而灵活的方法对于模拟后者的物理特性至关重要。本研究的重点是这样一种方法：自旋波理论。该方法应用于自旋哈密顿量的新公式，称为多项式局部自旋模型（PLSM），其中包括自旋算子中的任意阶项。这使得线性自旋波理论 (LSWT) 能够直接应用于分析任何现实磁性材料中任意共线和非共线相的能量景观和自旋波谱。此外，PLSM 的经典部分和量子部分完全分离。最后，提出了 LSWT 的开源实现，名为 Magnopy。它与现代参数化软件接口，支持任意共线和非共线系统，并且设计用于脚本编写和独立使用。

English Abstract

In the field of magnetism within condensed matter physics, magnetic systems are microscopically described by a spin Hamiltonian. Its parameterization is accessible through both ab initio calculations and fits to experimental data. Frequently, this is achieved via multi-stage calculations that either relate the parameters to observable material properties or map a Kohn-Sham Hamiltonian onto an effective spin model. In this realm, accurate and flexible methods are essential for simulating the physical properties of the latter. The present study focuses on one such method: spin wave theory. The approach is applied to a new formulation of the spin Hamiltonian, named polynomial localized spin model (PLSM), which includes terms of arbitrary order in the spin operators. This enables the direct application of linear spin wave theory (LSWT) to the analysis of the energy landscape and spin-wave spectrum of arbitrary collinear and non-collinear phases in any realistic magnetic material. Moreover, the classical and quantum parts of the PLSM are cleanly separated. Finally, an open-source implementation of LSWT, named Magnopy, is presented. It interfaces with modern parameterization software, supports arbitrary collinear and non-collinear systems, and is designed for both scripting and standalone use.

SrTiO₃ 腔体工程铁电体#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：ferroelectricity。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

将量子材料与光子腔结构连接起来是材料工程的前沿。暗腔环境的结构化电磁涨落可以重新规范基本的热力学材料特性和能量尺度，有望创建一个丰富的生态系统，其中包含空腔诱导的基态和裸材料中不存在的功能。然而，空腔耦合可以稳定或增强真正的新物质相的直接实验证明仍然很少。在这项工作中，我们确定了与暗太赫兹频率光子晶体腔耦合的独立 SrTiO₃ 膜中存在空腔诱导和空腔增强铁电相。使用太赫兹光谱，我们观察到 SrTiO₃ 中的铁电软模式与腔谐振之间的超强耦合 (USC)。 SrTiO₃对此 USC 的结构响应是通过二次谐波生成成像测量的，这揭示了腔环境中信号的显着增强（大于 400%）以及晶体对称性的明显改变，表明铁电性的开始和增强。我们将这些观察结果解释为重正化压电和挠曲电耦合的结果，而这些耦合又是由腔体修改的介电环境产生的。这种解释得到了基于 DFT 的多尺度动力学模拟的微观建模和宏观金兹堡-朗道理论的支持。总而言之，我们的实验和理论结果坚定地证实，空腔工程确实可以用于在材料中创建新的基态并增强现有的基态。这项工作为材料工程建立了一个新的范式，可直接应用于量子转导、量子传感和非线性集成光子学。

English Abstract

Interfacing quantum materials with photonic cavity architectures is at the vanguard of materials engineering. The structured electromagnetic fluctuations of a dark cavity environment can renormalize fundamental thermodynamical material properties and energy scales, holding promise to create a rich ecosystem of cavity-induced ground states and functionalities absent in the bare material. However, direct experimental demonstrations that cavity coupling can stabilize or enhance genuinely new phases of matter have remained scarce. In this work, we establish the presence of cavity-induced and cavity-enhanced ferroelectric phases in free-standing SrTiO₃ membranes coupled to dark THz-frequency photonic crystal cavities. Using THz spectroscopy, we observe ultrastrong coupling (USC) between the ferroelectric soft mode in SrTiO₃ and the cavity resonance. The structural response of SrTiO₃ to this USC is measured via second harmonic generation imaging, which reveals a substantial (greater than 400%) enhancement of signal in the cavity environment and a distinct modification of the crystalline symmetry, indicating the onset and enhancement of ferroelectricity. We interpret these observations as resulting from renormalized piezoelectric and flexoelectric couplings, which in turn arise from the cavity-modified dielectric environment. This interpretation is supported with microscopic modeling via DFT-based multi-scale dynamics simulations and by macroscopic Ginzburg-Landau theory. Altogether, our experimental and theoretical results firmly confirm that cavity engineering can indeed be used to create new ground states in materials and enhance existing ones. This work establishes a new paradigm for materials engineering with direct applications for quantum transduction, quantum sensing, and nonlinear integrated photonics.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：ferroelectric、phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

报道了迄今为止实验上从未见过的带电传播 3D 孤子的形成和结构特性的实验观察和计算机模拟。在从反铁电 (SmZA) 相快速冷却的情况下，在铁电向列 (NF) 液晶中，传播孤子在没有外场的情况下自发出现。观察到的孤子平行或反平行于铁电极化传播，并在彼此碰撞时湮灭。它们归因于带正电或带负电的传播局部极化展开，以筛选 NF 相中多余的负或正移动电荷块。移动电荷的存在归因于反铁电相中存在的空间周期性电荷分布，该分布在通过反铁电-铁电相变淬灭温度后暂时保留。

English Abstract

Experimental observations and computer simulation of formation and structural properties of so far experimentally never seen electrically charged propagating 3D solitons are reported. The propagating solitons appear spontaneously without an external field in a ferroelectric nematic (NF) liquid crystal under rapid cooling from the antiferroelectric (SmZA) phase. The observed solitons propagate either parallel or antiparallel to the ferroelectric polarization and annihilate when they collide with each other. They are attributed to either positively or negatively charged propagating local polarization splay formed to screen excess negative or positive mobile charge lumps in the NF phase. The presence of mobile charge is attributed to a spatially periodic charge distribution present in the antiferroelectric phase that temporarily remains after the temperature is quenched through the antiferroelectric- ferroelectric phase transition.

氧化物电极约束下氧化铪基铁电体的缺陷化学#

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

具有 La₀.₆₇Sr₀.₃₃MnO₃ (LSMO) 外延电极的 HfO2 异质结构作为具有电极依赖性缺陷化学的铁电系统引起了人们的兴趣。然而，人们对 LSMO 限制化学条件下缺陷的形成和稳定性仍然知之甚少。在这项工作中，我们使用密度泛函理论计算来系统地研究 LSMO 电极限制的化学环境下 HfO2 中的氧空位以及 La、Sr 和 Mn 相关的间隙和替代缺陷。我们发现，相对于块状 HfO2，LSMO 稳定性大大缩小了可接近的 O 化学势范围，从而改变了预测的缺陷形成能。虽然 La 和 Sr 替代缺陷主要受优选离子价态控制，但氧空位和 Mn 替代缺陷表现出对局部原子环境敏感的电荷态。此外，斜方晶系 HfO2 的缺陷复合体计算显示负结合能，表明有利的缺陷关联。 La 和 Mn 相关的缺陷配合物表现出热力学竞争，其相对稳定性取决于费米能级和氧化学势。最后，将分析扩展到 Hf0.5Zr0.5O2 和 ZrO2 揭示了常见的氧空位趋势，而 LSMO 衍生缺陷的稳定性更强烈地取决于主体材料和晶相。这些结果为理解电极约束的化学环境和主体结构如何影响 HfO2 基材料中的缺陷形成、电荷补偿和缺陷复合体稳定性提供了热力学基础。

English Abstract

HfO₂ heterostructures with La₀.₆₇Sr₀.₃₃MnO₃ (LSMO) epitaxial electrodes have attracted interest as ferroelectric systems with electrode-dependent defect chemistry. However, the formation and stability of defects under LSMO-constrained chemical conditions remain poorly understood. In this work, we use density functional theory calculations to systematically investigate oxygen vacancies and La-, Sr-, and Mn-related interstitial and substitutional defects in HfO₂ under chemical environments constrained by the LSMO electrode. We find that LSMO stability substantially narrows the accessible O chemical potential range relative to bulk HfO₂, altering the predicted defect formation energies. While La and Sr substitutional defects are governed primarily by preferred ionic valence states, oxygen vacancies and Mn substitutional defects exhibit charge states sensitive to the local atomic environment. In addition, defect-complex calculations in orthorhombic HfO₂ reveal negative binding energies, indicating favorable defect association. The La- and Mn-related defect complexes exhibit thermodynamic competition, with their relative stability depending on the Fermi level and oxygen chemical potential. Lastly, extending the analysis to Hf₀.₅Zr₀.₅O₂ and ZrO₂ reveals common oxygen-vacancy trends, while the stability of LSMO-derived defects depends more strongly on the host material and crystal phase. These results provide a thermodynamic basis for understanding how electrode-constrained chemical environments and host structures influence defect formation, charge compensation, and defect-complex stability in HfO₂-based materials.

振荡磁场中的动态重入斯格明子相#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：skyrmion、dzyaloshinskii-moriya interaction、monte carlo simulation。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们通过研究具有 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用的二维磁体在周期性振荡磁场下的非平衡响应，解决了驱动拓扑物质中的一个核心开放问题。通过广泛的蒙特卡罗模拟，我们发现了一种动态重入斯格明子相，它出现在场振幅和偏置场的有限窗口内，夹在传统斯格明子相和极化铁磁相之间。该相没有平衡对应物，源于单个场周期内斯格明子产生和湮灭之间的不平衡，导致周期平均拓扑电荷的逆转。我们表明，重入阶段仅存在于系统可以跟随驱动的动态响应区域中，并且在较高的驱动频率下被抑制。我们的结果将周期性驱动确立为稳定和反转斯格明子拓扑的非平衡控制参数。

English Abstract

We address a central open question in driven topological matter by investigating the nonequilibrium response of a two-dimensional magnet with Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions subjected to a periodically oscillating magnetic field. Using extensive Monte Carlo simulations, we uncover a dynamically reentrant skyrmion phase that emerges within a finite window of field amplitudes and bias fields, sandwiched between the conventional skyrmion and polarized ferromagnetic phases. This phase has no equilibrium counterpart and originates from an imbalance between skyrmion creation and annihilation over a single field cycle, leading to a reversal of the period-averaged topological charge. We show that the re-entrant phase exists only in the dynamic response regime where the system can follow the drive, and is suppressed at higher driving frequencies. Our results establish periodic driving as a nonequilibrium control parameter for stabilizing and reversing skyrmion topology.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们使用第一原理计算和紧束缚分析研究了两种砷取代的硼磷烯衍生单层 As@MBP 和 As@AMBP 的电子结构、局域轨道耦合和应变可调非线性霍尔响应。在没有自旋轨道耦合的情况下，两个系统都表现出有限的贝里曲率偶极子（BCD）和显着的非线性霍尔电导率，这表明非线性响应本质上是由轨道物理和反演对称性破缺驱动的。由于取代在 As@MBP 中打开了 120~meV 的带隙，在 As@AMBP 中打开了 90~meV 的带隙，远远超过了室温热波动（kBT 约 25.9~meV）。紧束缚分析表明，As 的掺入打破了 As@MBP 中 B-P 跳跃的等价性，并引起 As@AMBP 中关键最近邻跳跃的符号反转，反映了超越简单几何效应的明显电子重构。在机械应变下，贝里曲率的重新分布在As@MBP中产生了22~mathringA的最大BCD，大约比无应变结构大10倍，并引起BCD符号的反转。这些结果确立了砷取代的硼磷烯衍生物作为有前途的轻元素平台，用于机械可切换的非线性传输和量子材料应用，而不依赖于强自旋轨道耦合。

English Abstract

We investigate the electronic structure, local orbital coupling, and strain-tunable nonlinear Hall response of two arsenic-substituted borophosphene-derived monolayers, As@MBP and As@AMBP, using first-principles calculations and tight-binding analysis. In the absence of spin--orbit coupling, both systems exhibit a finite Berry-curvature dipole (BCD) and a substantial nonlinear Hall conductivity, demonstrating that the nonlinear response is intrinsically driven by orbital physics and inversion-symmetry breaking. As substitution opens sizable band gaps of 120~meV in As@MBP and 90~meV in As@AMBP, well exceeding room-temperature thermal fluctuations (kBT approx 25.9~meV). Tight-binding analysis reveals that As incorporation breaks the equivalence of B-P hoppings in As@MBP and induces sign reversals in key nearest-neighbor hoppings in As@AMBP, reflecting a pronounced electronic reconstruction beyond simple geometric effects. Under mechanical strain, the redistribution of Berry curvature yields a maximum BCD of 22~mathringA in As@MBP, approximately 10 times larger than that of unstrained structure, and induces a reversal of the BCD sign. These results establish As-substituted borophosphene derivatives as promising light-element platforms for mechanically switchable nonlinear transport and quantum-material applications without relying on strong spin-orbit coupling.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

范德华铁电 NbOI2 具有极性畸变，这通常归因于其 Nb 链的 Peierls 二聚化。在这里，我们使用第一性原理计算和朗道型能量表明，极性不稳定性实际上是一个独特的有序参数，并非源于 Peierls 模式凝聚，并且三个主要的不同模式在整个 MOI2 系列中很重要。它们是极性模式 P、反极性模式 η 和 Peierls 模式 δ，其稳定性和耦合很大程度上取决于 M 金属元素的性质，同构 TaOI2 系统在具有 Peirls 畸变的同时不是极性的，这一事实证明了这一点。

English Abstract

The van der Waals ferroelectric NbOI₂ hosts a polar distortion that is commonly attributed to the Peierls dimerization of its Nb chains. Here we show, using first-principles calculations and a Landau-type energy, that the polar instability is in fact a distinct order parameter that does not stem from the Peierls mode condensation and that three main distinct modes are important in the whole MOI₂ family. They are a polar mode P, an antipolar mode η, and a Peierls mode δ, whose stabilities and couplings strongly depend on the nature of the M metal element, as evidenced by the fact that the isostructural, TaOI₂ system is not polar while possessing Peirls distortions.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

稀土镍酸盐是析氧反应 (OER) 中最活跃的氧化物电催化剂之一，因为它们具有可调节的 Ni-O 共价、近乎一致的占据率以及对 A 位化学、氧化学计量和外延应变的敏感性。在这里，我们使用外延 A 位高熵 (La0.2Pr0.2Nd0.2Sm0.2Eu0.2)NiO3 薄膜来揭示外延应变如何影响化学无序稀土 (RE) 亚晶格中的缺陷形成、Ni 价态、电子结构和 OER 活性。拉伸应变促进 Ruddlesden-Popper 断层的形成，中断共享角的 NiO6 网络，并降低平均 Ni 氧化态。尽管费米能级附近的占据态密度基本保持不变，但 O K 边 X 射线吸收光谱揭示了拉伸应变下低能未占据态的增加。第一性原理计算表明，拉伸应变将 RE 4f 衍生态移向费米能级，从而增强 RE 4f-O 2p-Ni 3d 杂化。这些耦合的结构和电子变化导致 OER 更早开始、更强的预催化 Ni 氧化还原特性以及比压缩应变薄膜和代表性单 A 位镍酸盐更高的活性。与 LaNiO3 相比，高熵镍酸盐还表现出更强的抗 OER 引起的结构降解的能力。这些结果表明，A位构型紊乱和外延应变可以共同调节缺陷形成和稀土衍生的电子态，为改进钙钛矿氧化物电催化剂提供了一条途径。

English Abstract

Rare-earth nickelates are among the most active oxide electrocatalysts for the oxygen evolution reaction (OER), owing to their tunable Ni-O covalency, near-unity eg occupancy, and sensitivity to A-site chemistry, oxygen stoichiometry, and epitaxial strain. Here, we use epitaxial A-site high-entropy (La0.2Pr0.2Nd0.2Sm0.2Eu0.2)NiO3 thin films to reveal how epitaxial strain influences defect formation, Ni valence, electronic structure, and OER activity in a chemically disordered rare-earth (RE) sublattice. Tensile strain promotes Ruddlesden-Popper fault formation, interrupts the corner-sharing NiO6 network, and lowers the average Ni oxidation state. Although the occupied density of states near the Fermi level remains largely unchanged, O K-edge X-ray absorption spectroscopy reveals increased low-energy unoccupied states under tensile strain. First-principles calculations suggest that tensile strain shifts RE 4f-derived states toward the Fermi level, enhancing RE 4f-O 2p-Ni 3d hybridization. These coupled structural and electronic changes lead to earlier OER onset, stronger pre-catalytic Ni redox features, and higher activity than compressively strained films and representative single-A-site nickelates. The high-entropy nickelate also shows enhanced resistance to OER-induced structural degradation compared with LaNiO3. These results suggest that A-site configurational disorder and epitaxial strain can jointly tune defect formation and rare-earth-derived electronic states, providing a route for improving perovskite oxide electrocatalysts.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations、density functional theory、monte carlo simulation、van der waals magnets。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

通过结合第一原理计算、第二原理自旋模型和蒙特卡罗模拟，我们研究了单层CrPS和NiPS的磁性。与仅在少数壳体后截断交换相互作用并通过拟合总能量来确定它们的传统方法不同，我们使用LKAG形式直接从密度泛函理论中提取磁交换张量，并包括相互作用，直到实现数值收敛。我们表明，远程交换相互作用定性地改变了两种材料的磁性行为。在CrPS中，它们破坏了先前预测的铁磁基态并稳定了自旋螺旋相，将临界温度降低到约21 ， K ，这与现有实验一致。生成的磁相图包含多个共线和非共线相位，可以通过温度和外部磁场进行调谐。在NiPS中，实验观察到的锯齿状反铁磁有序仅在包括交换相互作用直到第五壳时才出现。这些结果表明，硫代磷酸盐单层的定量预测自旋模型需要远距离交换相互作用，并为准确描述二维范德瓦尔斯磁体提供了预测框架。

English Abstract

We investigate the magnetic properties of monolayer CrPS₄ and NiPS₃ by combining first-principles calculations, second-principles spin models, and Monte Carlo simulations. Unlike conventional approaches that truncate exchange interactions after only a few shells and determine them by fitting total energies, we extract the magnetic exchange tensors directly from density functional theory using the LKAG formalism and include interactions until numerical convergence is achieved. We show that long-range exchange interactions qualitatively modify the magnetic behavior of both materials. In CrPS₄, they destabilize the previously predicted ferromagnetic ground state and stabilize a spin-spiral phase, reducing the critical temperature to about 21,K, in agreement with available experiments. The resulting magnetic phase diagram contains multiple collinear and non-collinear phases that can be tuned by temperature and external magnetic fields. In NiPS₃, the experimentally observed zigzag antiferromagnetic order only emerges when exchange interactions up to the fifth shell are included. These results demonstrate that quantitatively predictive spin models for thiophosphate monolayers require long-range exchange interactions and provide a predictive framework for accurately describing two-dimensional van der Waals magnets.

Tt 对称反铁磁体中磁电可调谐非线性霍尔效应#

原题：Magnetically and electrically tunable nonlinear Hall effect in Tt-symmetric antiferromagnets

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations、ferroelectricity、multiferroic。

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

Tt对称反铁磁体可以表现出铁电性和磁序共存的多铁性，因此具有多种有序参数配置。然而，如何以电方式读出这些配置是一个值得进一步研究的问题。在这里，通过结合紧束缚和第一原理计算，我们提出了铁电 Tt 对称反铁磁体中电学和磁学可调的非线性霍尔传输。模型计算表明，铁电开关可以反转贝里曲率偶极子 (BCD) 的符号，而尼尔矢量旋转可以调制其幅度，从而允许铁电和磁序在非线性霍尔信号中反映出来。 VOCl2 单层的第一性原理计算证实了这些行为，并预测了一个相当大的 BCD，该 BCD 会产生潜在可检测的霍尔电压。我们的研究结果为将非线性霍尔效应的电和磁调制集成在单一材料中提供了机会，并在反铁磁自旋电子学的多态信息编码和读出中具有潜在的应用。

English Abstract

Tt-symmetric antiferromagnets can exhibit multiferroicity with coexisting ferroelectricity and magnetic order, and thus host multiple order-parameter configurations. However, how to electrically read out these configurations is an issue meriting further investigation. Here, by combining tight-binding and first-principles calculations, we propose electrically and magnetically tunable nonlinear Hall transport in ferroelectric Tt-symmetric antiferromagnets. Model calculations show that ferroelectric switching can reverse the sign of the Berry curvature dipole (BCD), whereas the Neel vector rotation modulates its magnitude, allowing both ferroelectric and magnetic order to be reflected in the nonlinear Hall signal. First-principles calculations of the VOCl2 monolayer confirm these behaviors and predict a sizable BCD that gives rise to a potentially detectable Hall voltage. Our findings open up opportunities to integrate electrical and magnetic modulations of the nonlinear Hall effect within a single material, with potential applications in multi-state information encoding and readout in antiferromagnetic spintronics.

电流辅助畴壁运动对Co3Sn2S2中的磁性调制#

原题：Magnetism modulation in Co3Sn2S2 by current-assisted domain wall motion
作者：Qiuyuan Wang、Yi Zeng、Kai Yuan、Qingqi Zeng、Pingfan Gu、Xiaolong Xu 等 13 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-06

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：domain wall、magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

自旋电子器件的效率可以通过以较低的工作电流产生更高的有效磁场来提高。自旋转移矩可以驱动由单一材料组成的器件中的磁畴壁运动，但通常需要高阈值电流密度来移动磁畴壁，并且提高普通巡回铁磁体中的有效磁场很困难。在这里，我们报告了通过自旋转移力矩驱动的磁畴壁运动对 Co₃Sn2S2（一种磁性韦尔半金属）进行磁调制。我们检查 d.c. 的影响。使用反常霍尔电阻测量来测量磁反转电流，并使用飞行时间测量来测量磁畴壁运动。在 160 K 时，驱动畴壁运动的阈值电流密度在零外场时小于 5.1×10⁵ A cm-2，在中等外场 (0.2 kOe) 时小于 1.5×105 A cm-2。自旋转移矩有效场在 150 K 时可达 2.4 至 5.6 kOe MA⁻1 cm²。

English Abstract

The efficiency of spintronic devices can be improved by generating higher effective magnetic fields with lower working currents. Spin-transfer torques can drive magnetic domain wall motion in a device composed of a single material, but a high threshold current density is typically required to move the domain wall and improving the effective magnetic field in common itinerant ferromagnets is difficult. Here we report magnetism modulation in Co₃Sn₂S₂, a magnetic Weyl semimetal, via spin-transfer-torque-driven domain wall motion. We examine the effect of d.c. current on magnetic reversal using anomalous Hall resistance measurements and domain wall motion using time-of-flight measurements. At 160 K, the threshold current density for driving domain wall motion is less than 5.1×10⁵ A cm⁻² at zero external field and less than 1.5×10⁵ A cm⁻² at a moderate external field (0.2 kOe). The spin-transfer-torque effective field can reach as high as 2.4 to 5.6 kOe MA⁻¹ cm² at 150 K.

基于单层六方晶格的交磁镜面对称工程#

原题：Mirror-symmetry engineering of altermagnetism based on monolayer hexagonal lattices
作者：Han-Bing Li、Zhi-Gang Shao。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-02

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、magnetic materials。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

交变磁性（AM）是一种新型共线反铁磁相，其特征是自旋极化带和零净磁化强度。然而，在单层六方晶格中实现增材制造受到晶格对称性的强烈限制。在这里，我们提出了一种基于镜面对称的设计原理和实现方案，用于实现基于单层六方晶格的增材制造，并使用多孔石墨炔（HGY）作为代表系统进行了验证。通过检查沿镜面对称轴引入的氢化非金属单元 X-H（X = B、C、N、P 和 S）并将分析扩展到 N-F，我们确定了形成两个通过镜面反射相关的反平行自旋亚晶格的构型。 N-H 结构形成稳定的交变磁基态，动量相关的自旋分裂高达 148.2 meV。磁性源自π-π超交换相互作用，H/F功能化通过调节局部电荷分布来稳定本征AM。我们的工作为轻元素交磁材料的设计提供了见解。

English Abstract

Altermagnetism (AM) is a novel collinear antiferromagnetic phase characterized by spin-polarized bands and zero net magnetization. However, realizing AM in monolayer hexagonal lattices is strongly constrained by lattice symmetry. Here, we propose a mirror-symmetry-based design principle and implementation scheme for realizing AM based on monolayer hexagonal lattices and validate it using holey graphyne (HGY) as a representative system. By examining hydrogenated nonmetal units X-H (X = B, C, N, P, and S) introduced along the mirror-symmetry axis and extending the analysis to N-F, we identify the configurations that form two antiparallel spin sublattices related by mirror reflection. The N-H structure forms a stable altermagnetic ground state, with a momentum-dependent spin splitting up to 148.2 meV. The magnetism originates from π-π superexchange interactions, and H/F functionalization stabilizes intrinsic AM by regulating the local charge distribution. Our work offers insights into the design of light-element altermagnetic materials.

两个斯格明子合并中的非线性相互作用和拓扑选择规则：斯格明子纹理的出现#

原题：Nonlinear Interaction and Topological Selection Rules in two Skyrmion Merging: Emergence of Skyrmionium Textures

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：skyrmion、dzyaloshinskii-moriya interaction。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

紧密间隔的磁性斯格明子的非线性相互作用仍然知之甚少，特别是控制它们合并成单个斯格明子或稳定的斯格明子的条件。在这里，我们使用基于朗道-利夫希茨-吉尔伯特动力学的分析模型和微磁模拟来研究两个尼尔斯格明子的合并动力学。我们通过改变初始分离、极性、缠绕数和斯格明子尺寸来研究交换、Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用、各向异性和静磁能的影响。我们的结果揭示了拓扑选择规则：具有相同极性和相反缠绕数的斯格明子在中间重叠处形成稳定的斯格明子，而所有其他配置松弛成单个斯格明子。我们还确定了一个临界半径，低于该半径，斯格明离子就会塌陷，这表明它的形成需要适当的拓扑结构和最小尺寸。能量分析表明，该转变涉及交换挫败的协同松弛以及 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 和静磁能的重新分配，从而形成双畴壁结构。由此产生的斯格米离子在平衡半径周围表现出持续的呼吸振荡，证实了其作为亚稳态的动态稳定性。这些发现建立了控制斯格明子合并的预测框架，并为手性磁性纳米结构中的斯格明子纹理工程提供了设计指南。

English Abstract

The nonlinear interaction of closely spaced magnetic skyrmions remains poorly understood, particularly the conditions governing their merger into a single skyrmion or a stable skyrmionium. Here, we investigate the merging dynamics of two Néel skyrmions using analytical modelling and micromagnetic simulations based on Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert dynamics. We examine the effects of exchange, Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction, anisotropy, and magnetostatic energies by varying the initial separation, polarity, winding number, and skyrmion size. Our results reveal a topological selection rule: skyrmions with identical polarity and opposite winding numbers form a stable skyrmionium at intermediate overlap, while all other configurations relax into a single skyrmion. We also identify a critical radius below which the skyrmionium collapses, showing that its formation requires both the appropriate topology and a minimum size. Energy analysis reveals that the transition involves cooperative relaxation of exchange frustration and redistribution of Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya and magnetostatic energies, leading to a double-domain-wall structure. The resulting skyrmionium exhibits persistent breathing oscillations around an equilibrium radius, confirming its dynamical stability as a metastable state. These findings establish a predictive framework for controlling skyrmion merging and provide design guidelines for engineering skyrmionium textures in chiral magnetic nanostructures.

压力相关相图揭示了Altermagnetic FeOOH中的量子相变以及压电异常霍尔效应的实现#

原题：Pressure-correlation phase diagram unraveling quantum phase transitions in altermagnetic ϵ-FeOOH and realization of piezo-anomalous Hall effect
作者：Arijit Mandal、Nayana Devaraj、Birabar Ranjit Kumar Nanda。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-03

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition、first-principles calculations、magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们建立了一个压力相关相图，该图在三个临界压力（ Pc > Pc ₂ > Pc ）下产生羟基铁（ FeOOH ）的多个压力可调量子相变。这种转变源于电子相关、晶体场和洪德耦合之间的竞争。第一原理计算表明，早期报道的相关性敏感Pc *的高自旋（ HS ）到低自旋（ LS ）交叉伴随着从电荷转移到莫特绝缘（ CTI-MI ）阶段的转变，以及自旋量子化轴（ SQA ）从HATY到HATX的变化。HS-LS交叉和CTI-MI过渡是由晶场分裂的同时增加和Hund耦合的减少所驱动的。控制从中心对称Pnnm相到非中心对称P2 nm相的结构转变的临界压力Pc ₂被发现与电子相关性无关。当相关性强时，在相图的低压状态下会出现第三个临界压力（ Pc * ）。为了验证最近的实验观察结果， Pc负责将SQA从HATZ更改为HATY。通过在P2 nm和Pnnm相中表现出改变磁性，并且仅对沿着hatx和haty的SQA具有有限的异常霍尔电导率（ AHC ） ， FeOOH实现了独特的压电异常霍尔效应，在这种效应中，人们可以在较低的压力下打开AHC ，并在较高的压力下改变其方向。

English Abstract

We establish a pressure-correlation phase diagram that yields multiple pressure-tunable quantum phase transitions in iron oxyhydroxide (ϵ-FeOOH) across three critical pressures, Pc₁>Pc₂>Pc₃. The transitions arise from the competition among electron-correlation, crystal-field, and Hund's coupling. First-principles calculations reveal that the earlier reported high-spin (HS) to low-spin (LS) crossover at correlation-sensitive Pc₁ is accompanied by a transition from charge-transfer to Mott insulating (CTI-MI) phase, and a change in the spin-quantization axis (SQA) from haty to hatx. The HS-LS crossover and CTI-MI transition is driven by the simultaneous increase in crystal-field splitting and decrease in Hund's coupling. The critical pressure Pc₂ governing the structural transition from centrosymmetric Pnnm phase to noncentrosymmetric P2₁nm phase is found to be independent of electron-correlation. When correlation is strong, a third critical pressure (Pc₃) emerges in the lower-pressure regime of the phase diagram. Validating the recent experimental observation, Pc₃ is responsible for changing the SQA from hatz to haty. By exhibiting altermagnetism in both P2₁nm and Pnnm phases and possessing finite anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) only for SQA along hatx and haty, ϵ-FeOOH enables a unique piezo-anomalous Hall effect where one can switch on the AHC at a lower pressure and change its direction at a higher pressure.

反铁磁单层中的拓扑自旋纹理#

原题：Topological spin textures in an antiferromagnetic monolayer
作者：Felix Zahner、Tim Drevelow、Roberto Lo Conte、Roland Wiesendanger、Stefan Heinze、Kirsten von Bergmann。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-02

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations、magnetic materials、skyrmion。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

拓扑自旋结构（例如磁性斯格明子）在自旋电子学中的各种应用中具有重要意义和前景。据预测，斯格明子也会出现在反铁磁材料中，它们表现出优异的传输特性。它们在合成反铁磁体中通过实验得到揭示，然而，在本征原子反铁磁体中仍然难以捉摸。在这里，我们演示了如何在反铁磁单层中稳定拓扑自旋结构。使用自旋偏振扫描隧道显微镜，我们观察到Ta(110)上Mn单层中的反铁磁自旋螺旋和Mn双层中的共线反铁磁状态。结合第一原理计算和微磁模拟揭示，双层自旋结构的边界附近相当于单层中反铁磁斯格明子形式的一半。我们的模拟显示了反铁磁材料系统中的拓扑状态如何通过横向异质结构内的配置来控制，从而产生微不足道的非共面状态或反铁磁斯格明子。

English Abstract

Topological spin structures such as magnetic skyrmions are of fundamental interest and promising for various types of applications in spintronics. Skyrmions have been predicted to emerge also in antiferromagnetic materials where they exhibit superior transport properties. They were experimentally revealed in synthetic antiferromagnets, however, still remain elusive in intrinsic atomic antiferromagnets. Here, we demonstrate how topological spin structures can be stabilized in an antiferromagnetic monolayer. Using spin-polarized scanning tunneling microscopy, we observe an antiferromagnetic spin spiral in the Mn monolayer and a collinear antiferromagnetic state in the Mn double-layer on Ta(110). Near the boundary to the double-layer spin structures equivalent to half of an antiferromagnetic skyrmion form in the monolayer as revealed in combination with first-principles calculations and micromagnetic simulations. Our simulations show how the topological state in antiferromagnetic material systems can be controlled by the configuration within a lateral heterostructure, resulting in trivial non-coplanar states or antiferromagnetic skyrmions.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：spin-orbit coupling、phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

伊辛超导体最近引起了极大的关注，因为它们对远远超出泡利顺磁极限的磁场的抵抗能力，以及它们作为探索二维材料中自旋轨道耦合、谷自由度和超导配对相互作用的平台的更广泛作用。它们对外部磁场的保护依赖于强伊辛自旋轨道耦合，这种耦合源于面内反演对称性破缺。由于 Ising SC 的重原子性质，门控或界面效应可能会产生较小但相当大的 Rashba SOC。在这里，我们研究具有吸引力的 s 波相互作用的伊辛超导体双谷模型内的 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合。我们证明 Rashba SOC 在伊辛超导体中引发一级量子相变。在低温下，这意味着超导有序参数在量子临界点附近突然消失。然而，这种效应在高温下消失。我们的研究结果表明，伊辛 SC 的低温和高温物理在双谷模型中在数量和质量上都有所不同。

English Abstract

Ising superconductors have attracted significant attention recently due to their resilience against magnetic fields far beyond the Pauli-paramagnetic limit as well as their broader role as a platform for exploring the interplay of spin-orbit coupling, valley degrees of freedom and superconducting pairing in two-dimensional materials. Their protection against external magnetic field relies on strong Ising spin-orbit coupling, which originates from in-plane inversion symmetry breaking. Due to the heavy atom nature of Ising SCs, a smaller but sizable Rashba SOC can arise from gating or interfacial effects. Here, we study Rashba spin-orbit coupling within a two-valley model of Ising superconductors with an attractive s-wave interaction. We show that Rashba SOC induces a first order quantum phase transition in Ising superconductors. At low temperatures, this means the superconducting order parameter vanishes abruptly in the vicinity of the quantum critical point. This effect, however, disappears at high temperatures. Our findings demonstrate that the low- and high temperature physics of Ising SCs are quantitatively and qualitatively different in the two-valley model.

IRIS：对结构相变的对称衍生路径和能量景观的代理眼#

原题：IRIS: an agentic eye on symmetry-derived paths and energy landscapes of structural phase transitions
作者：Sukriti Manna、Sraddha Agrawal、Ramamoorthy Ramesh、Panchapakesan Ganesh、Pierre Darancet、Subramanian K. R. S. Sankaranarayanan。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-03

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition、ferroelectric、multiferroic。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

结构相变支撑着铁电和多铁功能，但构建可解释的能源景观仍然依赖于专业知识。预测这种行为取决于以对称适应序参数扩展的朗道自由能：它们的系数对转变进行分类，揭示畸变之间的耦合，设置切换路径和障碍，并在更大尺度上参数化相场和有效哈密尔顿模型。从第一原理获得这些系数意味着沿着对称导出的结构路径对玻恩-奥本海默能量表面进行采样，这需要晶体学分析、可靠的结构生成和广泛的计算，特别是对于耦合的多维有序参数。在这里，我们介绍 IRIS，这是一种代理工作流程，可将简单语言请求和晶体结构转换为对称适应的能量景观和拟合的朗道能量模型。确定性门控制结构、计算和拟合的接受，而语言模型解释请求并选择受约束的恢复操作。六种化合物的应用包括六维 BiFeO₃ 景观，它将极性和倾斜振幅之间的竞争与方向锁定分开，并对同质开关路径进行排序。受控重放检测到所有 53 个注入故障；禁用门会导致 14 个无提示错误。四次预期的 BaTiO₃ 运行总共在 34 分钟内通过 96 次密度泛函计算生成了合适的景观，而无需干预人类决策。 IRIS 使物理模型构建成为一种可重用的计算能力，并提供了一个基于显式验证的科学自动化框架。

English Abstract

Structural phase transitions underpin ferroelectric and multiferroic functionality, but constructing interpretable energy landscapes remains dependent on specialist expertise. Predicting this behaviour rests on Landau free energies expanded in symmetry-adapted order parameters: their coefficients classify a transition, expose the couplings between distortions, set switching paths and barriers, and parametrize phase-field and effective-Hamiltonian models at larger scales. Obtaining these coefficients from first principles means sampling the Born--Oppenheimer energy surface along symmetry-derived structural paths, which requires crystallographic analysis, reliable structure generation and extensive computation, especially for coupled multidimensional order parameters. Here we introduce IRIS, an agentic workflow that converts plain-language requests and crystal structures into symmetry-adapted energy landscapes and fitted Landau energy models. Deterministic gates govern acceptance of structures, calculations and fits, while language models interpret requests and select constrained recovery actions. Applications across six compounds include a six-dimensional BiFeO₃ landscape that separates competition between polar and tilt amplitudes from directional locking and ranks homogeneous switching pathways. Controlled replays detected all 53 injected faults; disabling gates allowed 14 silent errors. Four prospective BaTiO₃ runs produced fitted landscapes from 96 density-functional calculations in 34 minutes total without intervening human decisions. IRIS makes physical model construction a reusable computational capability and provides a framework for scientific automation grounded in explicit verification.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

交变磁学通过揭示非相对论性自旋分裂推进了补偿磁体的研究，传统上将其分为严格的偶校验或奇校验自旋纹理。在这里，我们在全三维补偿磁体中建立了分量解析的混合宇称自旋分裂，并确定了其微观起源。利用第一原理计算、紧束缚和 k·p 模型以及自旋群对称性分析，我们证明了 TbB₄ 的非共面基态对面内自旋分量强制执行奇宇称 p 波和 f 波状纹理，而面外分量则保留偶宇称 d 波交磁特性。至关重要的是，p 波结构不是由相对论自旋轨道耦合驱动的，而是由非共面序的标量自旋手性产生的交错出射通量驱动的。这种混合宇称结构决定了不同的传输指纹，包括对手性域反转做出特征性响应的体非相对论埃德尔斯坦响应和自旋霍尔响应。自旋轨道耦合还可以实现贝里曲率偶极子。这些结果确立了稀土四硼化物作为工程复杂自旋电荷转换现象的强大平台。

English Abstract

Altermagnetism has advanced the study of compensated magnets by revealing non-relativistic spin splitting, traditionally classified into strictly even- or odd-parity spin textures. Here, we establish component-resolved mixed-parity spin splitting in a fully three-dimensional compensated magnet and identify its microscopic origin. Using first-principles calculations, tight-binding and k · p models, along with spin-group symmetry analysis, we demonstrate that the non-coplanar ground state of TbB₄ enforces odd-parity p- and f-wave-like textures for the in-plane spin components while the out-of-plane component retains an even-parity d-wave altermagnetic character. Crucially, the p-wave texture is driven not by relativistic spin-orbit coupling but by a staggered emergent flux arising from the scalar spin chirality of the non-coplanar order. This mixed-parity structure dictates distinct transport fingerprints, including bulk non-relativistic Edelstein and spin Hall responses that respond characteristically to reversal of the chirality domains. Spin-orbit coupling additionally enables a Berry curvature dipole. These results establish the rare-earth tetraborides as a robust platform for engineering complex spin-charge conversion phenomena.

无定形二氧化硅中极化子和双极化子本征陷阱的统计稳健表征揭示了网络灵活性在电子定位中的作用#

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：polaron、density functional theory、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

无定形二氧化硅 (a-SiO2) 中的本征电子局域化会导致二氧化硅基电子材料中的电荷俘获和缺陷产生，这种现象会降低逻辑器件的性能，但可用于存储器应用。然而，一电子和二电子陷阱的结构分布仍未完全表征，这主要是因为a-SiO2的无序共价网络跨越了巨大的构型空间。为了解决这个问题，我们进行统计采样，从通过经典熔体淬火分子动力学模拟生成的 2512 个模型的结构收敛父集中选择具有代表性的 200 个无缺陷块状 a-SiO2 结构的模型子集。然后，我们使用 HSE06 密度泛函理论计算来表征该子集中固有的一电子陷阱和二电子陷阱。对中性前体位点的分析表明，捕获倾向并非由通用的 O-Si-O 角度或 Si-O 键长决定；它由松弛陷阱几何结构的可访问性控制，需要局部应变和中程网络灵活性。单电子主要在扭曲的 SiO4 四面体上形成边缘中心极化子，占据态比导带最小值低 3.1-4.3 eV。第二个电子的注入会驱动更强的晶格弛豫，最常见的是 Si-O 键伸长导致断裂，从而形成三重配位的 Si (Si3) 双极化子，在某些情况下形成 Si-Si 键合的双极化子构型。计算出的双极化子能量范围与与本征电子局域化相关的 3.7、4.7 和 6.4 eV 处的光学吸收特征一致。

English Abstract

Intrinsic electron localization in amorphous silica (a-SiO2) contributes to charge trapping and defect generation in silica-based electronic materials - a phenomenon that degrades logic-device performance but can be exploited for memory applications. However, the structural distribution of one- and two-electron traps remains incompletely characterized, largely because the disordered covalent network of a-SiO2 spans a vast configurational space. To address this, we perform statistical sampling to select a representative 200-model subset of defect-free bulk a-SiO2 structures from a structurally converged parent set of 2512 models, generated via classical melt-quench molecular dynamics simulations. We then characterize the intrinsic one- and two-electron traps in this subset using HSE06 density functional theory calculations. Analysis of neutral precursor sites shows that trapping propensity is not determined by a universal O-Si-O angle or Si-O bond length; it is controlled by the accessibility of the relaxed trap geometry, requiring both local strain and medium-range network flexibility. Single electrons predominantly form edge-centred polarons on distorted SiO4 tetrahedra, with occupied states 3.1-4.3 eV below the conduction band minimum. Injection of a second electron drives stronger lattice relaxation, most commonly Si-O bond elongation leading to rupture, thereby forming threefold-coordinated-Si (Si3) bipolarons and, in some cases, Si-Si-bonded bipolaron configurations. The calculated bipolaron energy ranges are consistent with optical absorption features at 3.7, 4.7, and 6.4 eV associated with intrinsic electron localization.

Temperature-Dependent Raman Signatures of Local Symmetry Breaking in Vacancy Double Perovskite Cs₂SnI₆#

作者：Tomke E. Glier、Nicholas J. Weadock、Julian A. Vigil、Cole Shank、Xixi Qin、Gabriel J. Graf 等 10 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-03

📄 摘要：Understanding and controlling the optical, thermal and mechanical properties of K₂PtCl₆-type double perovskites is essential for the design of lead-free, high-performance thermoelectric and optoelectronic materials. In t…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite、density functional theory、phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Understanding and controlling the optical, thermal and mechanical properties of K₂PtCl₆-type double perovskites is essential for the design of lead-free, high-performance thermoelectric and optoelectronic materials. In this study, we investigate Cs₂SnI₆ using temperature-dependent near-resonance Raman scattering, complemented by density functional theory-based phonon calculations. The appearance of Raman-forbidden phonon modes in the Raman response at cryogenic temperatures provides strong evidence for local symmetry breaking that does not develop into a global structural phase transition. Through detailed analysis of phonon intensities, energies, and spectral lineshapes, we characterize the temperature evolution of the symmetry-broken structural response of Cs₂SnI₆. Below 200 K, multi-phonon scattering is strongly enhanced, and symmetry-forbidden Raman modes emerge. The temperature dependence of phonon intensities follows a phenomenological Arrhenius behavior, revealing an activation energy of 15-20 meV. These results establish temperature-dependent Raman spectroscopy as a sensitive probe of local structural distortions and anharmonic structural dynamics in Cs₂SnI₆, providing a robust experimental basis for discussing the microscopic origins of symmetry breaking and electron-phonon coupling in halide double perovskites.

Universal dynamics of quantum phase transitions with fractional long-range interactions#

作者：Andraás Grabarits、Lincoln D. Carr、Adolfo del Campo。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-04
⚡ 磁性/铁电
AI×科学交叉2方向匹配8

📄 摘要：The complete statistical characterization of defects generated in a quantum phase transition has mostly been studied in exactly solvable models, and remains little explored in nonintegrable systems such as long-range int…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition、domain wall。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

The complete statistical characterization of defects generated in a quantum phase transition has mostly been studied in exactly solvable models, and remains little explored in nonintegrable systems such as long-range interacting spin chains. We characterize the full defect counting statistics in a nonintegrable long-range spin chain, the fractional Ising model, by time-dependent variational-principle simulations within the matrix-product-state and matrix-product-operator formalism. Fractional interactions capture emergent multiscale correlations and long-range effects in nature, governed by the order of the underlying fractional derivative. Such couplings can be approximated on existing quantum simulation platforms, where the full defect statistics can be measured. The defect number cumulants follow power laws in the driving time, with exponents that vary continuously with the fractional order. At low fractional order, deep in the long-range regime, the mean defect number decays more slowly than predicted by the Kibble-Zurek mechanism (KZM), while at intermediate order the KZM holds with the numerically extracted many-body critical exponents. At large fractional order, the exactly solvable free-fermion limit of the model becomes progressively more accurate towards the short-range limit. We derive exact bounds on the kink-number cumulants and on the kink-kink correlations, implying an exact Gaussian limiting distribution for large system sizes. In the many-body simulations, however, kinks are more correlated, consistent with the confinement of domain walls, and these bounds are violated in the long-range regime, also in the bulk of the chain. Nevertheless, the kink distribution still approaches a Gaussian for large defect numbers, with proportionality partially broken between the cumulants.

Nonadiabatic molecular dynamics simulation with external field#

作者：Chengji Li、Fan Zheng。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-03
⚡ 磁性/铁电
AI×科学交叉2方向匹配8

📄 摘要：Nonadiabatic molecular dynamics simulations with classical path approximation (CPA-NAMD) cannot simulate the carrier dynamics under an external field. We develop CPA-NAMD-extF, a first-principles density-matrix framework…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics、perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Nonadiabatic molecular dynamics simulations with classical path approximation (CPA-NAMD) cannot simulate the carrier dynamics under an external field. We develop CPA-NAMD-extF, a first-principles density-matrix framework for calculating field-driven electronic responses along precomputed finite-temperature ionic trajectories. The method combines explicit external-field coupling with self-consistent updates of electronic density while retaining the computational efficiency of the classical-path approximation. Benchmarks against regular TDDFT methods show close agreement for time-dependent dipoles, finite-field currents, and linear optical responses. Applications to the linear absorption spectrum of crystalline and amorphous SiO₂ demonstrate its capability to large and structurally complex systems. This method is also applied to the 2D hybrid perovskite (BA)₂CsPb₂Br₇. Finite-temperature calculations show that the second-order opto-current response is strongly affected by structural fluctuations. Real-space DC current density further reveals pronounced Br-centered vortex currents, and its microscopic origin is investigated.

预热动态相变和共振熔化的观察#

原题：Observation of prethermal dynamical phase transitions and resonant melting
作者：Siva Darbha、Alexey Khudorozhkov、Pedro L. S. Lopes、Fangli Liu、Ermal Rrapaj、Jan Balewski 等 14 人。Nature，2026-11-04
⚡ 磁性/铁电
AI×科学交叉2方向匹配6

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：可编程中性原子量子模拟器用于探索多达 180 个量子位的自旋模型中的淬灭动力学，揭示预热动态相变和共振熔化。

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

可编程中性原子量子模拟器用于探索多达 180 个量子位的自旋模型中的淬灭动力学，揭示预热动态相变和共振熔化。

English Abstract

The dynamics of isolated quantum systems following a sudden quench has a central role in many areas of material science, high-energy physics and quantum chemistry1,2,3,4. Such far-from-equilibrium quantum dynamics—which features complex phenomena with implications for thermalization, non-equilibrium phase transitions and Floquet phase engineering5,6,7,8—is challenging to study numerically, in particular, in high-dimensional systems9,10. Here we use a programmable neutral-atom quantum simulator11to systematically explore quench dynamics in spin models with up to 180 qubits. By initializing the system in a product state and performing quenches across a broad parameter space, we discover several stable, qualitatively distinct dynamical regimes. We trace their robustness to Floquet-like prethermal steady states8that are stabilized over exponentially long emergent timescales by strong dynamical constraints. We further observe sharp peaks in the dynamical response that are quantitatively explained by the structured melting of prethermalization through resonances. In two dimensions, we uncover a prethermally driven, first-order dynamical phase transition linked to the proliferation of Néel-order defects6,12, and probe it quantitatively using a generalized Clausius–Clapeyron test. Together, these results establish a programmable route to high-energy prethermal phase structure: long-lived dynamical phases, their resonant breakdown and a first-order dynamical phase transition that is not inherited from the equilibrium phase boundaries.

English Abstract

Wurtzite-structured aluminum scandium nitride (AlScN) ferroelectric materials are potential candidates for non-volatile memory (NVM) applications because of their high remanent polarization (Pr> 100 μC·cm−2) and compatibility with back-end-of-line (BEOL) processes. English Abstract

Perovskite light-emitting diodes (PeLEDs) offer exceptional colour purity and efficiency for next-generation display applications. Despite the recent advances in device stability, achieving long-term stable PeLEDs under display-relevant conditions remains a critical challenge. The actual device half-life (T50) experimentally measured for visible PeLEDs were in most cases limited to below 1000 h for any given brightness, in contrast to the much longer extrapolated lifetimes reported. Here, we demonstrate stable green PeLEDs with a T50lifetime of 1350 h at an initial luminance of 1000 cd m−2(nits), a maximum energy-conversion efficiency of 28.0%, and a peak external quantum efficiency of 24.5%. The exceptional performance is enabled by the insertion of a fluorinated self-assembled monolayer between the metal oxide and polymeric hole-transport layers. This molecular interlayer leads to Ohmic-like characteristics for charge injection, lowering the operating voltage at ~1000 nits to sub-bandgap levels (~2.1 V) where ion migration is suppressed. The accumulated photon output of our PeLEDs over the course of their T50lifespan is found to sharply increase as the driving voltage drops to sub-bandgap values, highlighting the critical role of employing low-voltage device architectures in reaching the 1350-h/1000-nit stability milestone. Finally, a flexible active-matrix PeLED display prototype is developed, showing the potential of the devices for wearable optoelectronics.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations、electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

Altermagnet表现出动量依赖的自旋分裂，没有净磁化，提供了一个独特的平台，磁序、电子结构和晶格动力学交织在一起。在这里，使用第一原理计算，我们证明了原型改变磁体CrSb和MnTe在动量空间中承载有限声子角动量的局部手性声子模，具有六瓣f波纹理。我们的结果表明，手性晶格运动起源于pnictogen/chalcogen亚晶格，而altermagnetic自旋分裂是由磁性过渡金属原子产生的，这表明手性晶格动力学和altermagnetic电子态起源于同一晶体的不同原子亚晶格。在原始化合物中，在每个山谷，反转对称性抑制了净声子角动量，尽管局部环形原子运动。我们进一步证明，由化学取代引起的等电子对称性降低提升了这种抵消，并产生了有限谷声子手性，同时保持了化合物的改变磁性。最重要的是，我们揭示了手性声子通过动量依赖性电子-声子相互作用与动量依赖性自旋分裂电子带耦合，产生电子结构的特征修改，可能通过光发射实验获得。我们的研究结果确立了Altermagnets作为手性声子和自旋选择晶格控制的有前途的平台。

English Abstract

Altermagnets exhibit momentum-dependent spin splitting without net magnetization, providing a unique platform where magnetic order, electronic structure and lattice dynamics intertwine. Here, using first-principles calculations, we demonstrate that the prototypical altermagnets CrSb and MnTe host locally chiral phonon modes carrying finite phonon angular momentum with a six-lobes f-wave texture in momentum space. Our results show that the chiral lattice motion originates from the pnictogen/chalcogen sublattice, while the altermagnetic spin splitting is generated by the magnetic transition-metal atoms, indicating that chiral lattice dynamics and altermagnetic electronic states originate from different atomic sublattices of the same crystal. In pristine compounds, at each valley, inversion symmetry suppresses the net phonon angular momentum despite local circular atomic motion. We further demonstrate that isoelectronic symmetry lowering induced by chemical substitution lifts this cancellation and generates finite valley phonon chirality, while keeping the altermagnetic nature of the compounds intact. Most importantly, we reveal that chiral phonons couple to momentum-dependent spin-split electronic bands through momentum-dependent electron-phonon interaction, producing characteristic modifications of the electronic structure, possibly accessible by photoemission experiments. Our results establish altermagnets as a promising platform for chiral phononics and spin-selective lattice control.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、spin-orbit coupling。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

最近双层和三层菱面体石墨烯的电容测量报告了高于居里温度的大磁熵，表明波动局部矩的形成。进一步的实验支持来自菱面体六层石墨烯的扫描隧道显微镜实验，该实验直接可视化了蜂窝状电子晶体顺序，该顺序在熔化前加热时增强，与波美兰丘克效应相关。受这些实验的启发，我们构建了四分之一金属和半金属相的自旋谷局部矩哈密顿量。我们从约束 Hartree-Fock 计算中提取它们的耦合，并使用随机级数展开量子蒙特卡罗求解它们的有限温度热力学。对于四分之一金属，我们发现 T_c 处有一个连续的 2D-Ising 转变，约为 0.7 K，这个数量级比 Hartree-Fock 估计值 (~30 K) 小，并且更接近几个开尔文的实验尺度。对于半金属，我们研究了在有或没有邻近增强自旋轨道耦合的情况下场感应磁化强度与温度的函数关系。当自旋轨道耦合可以忽略不计时，间隔洪德耦合增强了磁响应。强自旋轨道耦合（可由邻近的过渡金属二硫族化物引起）在零温度下产生标准自旋倾斜基态。加热后，面内偏振比面外偏振下降得更快。令人惊讶的是，尽管电子具有巡回性质，但具有微观确定耦合的局部矩模型似乎无需任何拟合即可捕获菱面体石墨烯的关键有限温度磁特性。

English Abstract

Recent capacitance measurements in bilayer and trilayer rhombohedral graphene reported large magnetic entropy above the Curie temperature, suggesting the formation of fluctuating local moments. Further experimental support comes from scanning tunneling microscopy experiments in rhombohedral hexalayer graphene, which directly visualized honeycomb electron-crystal order that strengthens upon heating before melting, related to the Pomeranchuk effect. Motivated by these experiments, we construct spin-valley local-moment Hamiltonians for the quarter-metal and half-metal phases. We extract their couplings from constrained Hartree-Fock calculations, and solve their finite-temperature thermodynamics with stochastic-series-expansion quantum Monte Carlo. For the quarter metal we find a continuous 2D-Ising transition at T_c approx 0.7 K, an order of magnitude smaller than the Hartree-Fock estimate (~30 K) and closer to the experimental scale of a few kelvin. For the half metal, we study the field-induced magnetization as a function of temperature with and without proximity-enhanced spin-orbit coupling. When spin-orbit coupling is negligible, intervalley Hund's coupling enhances the magnetic response. Strong spin-orbit coupling, as can be induced by a neighboring transition-metal dichalcogenide, gives rise to the standard spin-canted ground state at zero temperature. Upon heating, the in-plane polarization decreases more rapidly than the out-of-plane polarization. Surprisingly, despite the itinerant nature of the electrons, local-moment models with microscopically determined couplings appear to capture key finite-temperature magnetic properties of rhombohedral graphene without any fitting.

SKFitter：根据 s、p、d 和 f 电子、自旋轨道耦合和磁性的第一原理，对称性解析了 Slater Koster 紧束缚模型#

原题：SKFitter: Symmetry resolved Slater Koster tight-binding models from first principles for s, p, d and f electrons, spin orbit coupling and magnetism
作者：Sundeep Kapila、Bhaskaran Muralidharan、Satadeep Bhattacharjee。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-02

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：从第一原理计算构建准确且物理上可解释的紧束缚（TB）哈密顿量仍然具有挑战性，特别是对于表现出复杂轨道杂化、自旋轨道耦合（SOC）和磁序的材料。 SKFitter 是一个开源 Python 框架，用于直接根据使用 Quantum ESPRESSO 或 VASP 执行的密度泛函理论 (DFT) 计算构建和优化对称解析的 Slater Koster TB 模型。该框架支持 s 到 f 轨道，结合了现场 SOC 和现场解决的磁交换相互作用，并适…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

从第一原理计算构建准确且物理上可解释的紧束缚（TB）哈密顿量仍然具有挑战性，特别是对于表现出复杂轨道杂化、自旋轨道耦合（SOC）和磁序的材料。 SKFitter 是一个开源 Python 框架，用于直接根据使用 Quantum ESPRESSO 或 VASP 执行的密度泛函理论 (DFT) 计算构建和优化对称解析的 Slater Koster TB 模型。该框架支持 s 到 f 轨道，结合了现场 SOC 和现场解决的磁交换相互作用，并适应正交和非正交基础。一个中心特征是轨道流形的对称解析参数化，它允许在对称允许的轨道组之间以及不同的相邻壳层之间进行独立的两个中心跳跃积分。 SKFitter 还可以识别缺失的轨道贡献并指导系统基组细化并通过权重优化参数。由此产生的哈密顿量可用于评估反常 (AHC) 和自旋霍尔电导率 (SHC) 以及拓扑不变量。我们使用半金属、半导体、过渡金属、f 电子化合物和磁性系统的八种代表性材料演示了该框架。对称解析模型在费米能级 1 eV 范围内实现了 9 至 41 meV 的带能误差。拟合的哈密顿量再现了 Pt 的 SHC 响应、Fe 的 AHC 的 SOC 依赖性以及 MnTe 的动量依赖性交变磁自旋分裂的特征，而没有任何交变磁体特定相互作用。对于 EuMn2Bi2，投影引导轨道细化将近费米能级拟合误差从 120 meV 降低至 32 meV。总之，这些结果将 SKFitter 确立为一个灵活且可重复的框架，用于开发复杂材料的对称一致、物理可解释的 TB 模型。

English Abstract

Constructing accurate and physically interpretable tight binding (TB) Hamiltonians from first principles calculations remains challenging, particularly for materials exhibiting complex orbital hybridization, spin orbit coupling (SOC), and magnetic order. SKFitter is an open-source Python framework for constructing and optimizing symmetry resolved Slater Koster TB models directly from density functional theory (DFT) calculations performed using Quantum ESPRESSO or VASP. The framework supports s to f orbitals, incorporates on site SOC and site resolved magnetic exchange interactions, and accommodates both orthogonal and nonorthogonal basis. A central feature is the symmetry resolved parameterization of orbital manifolds, which allows independent two centre hopping integrals between symmetry allowed orbital groups and across distinct neighbour shells. SKFitter also identifies missing orbital contributions and guide systematic basis set refinement and optimizes parameters through weights. The resulting Hamiltonian can be used to evaluate anomalous (AHC) and spin Hall conductivities (SHC), and topological invariants. We demonstrate the framework using eight representative materials spanning semimetals, semiconductors, transition metals, f-electron compounds, and magnetic systems. Symmetry resolved models achieve band energy errors of 9 to 41 meV within 1 eV of the Fermi level. The fitted Hamiltonians reproduce characteristic features of the SHC response of Pt, the SOC dependence of the AHC of Fe, and the momentum dependent altermagnetic spin splitting of MnTe without any altermagnet specific interaction. For EuMn2Bi2, projection guided orbital refinement reduces the near Fermi level fitting error from 120 to 32 meV. Together, these results establish SKFitter as a flexible and reproducible framework for developing symmetry consistent, physically interpretable TB models of complex materials.

作者：Daniel Lozano-Gómez、Owen Benton、Michel J. P. Gingras、Han Yan。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-04

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：烧绿石晶格磁体中的受挫磁性已被证明是自旋液体的实验和理论研究中最富有成果的环境。除了自旋冰的典型情况之外，最近的工作还发现了由该晶格的通用最近邻各向异性自旋哈密顿量产生的各种新的经典和量子自旋液体。然而，一直缺乏对这些奇异物质状态进行彻底、系统分类和表征的通用框架，以及该模型可以支持的所有可能的自旋液体的详尽列表，也许最有趣的是，它们的新兴场论描述的相应结构是什么。在这项工作中，我们开发了这样一个理论框架来识别稳定不同经典自旋液体的相互…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：monte carlo simulation、spin hamiltonian、magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

烧绿石晶格磁体中的受挫磁性已被证明是自旋液体的实验和理论研究中最富有成果的环境。除了自旋冰的典型情况之外，最近的工作还发现了由该晶格的通用最近邻各向异性自旋哈密顿量产生的各种新的经典和量子自旋液体。然而，一直缺乏对这些奇异物质状态进行彻底、系统分类和表征的通用框架，以及该模型可以支持的所有可能的自旋液体的详尽列表，也许最有趣的是，它们的新兴场论描述的相应结构是什么。在这项工作中，我们开发了这样一个理论框架来识别稳定不同经典自旋液体的相互作用参数，并推导出它们在低温下相应的有效广义高斯定律。将此与蒙特卡罗模拟相结合，我们系统地识别了烧绿石晶格上一般最近邻各向异性自旋哈密顿量的所有经典自旋液体。在此过程中，我们发现了具有奇异形式的广义高斯定律和多极守恒定律的新自旋液体模型。我们的方法使我们能够编译相图中实现的所有自旋液体的图集，提供它们在参数空间中的相互连接以及它们之间的转换的全局图片。我们的工作将为未来对烧绿石晶格上的经典和量子自旋液体的理论和实验研究提供信息，并有助于合理化烧绿石磁体的奇异特性。

English Abstract

Frustrated magnetism in the pyrochlore lattice magnet has proven to be a most fruitful setting for the experimental and theoretical search for spin liquids. Besides the canonical case of spin ice, recent works have identified a variety of new classical and quantum spin liquids engendered by the generic nearest-neighbor anisotropic spin Hamiltonian for that lattice. However, a general framework for the thorough and systematic classification and characterization of these exotic states of matter has been lacking, as has an exhaustive list of all possible spin liquids that this model can support and, perhaps most interesting, what is the corresponding structure of their emergent field theory description. In this work, we develop such a theoretical framework to identify the interaction parameters stabilizing different classical spin liquids and derive their corresponding effective generalized Gauss's laws at low temperatures. Combining this with Monte Carlo simulations, we systematically identify all classical spin liquids for the general nearest-neighbor anisotropic spin Hamiltonian on the pyrochlore lattice. In doing so, we uncover new spin liquid models with exotic forms of generalized Gauss's law and multipole conservation laws. Our approach allows us to compile an atlas of all spin liquids realized in the phase diagram, providing a global picture of their mutual connections in parameter space and transitions between them. Our work will inform future theoretical and experimental studies of classical and quantum spin liquids on the pyrochlore lattice and help rationalize the exotic properties of pyrochlore magnets.

SrMn2Ge2：具有异常磁输运特性的金属系统#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure calculation、phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

报告了先前未探索的层状四方化合物 SrMn2Ge2 的结构、电子和物理性质。该化合物以 ThCr2Si2 型结构结晶，晶体学参数 c/a 比率为 2.535(3)，明显小于相关的 BaMn2Pn2 化合物 (Pn = P、As、Sb、Bi)，表明承载矩的 Mn 离子之间的层间相互作用增强。磁化率测量表明反铁磁基态具有高尼尔温度 Tᵣₘ N > 350~K@。在低温下观察到约 50% 的大正磁阻 (MR)，其遵循指数 n = 1.50(8) 的幂律场依赖性。低温电阻率数据揭示了类似近藤的特征。热容测量显示低于 300~K 时没有相变迹象，进一步证实了金属基态。电子结构计算揭示了三维费米面，并表明存在大量的电子关联效应。

English Abstract

The structural, electronic, and physical properties of previously unexplored layered tetragonal compound SrMn₂Ge₂ are reported. The compound crystallizes in the ThCr₂Si₂-type structure with a crystallographic parameters c/a ratio of 2.535(3), significantly smaller than those of the related BaMn₂Pn₂ compounds (Pn = P, As, Sb, Bi), indicating enhanced interlayer interaction between the moment-bearing Mn-ions. Magnetic susceptibility measurements suggest an antiferromagnetic ground state with a high Neél temperature Tᵣₘ N > 350~K@. A large and positive magnetoresistance (MR) of approximately 50% is observed at low temperatures, which follows a power-law field dependence with exponent n = 1.50(8). Low-temperature electrical resistivity data reveal Kondo-like features. Heat capacity measurements show no signs of a phase transition below 300~K and further confirm a metallic ground state. Electronic structure calculations reveal a three-dimensional Fermi surface and suggest the presence of substantial electronic correlation effects.

作者：Yun-Tak Oh、Hyun-Yong Lee。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-04

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们研究了一系列变形 mathbb Z₃ 环面码波函数中的拓扑相变，这些波函数是通过局部变形和投影测量从簇状态准备的。它们的范数映射到单参数变形的 Q=3 Potts 模型，以及一般情况下具有两个独立的四自旋耦合的三态 Ashkin-Teller 式 (AT₃) 结构。投影纠缠对态 (PEPS) 和变分均匀矩阵乘积态 (VUMPS) 计算可识别环面码 (TC) 相位以及电 (e) 任意子被限制或凝聚的相位。这些相由具有中心电荷 c=4/5、8/5 和孤立的 c=1 反铁磁 (AFM) 端点的关键结构分隔开。归一化有限距离 e-任意子对态范数提供了限制边界的 Fredenhagen-Marcu 型检查，而量子双 D(mathbb Z₃) 的拓扑数据意味着整个有间隙环面码相的拓扑纠缠熵 γ=log3。相对于 mathbb Z2 情况，符号变化折叠的缺失使 AFM 端点展开，并且极端变形达到方形冰，并具有新兴的 U(1) 单形对称性、希尔伯特空间碎片和精确的疤痕配置。

English Abstract

We investigate topological phase transitions in a family of deformed mathbb Z₃ toric-code wavefunctions prepared from a cluster state by local deformations and projective measurements. Their norms map to the Q=3 Potts model for single-parameter deformations and to a three-state Ashkin--Teller-like (AT₃) construction with two independent four-spin couplings in the general case. Projected entangled-pair-state (PEPS) and variational uniform matrix-product-state (VUMPS) calculations identify the toric-code (TC) phase and phases in which electric (e) anyons are confined or condensed. These phases are separated by critical structures with central charges c=4/5, 8/5, and isolated c=1 antiferromagnetic (AFM) endpoints. A normalized finite-distance e-anyon pair-state norm provides a Fredenhagen--Marcu-type check of the confinement boundary, while the topological data of the quantum double D(mathbb Z₃) imply a topological entanglement entropy γ=log3 throughout the gapped toric-code phase. Relative to the mathbb Z₂ case, the absence of sign-change folding leaves the AFM endpoints unfolded, and the extreme deformation reaches square ice with an emergent U(1) one-form symmetry, Hilbert-space fragmentation, and exact scar configurations.

硼碳包合物中的笼型强化i波改变磁性#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

改变磁体的自旋分裂源于与其两个磁性子网格相关的旋转。在已知情况下，它是由晶体结构固定的。在这里，我们展示了房客/体验达人框架将这两个角色分开。在硼-碳包合物中，有序的笼子移除两个访客部位之间的平移，并对处于补偿状态的任何磁性访客施加六个对称强制节点平面的立方i波改变磁性；应变将类重新调整为d-或g-波。我们演示了GdB · C ·的平台：其补偿的反铁磁基态是一种窄间隙半导体，在传导带边缘的自旋分裂为0.19 eV ，其弱固定的Néel矢量根据磁点群打开和关闭螺旋度分辨的光学响应。高压下的热力学稳定性和环境压力下的动力学稳定性表明，可以通过为该族建立的路线来实现。

English Abstract

The spin splitting of an altermagnet arises from a rotation that relates its two magnetic sublattices. In known cases it is fixed by the crystal structure. Here we show that guest-host frameworks separate the two roles. In the boron-carbon clathrates MB₃C₃ the ordered cage removes the translation between the two guest sites and enforces cubic i-wave altermagnetism, with six symmetry-enforced nodal planes, for any magnetic guest in the compensated state; strain retunes the class to d- or g-wave. We demonstrate the platform with GdB₃C₃: its compensated antiferromagnetic ground state is a narrow-gap semiconductor with spin splitting of 0.19 eV at the conduction-band edge, and its weakly pinned Néel vector switches the helicity-resolved optical response on and off according to the magnetic point group. Thermodynamic stability at high pressure and dynamical stability at ambient pressure indicate that it can be made by the route established for this family.

AI / 机器学习专题#

覆盖模型、方法与 AI for Science 应用工作。

01

机器学习分子动力学揭示了浓电解质中的 Grotthuss 输运#

原题：Machine-Learning Molecular Dynamics Reveals Grotthuss Transport in Concentrated Electrolytes
作者：Øystein Gullbrekken、Sondre Kvalvåg Schnell。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-04
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：质子转移是酸性或碱性水溶液中异常高电导率的原因。然而，质子转移机制对浓水溶液电导率的贡献尚不清楚。在这项工作中，我们使用分子动力学模拟和机器学习原子间势 (MLIP)（包括质子转移）来计算不同浓度的 KOH(aq) 系统的输运特性。我们量化了质子转移机制对氢氧根离子和水分子扩散性以及电解质的离子电导率和传输数的贡献。在较低浓度下，贡献很大，但在浓度非常高时，贡献会减弱。

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

质子转移是酸性或碱性水溶液中异常高电导率的原因。然而，质子转移机制对浓水溶液电导率的贡献尚不清楚。在这项工作中，我们使用分子动力学模拟和机器学习原子间势 (MLIP)（包括质子转移）来计算不同浓度的 KOH(aq) 系统的输运特性。我们量化了质子转移机制对氢氧根离子和水分子扩散性以及电解质的离子电导率和传输数的贡献。在较低浓度下，贡献很大，但在浓度非常高时，贡献会减弱。

English Abstract

Proton transfer is responsible for the anomalous high conductivity in acidic or basic aqueous solutions. Yet, the contribution of the proton transfer mechanism to the conductivity of concentrated aqueous solutions is unknown. In this work, we compute the transport properties of KOH(aq) systems with different concentrations, using Molecular Dynamics simulations with a Machine Learning Interatomic Potential (MLIP), which includes proton transfer. We quantify the contribution of the proton transfer mechanism to the diffusivities of hydroxide ions and water molecules, and the ionic conductivity and transport numbers of the electrolytes. The contribution is substantial at lower concentrations, but diminishes at very high concentrations.

02

具有机器学习原子间势的静电 QM/MM 嵌入的混合消息传递和电场表示#

原题：A Hybrid Message-Passing and Electric-Field Representation for Electrostatic QM/MM Embedding with Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials
作者：Raphael T. Husistein、Shobhit S. Chaturvedi、Thomas Weymuth、Beatriz Piniello、Sélène Forget、Kresten Lindorff-Larsen 等 8 人。arXiv · physics，2026-11-02
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：在混合量子力学/分子力学方法中，大型原子系统的一小部分区域通过量子力学进行描述，而系统的其余部分则由经典力场表示。尽管如此，量子计算的计算成本严重限制了可实现的模拟和采样时间。用在量子参考数据上训练的机器学习原子间势（MLIP）代替量子计算可以大大增加可用的模拟时间。然而，准确性和效率很大程度上取决于 MLIP 结合经典（静电）环境信息的具体方式。在这里，我们证明了纯粹基于外部电场衍生特征的现有策略无法区分某些环境，最终限制了其准确性。…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在混合量子力学/分子力学方法中，大型原子系统的一小部分区域通过量子力学进行描述，而系统的其余部分则由经典力场表示。尽管如此，量子计算的计算成本严重限制了可实现的模拟和采样时间。用在量子参考数据上训练的机器学习原子间势（MLIP）代替量子计算可以大大增加可用的模拟时间。然而，准确性和效率很大程度上取决于 MLIP 结合经典（静电）环境信息的具体方式。在这里，我们证明了纯粹基于外部电场衍生特征的现有策略无法区分某些环境，最终限制了其准确性。作为解决方案，我们提出了对等变 TensorNet 架构的扩展，通过将靠近量子区域的经典原子合并到消息传递方案中，并结合从电场导出的特征来描述更远的环境，从而减轻了这一缺点。我们证明，这种组合比单独使用任何一种方法都能实现更高的准确性。

English Abstract

Within a hybrid quantum mechanics/molecular mechanics approach a small region of a large atomistic system is described quantum mechanically, while the rest of the system is represented by a classical force field. Despite this, the computational cost of the quantum calculation severely limits the accessible simulation and sampling time. Replacing the quantum calculation with a machine learning interatomic potential (MLIP) trained on quantum reference data can greatly increase the accessible simulation time. However, the accuracy and efficiency strongly depend on the specific way in which the MLIP incorporates information about the classical (electrostatic) environment. Here, we demonstrate that existing strategies based purely on features derived from an external electric field fail to distinguish certain environments, ultimately limiting their accuracy. As a solution, we propose an extension to the equivariant TensorNet architecture that mitigates this shortcoming by incorporating classical atoms close to the quantum region into the message-passing scheme, combined with features derived from the electric field for describing the environment farther away. We show that this combination achieves higher accuracy than either approach alone.

机器学习完整的主动空间波函数重建#

原题：Machine Learning the Complete Active-Space Wavefunction Reconstruction
作者：Aniruddha Seal、Bhavnesh Jangid、Helen S. Clifford、Andrew L. Ferguson、Laura Gagliardi。arXiv · physics，2026-11-05

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、electronic structure、active learning。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习的原子间势能实现多参考质量的反应动力学，但不保留预测能量和力背后的电子结构。通过独立执行完整的活动空间自洽场 (CASSCF) 计算来恢复此信息既昂贵又可能不一致。我们引入了完整主动空间波函数重建（CAS-WR）模型，该模型从 CASSCF 数据中学习几何相关的电子结构，并重建 CAS 波函数，而无需重复轨道优化。结合基于不确定性的主动学习，CAS-WR 被证明适用于 Diels--Alder 反应和 ceTiC+ 催化的 C--H 激活。该方法能够对跨反应系综的多构型电子结构进行轨迹解析分析。激发态质子转移和过渡金属光催化的初步应用表明，CAS-WR 可以提供沿着复杂的基态和激发态反应动力学恢复电子结构的通用途径。

English Abstract

Machine-learned interatomic potentials enable multireference-quality reaction dynamics but do not retain the electronic structure underlying the predicted energies and forces. Recovering this information by independently performing complete active space self-consistent field (CASSCF) calculations is both expensive and potentially inconsistent. We introduce the Complete Active-Space Wave Function Reconstruction (CAS-WR) model, which learns the geometry-dependent electronic structure from CASSCF data and reconstructs the CAS wave function without repeated orbital optimization. Combined with uncertainty-based active learning, CAS-WR is demonstrated for the Diels--Alder reaction and ceTiC+-catalyzed C--H activation. The approach enables trajectory-resolved analysis of multiconfigurational electronic structure across reactive ensembles. Preliminary applications to excited-state proton transfer and transition-metal photocatalysis suggest that CAS-WR can provide a general route toward recovering electronic structure along complex ground- and excited-state reactive dynamics.

具有神经网络势和核量子效应的激发态分子内质子转移动力学#

原题：Excited-state intramolecular proton transfer dynamics with neural network potentials and nuclear quantum effects

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：neural network potential、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

激发态分子内质子转移是一种典型的超快过程，它构成了许多化学和生物现象的基础，并且对潜在势能景观的变化高度敏感。在这里，我们开发了 10-羟基苯并[h]喹啉的神经网络势，以接近从头计算的精度描述基态和激发态动力学。这些神经网络势能够对质子转移坐标进行有效采样，并通过路径积分分子动力学合并核量子效应。自由能分布表明，激发态分子内质子转移在基本上无势垒的激发态表面上进行，核量子效应减少并平滑了残余势垒。非平衡模拟得出量子氢的平均质子转移时间为 ∼11.5 fs，与实验一致，并揭示了明显的同位素效应，氘和经典原子核的动力学较慢。机理分析将瞬态质子供体-受体收缩确定为关键的启用坐标，而质子在达到该坐标的最小值之前穿过中点，表明动态驱动的转移。共轭主链的微妙变化发生在质子转移之前，反映了早期的电子重组以及质子运动和主链振动之间的相干耦合。键长交替和振动相干性分析揭示了同位素依赖性相干性损失，相对于氢，氘化产生更强的阻尼和更局域的质子/主链动力学。这些结果建立了基于神经网络势的方法作为模拟具有显式核量子效应的激发态质子转移的有效框架。

English Abstract

Excited-state intramolecular proton transfer is a prototypical ultrafast process that forms the foundation for many chemical and biological phenomena and is highly sensitive to changes in the underlying potential energy landscape. Here, we develop neural network potentials for 10-hydroxybenzo[h]quinoline that describe ground- and excited-state dynamics with near ab initio accuracy. These neural network potentials enable efficient sampling of the proton transfer coordinate and incorporation of nuclear quantum effects through path-integral molecular dynamics. The free energy profiles show that excited-state intramolecular proton transfer proceeds on a largely barrierless excited-state surface, with nuclear quantum effects reducing and smoothing the residual barrier. Non-equilibrium simulations yield an average proton transfer time of ∼11.5 fs for quantum hydrogen, consistent with experiment, and reveal a clear isotope effect, with slower dynamics for deuterium and classical nuclei. Mechanistic analysis identifies transient proton donor–acceptor contraction as the key enabling coordinate, while the proton crosses the midpoint prior to reaching the minimum of this coordinate, indicating a dynamically driven transfer. Subtle changes in the conjugated backbone precede proton transfer, reflecting early electronic reorganization and coherent coupling between proton motion and backbone vibrations. Bond-length alternation and vibrational coherence analysis reveal isotope-dependent coherence loss, with deuteration producing stronger damping and more localized proton/backbone dynamics relative to hydrogen. These results establish neural network potential-based approaches as an effective framework for simulating excited-state proton transfer with explicit nuclear quantum effects.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

线性机器学习原子间势，例如超快力场 (UF3)，可以以较低的计算成本提供接近密度泛函理论的准确性。然而，它们的参数数量随着化学物质的数量而增加，限制了它们在多元素系统中的使用。我们用伪相互作用 UF3 (PI-UF3) 来解决这个缩放问题，这是一种因式分解的线性模型，其中 Z ( k ) 真实 k 体势被表示为少量共享伪相互作用 P ( k ) ≪ Z ( k ) 的线性组合。学习元素嵌入是非线性势的标准；在线性模型内部，嵌入成为每个体阶系数矩阵的等级约束，并且使用交替最小二乘法训练所得的双线性问题，其中每个块更新都是一个封闭式线性求解。我们在五元素难熔高熵合金 (HEA) 和 MD22 的子集上对 PI-UF3 进行基准测试，对力进行训练，并进行重新启动和交叉验证。在化学均质 HEA 上，压缩起到隐式正则化的作用：在包含三体项的情况下，PI-UF3 的测试力均方根误差降至未因式分解的 UF3 基线以下，预定义分割上基线参数的 3%，每个交叉验证折叠上基线参数的 7%，并且最大压缩模型恢复了与实验本体熔化温度一致的同核相互作用强度的化学上有意义的排序。在化学异质性 MD22 子集上，UF3 的表达能力不足以导致过度拟合，PI-UF3 从上方接近基线，并在参数计数的约三分之二处达到与基线相当的水平。因此，压缩仅在未分解模型过度拟合的情况下进行正则化，而参数减少在两种情况下都可用。

English Abstract

Linear machine-learning interatomic potentials, such as the Ultra-Fast Force Field (UF3), deliver near-density functional theory accuracy at low computational cost. Their number of parameters, however, grows combinatorially with the number of chemical species, limiting their use on multi-element systems. We address this scaling problem with pseudo-interaction UF3 (PI-UF3), a factorized linear model in which the Z ( k ) real k -body potentials are expressed as linear combinations of a small number P ( k ) ≪ Z ( k ) of shared pseudo-interactions. Learned element embeddings are standard in nonlinear potentials; inside a linear model, the embedding becomes a rank constraint on the coefficient matrix of each body order, and the resulting bilinear problem is trained with alternating least squares, where each block update is one closed-form linear solve. We benchmark PI-UF3 on a five-element refractory high-entropy alloy (HEA) and a subset of MD22, training on forces, with restarts and cross-validation. On the chemically homogeneous HEA, compression acts as implicit regularization: with three-body terms included, PI-UF3’s test force root-mean-squared error drops below the unfactorized UF3 baseline with 3% of the baseline’s parameters on the predefined split and 7% on every cross-validation fold, and the maximally compressed model recovers a chemically meaningful ordering of homonuclear interaction strengths consistent with experimental bulk melting temperatures. On the chemically heterogeneous MD22 subset, where UF3 is not expressive enough to overfit, PI-UF3 approaches the baseline from above and reaches parity with it at about two thirds of the parameter count. Compression thus regularizes only where the unfactorized model overfits, while a parameter reduction is available in both regimes.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

铁 (Fe) 氧化是一种具有重要科学意义和技术意义的基本现象。尽管进行了大量的实验工作，但铁氧化的准确原子建模仍然难以实现，这需要可靠的原子间势，其发展取决于代表性的第一原理训练数据集。然而，铁氧（O）系统以其结构复杂性而闻名，这使得生成高质量的第一原理数据集具有挑战性。在这项工作中，我们提出了微扰增强空间群结构采样（PASS）方法，为由小细胞结构（不超过 10 个原子）组成的 Fe-O 系统生成广泛的数据集。基于原子簇扩展（ACE）框架，我们提出了一种开发可转移机器学习原子间势（MLIP）的系统方法。 ACE 电势的准确性和能力在纯 Fe 和 Fe-O 系统中通过体积、表面和界面特性得到了彻底验证。使用 ACE 电位进行的大规模 Fe 氧化模拟捕获了氧化过程的关键特征，包括与最近的实验观察结果一致的 FeO 样结构的形成。这项工作表明，PASS 方法产生了准确且可转移的 MLIP，可以捕获氧化物生长的反应复杂性，同时保持扩展系统的计算实用性。

English Abstract

Iron (Fe) oxidation is a fundamental phenomenon that is scientifically vital and technologically significant. Despite substantial experimental efforts, accurate atomistic modelling of Fe oxidation remains elusive, which requires a reliable interatomic potential, whose development hinges on a representative first-principles training dataset. However, Fe-oxygen (O) system is known for its structural complexity, rendering the generation of high-quality first-principles dataset challenging. In this work, we propose the Perturbation Augmented Space group structure Sampling (PASS) method to generate extensive dataset for Fe-O system consisting of small-cell structures (no more than 10 atoms). Based on atomic cluster expansion (ACE) framework, we present a systematic approach to developing a transferable machine learning interatomic potential (MLIP). The accuracy and capability of the ACE potential are thoroughly validated across pure Fe and Fe-O systems through bulk, surface, and interface properties. The large-scale Fe oxidation simulations with the ACE potential capture key features of the oxidation process, including the formation of an FeO-like structure consistent with recent experimental observations. This work demonstrates that the PASS method yields an accurate and transferable MLIP that can capture the reactive complexity of oxide growth while remaining computationally practical for extended systems.

等变量表示中的光谱对称指纹#
