关联：与你方向的关键词重合：equivariant graph neural network、graph neural networks、symmetry analysis。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

对称性支配着物理结构的组织，而对称性的破坏揭示了它们的转换。在几何深度学习中，对称性对等变量表示施加了秩约束，限制了等变量图神经网络（ GNN ）的表达能力。在这里，我们表明这些约束可以被利用：等变表示的奇异值谱提供了结构对称的统一指纹。我们引入了光谱对称指纹（ SymFP ） ，通过等级模板匹配和连续对称分析，通过量化超出目标等级约束的光谱能量，实现离散点群识别。SymFP是定向不变的，与架构无关，实现了无参数的GNN实现。其光谱可以在多个靶组中重复使用，由此产生的泄漏评分满足亚组单调性。在合成结构、分子、配位复合物和大型生物组件中， SymFP准确识别点群，跟踪受控对称性破坏，并揭示局部和程度分辨的对称性变化，这些变化可能被全局几何测量所掩盖。这些结果建立了等变表示的光谱结构，作为表征物理系统对称性的一般框架。

English Abstract

Symmetry governs the organization of physical structures, while symmetry breaking reveals their transformations. In geometric deep learning, symmetry imposes rank constraints on equivariant representations, limiting the expressive capacity of equivariant graph neural networks (GNNs). Here we show that these constraints can instead be exploited: the singular-value spectra of equivariant representations provide a unified fingerprint of structural symmetry. We introduce spectral symmetry fingerprints (SymFP), which enable discrete point-group identification through rank-template matching and continuous symmetry analysis by quantifying spectral energy beyond target rank constraints. SymFP is orientation invariant and architecture agnostic, enabling a parameter-free GNN implementation. Its spectra can be reused across multiple target groups, and the resulting leakage scores satisfy subgroup monotonicity. Across synthetic structures, molecules, coordination complexes and large biological assemblies, SymFP accurately identifies point groups, tracks controlled symmetry breaking and reveals local and degree-resolved symmetry changes that can be obscured by global geometric measures. These results establish the spectral structure of equivariant representations as a general framework for characterizing symmetry across physical systems.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural network、density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在可扩展的绿色氢气生产中，识别用于氢气演化反应（ HER ）的地球丰富催化剂是一项核心挑战。我们提出了一种计算流水线，将基于扩散的生成模型、机器学习力场弛豫和图形神经网络（ GNN ）属性预测相结合，以发现现有吸附数据库所涵盖的结构和化学计量之外的双金属SHER催化剂。生成模型（ MatterGen ）在标记有最小氢吸附能量的散装合金结构上进行微调，并朝向接近零的吸附能量。从生成的池中，我们保留了作为新型双金属合金的稳定结构：由训练集金属构建的二元成分，但在该数据库中不存在的化学计量或结构。每个候选人的低指数面通过机器学习力场（ MatterSim ）表面能屏幕进行排名，只有最稳定的终端被转换为显式板坯模型，在所有高对称表面位置吸附氢气，并在相同的力场下放松。在高通量密度泛函理论（ DFT ）数据集上训练的两个GNN代理（ MEGNet和ALIGNN ） ，然后筛选得到的板坯候选。由于这两个模型通过不同的感应偏置对局部环境进行编码，因此我们仅保留两者一致的合金，从而减少了任何单个模型的影响。这种共识筛选与MatterSim能量差三角测量相结合，产生了近热中性双金属候选者的入围名单。八个用平面波DFT验证，其中四个Pd5Au、Pt5Rh、Cu5Pt3和Pd13Ru3在热中性窗口内结合氢。通过声子计算，所有八种合金都是动态稳定的。这四种合金被突出显示为DFT验证的双金属SHER候选物，用于实验随访。

English Abstract

Identifying earth-abundant catalysts for the hydrogen evolution reaction (HER) is a central challenge in scalable green hydrogen production. We present a computational pipeline that couples diffusion-based generative modelling, machine-learning force-field relaxation, and graph neural network (GNN) property prediction to discover bimetallic HER catalysts beyond the structures and stoichiometries covered by existing adsorption databases. A generative model (MatterGen) is fine-tuned on bulk alloy structures labelled with their minimum hydrogen adsorption energy and steered towards adsorption energies near zero. From the generated pool we retain the stable structures that are novel bimetallic alloys: binary compositions built from the training-set metals but at stoichiometries or structures absent from that database. Each candidate's low-index facets are ranked by a machine-learning-force-field (MatterSim) surface-energy screen, and only the most stable terminations are converted into explicit slab models with hydrogen adsorbed at all high-symmetry surface sites and relaxed with the same force field. Two GNN surrogates (MEGNet and ALIGNN), trained on a high-throughput density functional theory (DFT) dataset, then screen the resulting slab candidates. Because the two models encode the local environment through different inductive biases, we retain only the alloys on which both agree, reducing the influence of any single model. This consensus screening, combined with a MatterSim energy-difference triangulation, resulted in a shortlist of near-thermoneutral bimetallic candidates. Eight are validated with plane-wave DFT, and four of them, Pd5Au, Pt5Rh, Cu5Pt3, and Pd13Ru3, bind hydrogen within the thermoneutral window. All eight alloys are dynamically stable by phonon calculations. These four alloys are highlighted as DFT-validated bimetallic HER candidates for experimental follow-up.

中文摘要

第一原理缺陷计算现在可以由 AI 代理自主执行，并通过已建立的计算方法和开源 Python 工具包实现。然而，可靠且可重复的缺陷计算需要明确的科学和技术指导，帮助代理解决歧义并避免计算设置、执行、后处理和分析中的错误。在这里，我们展示了 DefectSkills，这是一组结构化提示，为人工智能代理使用维也纳从头算仿真包 (VASP) 执行半导体和绝缘体点缺陷的第一性原理计算提供人机交互指导。这些提示对人类专业知识进行编码，并指导代理完成晶胞弛豫、电子结构和介电计算、相稳定性和化学势分析、带电缺陷超级晶胞生成和计算、有限尺寸校正、地层能量计算和热力学分析以及数据备份。通过将这种专业知识打包为可重用的代理技能，DefectSkills 支持跨代理 AI 系统和可重复的缺陷计算的一致工作流程执行。

English Abstract

First-principles defect calculations can now be performed autonomously by AI agents, enabled by established computational methods and open-source Python toolkits. However, reliable and repeatable defect calculations require clear scientific and technical guidance that helps agents resolve ambiguities and avoid errors in calculation setup, execution, postprocessing, and analysis. Here, we present DefectSkills, a set of structured prompts that provides human-in-the-loop guidance to AI agents performing first-principles calculations of point defects in semiconductors and insulators using the Vienna Ab initio Simulation Package (VASP). The prompts encode human expertise and guide agents through unit-cell relaxation, electronic-structure and dielectric calculations, phase-stability and chemical-potential analysis, charged-defect supercell generation and calculations, finite-size corrections, formation-energy calculations and thermodynamic analysis, and data backup. By packaging this expertise as reusable agent skills, DefectSkills supports consistent workflow execution across agentic AI systems and reproducible defect calculations.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

金属有机化学气相沉积 (MOCVD) 生长参数的传统工艺优化以获得高质量薄膜依赖于迭代、资源密集型实验运行。虽然预测建模可以规避这种成本和精力，但模拟薄膜生长受到沉积过程截然不同的长度和时间尺度的限制。虽然连续体（例如计算流体动力学（CFD））和第一原理方法（例如分子动力学（MD））努力平衡实验长度/时间尺度和原子精度，但动力学蒙特卡罗（KMC）通过解决原子事件来弥合这一鸿沟，同时在实验长度和时间尺度上保持易于处理。然而，KMC 的准确性依赖于动力学速率参数，而这些参数需要昂贵的密度泛函理论 (DFT) 计算。为了避免这种情况，我们提出了一个框架，可以直接根据实验生长数据校准速率参数。前向 KMC 增长模型与蒙特卡罗树搜索 (MCTS) 优化器耦合在闭环中，该优化器导航高维参数空间，搜索由每个反应速率参数的速率控制程度控制。该方法不调用特定于材料的假设，因此，任何其正向动力学可以简化为一组速率控制事件的沉积过程都适合相同的处理。我们在 GaAs 的 MOCVD 中进行了演示，其中校准模型捕获了所测量的生长速率的特征温度依赖性。该框架还能够恢复三种众所周知的动力学状态，其特征在于速率决定步骤从前体化学吸附到解吸的变化。因此，该框架将常规测量的生长数据转换为沉积过程的机械可解释模型。

English Abstract

Conventional process optimization of the Metal-Organic Chemical Vapor Deposition (MOCVD) growth parameters to achieve high-quality thin films relies on iterative, resource-intensive experimental runs. While predictive modeling can circumvent this cost and effort, simulating thin-film growth is constrained by the vastly different length and time scales of the deposition process. While continuum (e.g., Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD)) and first-principles methods (e.g., Molecular Dynamics (MD)) struggle to balance experimental length/time scales and atomistic accuracy, Kinetic Monte Carlo (KMC) bridges this divide by resolving atomistic events whilst remaining tractable at experimental length and time scales. However, the accuracy of KMC relies on kinetic rate parameters that require costly density functional theory (DFT) calculations. In order to circumvent this, we present a framework that calibrates the rate parameters directly against experimental growth data. A forward KMC growth model is coupled in a closed loop with a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) optimizer that navigates the high-dimensional parameter space, with the search steered by the degree of rate control of each reaction rate parameter. The approach invokes no material-specific assumptions and hence, any deposition process whose forward kinetics can be reduced to a set of rate-controlled events is amenable to the same treatment. We demonstrate it for the MOCVD of GaAs, where the calibrated model captures the characteristic temperature dependence of the measured growth rate. The framework is also able to recover the three well known kinetic regimes characterized by change in the rate determining step from precursor chemisorption to desorption. As a result, the framework converts routinely measured growth data into a mechanistically interpretable model of the deposition process.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 用于高通量材料筛选，但没有正式的可靠性保证。我们表明，在 25,000 种材料的基准上，用作稳定性过滤器的单个 MLIP 会错过 93% 的密度泛函理论 (DFT) 稳定材料（召回率 0.07）。证明材料 (PCM) 通过三个阶段缩小了这一差距：跨组合空间的对抗性伪造、具有 95% 置信区间的引导包络细化以及精益 4 正式认证。审核 CHGNet、TensorNet 和 MACE 揭示了特定于架构的盲点，其成对误差相关性接近于零（r <= 0.13；n = 5,000），这已通过独立 Quantum ESPRESSO 验证（20/20 收敛；中值 DFT/CHGNet 力比 12x）证实。根据 PCM 发现的特征训练的风险模型可预测未见材料的故障 (AUC-ROC = 0.938 +/- 0.004) 并跨架构转移 (跨 MLIP AUC-ROC ~ 0.70；特征重要性 r = 0.877)。在热电筛选案例研究中，经过 PCM 审核的方案发现了单 MLIP 筛选遗漏的 62 种额外稳定材料 - 发现率提高了 25%。

English Abstract

Machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are deployed for high-throughput materials screening without formal reliability guarantees. We show that a single MLIP used as a stability filter misses 93% of density functional theory (DFT)-stable materials (recall 0.07) on a 25,000-material benchmark. Proof-Carrying Materials (PCM) closes this gap through three stages: adversarial falsification across compositional space, bootstrap envelope refinement with 95% confidence intervals, and Lean 4 formal certification. Auditing CHGNet, TensorNet and MACE reveals architecture-specific blind spots with near-zero pairwise error correlations (r <= 0.13; n = 5,000), confirmed by independent Quantum ESPRESSO validation (20/20 converged; median DFT/CHGNet force ratio 12x). A risk model trained on PCM-discovered features predicts failures on unseen materials (AUC-ROC = 0.938 +/- 0.004) and transfers across architectures (cross-MLIP AUC-ROC ~ 0.70; feature importance r = 0.877). In a thermoelectric screening case study, PCM-audited protocols discover 62 additional stable materials missed by single-MLIP screening - a 25% improvement in discovery yield.

FACTO：快速以原子为中心的张量算子，用于准确高效的基础机器学习原子间势#

原题：FACTO: fast atom-centered tensor operator for accurate and efficient foundational machine-learned interatomic potentials

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

高通量推理仍然是等变机器学习原子间势的核心挑战。在这里，我们介绍 FACTO，一种以原子为中心的等变架构，它将不断演化的学习状态和密集通道混合限制在原子上，将邻居相互作用表示为完全可融合的约简。我们通过自定义寄存器累积聚合内核和张量核心 FP32 仿真来补充此设计，加速推理，同时在分子动力学仿真算术下保留 FP32 级能量守恒。对 Matbench Discovery、MLIP Arena 和 MatCalc 的综合评估证明了高吞吐量以及强大的预测准确性和物理可靠性。在 Matbench Discovery 上，四个 FACTO-OAM 变体在具有完整分数的 46 个模型中显示出总体排名总和的顶级性能，推进了准确性-吞吐量 Pareto 前沿。在 MLIP Arena 上，FACTO 在极端加热和压缩下实现了最先进的稳定性，同时实现了氢气燃烧的高保真度。在 MatCalc 上，FACTO 在结构和弹性预测方面优于比较基线，同时在能量和热性能方面保持有竞争力的准确性。

English Abstract

High-throughput inference remains a central challenge for equivariant machine-learned interatomic potentials. Here we introduce FACTO, an atom-centered equivariant architecture that confines evolving learned state and dense channel mixing to atoms, expressing neighbor interactions as fully fusible reductions. We complement this design with custom register-accumulated aggregation kernels and tensor-core FP32 emulation, accelerating inference while retaining FP32-level energy conservation under emulated arithmetic in molecular dynamics. Comprehensive evaluations on Matbench Discovery, MLIP Arena and MatCalc demonstrate high throughput alongside strong predictive accuracy and physical reliability. On Matbench Discovery, the four FACTO-OAM variants show top-tier performance by overall rank sum among 46 models with complete scores, advancing the accuracy-throughput Pareto frontier. On MLIP Arena, FACTO achieves state- of-the-art stability under extreme heating and compression, alongside high fidelity in hydrogen combustion. On MatCalc, FACTO outperforms the compared baselines in structural and elastic predictions while retaining competitive accuracy for energetic and thermal properties.

PaddleMaterials：材料科学的端到端深度学习工具包#

原题：PaddleMaterials: An End-to-End Deep Learning Toolkit for Materials Science

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习支持材料设计、原子模拟和实验表征，但模型和工作流程仍然分散。我们提出了 PaddleMaterials，这是一个集成异构材料学习任务的开源框架，同时保留特定于任务的表示和科学语义。它统一了六个任务类别的数据处理、训练、评估、预训练模型管理和工作流程：属性预测、结构生成、机器学习原子间势、电子结构场、光谱阐明和显微图像处理。任务接口记录物理量、单位、聚合约定和参考，使跨任务组合可验证。共享编译器后端可以使用图形优化、运算符融合和针对硬件的代码生成，通过一种设置从动态图切换到编译执行，而无需更改模型定义。在 11 组的 65 个模型实例中，编译执行加速了 8 组中的每个实例，组中值加速为 11%-224%，组最大加速为 11%-336%。以同几何密度泛函理论（DFT）计算为参考，对LiMnO2和甲苯链接结构生成或光谱阐明、原子间势弛豫、性质预测和电子密度预测进行案例研究。对于 LiMnO2，生成结构和松弛结构的预测形成能与相同几何形状的 PBE+U 参考的差异最多为每个原子 7 meV，而模型步骤的计算成本不到 DFT 单点计算的二十分之一。 PaddleMaterials 将统一材料工作流程扩展到传统属性、潜力和结构生成任务之外，同时支持可验证的跨任务操作和编译执行。

English Abstract

Machine learning supports materials design, atomistic simulation and experimental characterization, yet models and workflows remain fragmented. We present PaddleMaterials, an open-source framework integrating heterogeneous materials-learning tasks while preserving task-specific representations and scientific semantics. It unifies data processing, training, evaluation, pretrained-model management and workflows across six task classes: property prediction, structure generation, machine-learning interatomic potentials, electronic-structure fields, spectrum elucidation and microscopy image processing. Task interfaces record physical quantities, units, aggregation conventions and references, making cross-task composition verifiable. A shared compiler backend enables switching from dynamic-graph to compiled execution through one setting, using graph optimization, operator fusion and hardware-targeted code generation without changing model definitions. Across 65 model instances in 11 groups, compiled execution accelerated every instance in eight groups, with group median speedups of 11%-224% and group maximum speedups of 11%-336%. Case studies on LiMnO2 and toluene link structure generation or spectrum elucidation, interatomic-potential relaxation, property prediction and electron-density prediction, using same-geometry density functional theory (DFT) calculations as references. For LiMnO2, predicted formation energies for generated and relaxed structures differed from same-geometry PBE+U references by at most 7 meV per atom, while the computational cost of model steps was less than one-twentieth of a DFT single-point calculation. PaddleMaterials extends unified materials workflows beyond conventional property, potential and structure-generation tasks while supporting verifiable cross-task operations and compiled execution.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、point defects。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们开发了一种适用于 Mo-S-Al-O 系统的机器学习神经进化势 (NEP)，目标是 c 面蓝宝石 α-Al2O₃(0001) 上的 MoS2 异质外延。使用直接针对界面和方向相关能量的能量差损失函数，对从约 110 万个精心设计的密度泛函理论 (DFT) 配置中提取的 63,611 个 PBE+D3 结构进行了训练。根据保留的数据，能量和力误差为 29 meV/atom 和 0.23 eV/mathringA。通过 DFT 对一系列物理特性进行验证，NEP 的晶格常数再现误差在 0.3% 以内，弹性常数误差在 17% 以内，声子色散的平均偏差低于 2.5%，表面能误差在 1.3% 以内。在MoS2/蓝宝石界面，它再现了0.8%以内的最小粘附能、2.2%以内的界面能以及最稳定的单层取向。它保留了 DFT 预测的边缘、点缺陷 (Spearman ρ = 0.995) 和镜像孪晶边界 (ρ = 1.0) 的能量排序，并再现了 8% 以内的倾斜晶界能量 (ρ = 0.98) 和 14% 以内的堆垛层错能垒。由此产生的潜力使得未来能够研究二维异质外延中缺陷形成的原子尺度机制。

English Abstract

We develop a machine-learned neuroevolution potential (NEP) for the Mo-S-Al-O system, targeting MoS₂ heteroepitaxy on c-plane sapphire, α-Al₂O₃(0001). The potential is trained on 63,611 PBE+D3 structures distilled from about 1.1 million curated density-functional-theory (DFT) configurations, using an energy-difference loss function that directly targets interfacial and orientation-dependent energies. On held-out data, the energy and force errors are 29 meV/atom and 0.23 eV/mathringA. Validated against DFT on a suite of physical properties, the NEP reproduces the lattice constants within 0.3 %, the elastic constants within 17 %, the phonon dispersions with mean deviations below 2.5%, and the surface energies within 1.3 %. At the MoS₂/sapphire interface, it reproduces the adhesion energy minimum within 0.8 %, the interfacial energy within 2.2 % and the most stable orientation of the monolayer. It preserves the DFT-predicted energetic ordering of edges, point defects (Spearman ρ = 0.995) and mirror-twin boundaries (ρ = 1.0), and reproduces tilt grain-boundary energies within 8 % (ρ = 0.98) and stacking-fault energy barriers within 14 %. The resulting potential enables future studies of the atomic-scale mechanisms of defect formation in two-dimensional heteroepitaxy.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

已经针对立方碳化硅 (3C-SiC) 开发了几种机器学习原子间势 (MLIP)。在这里，我们针对一组常见的结构、热、缺陷、辐射损伤、无序相和计算性能测试对七个现有的 MLIP 进行了基准测试。我们还引入了两种互补的 MLIP，它们经过 DFT 标记的晶体、液体、无定形和缺陷配置的训练。 TGAP，基于动词 |soap_turbo| 的高斯近似势描述符，在采样的配置空间中提供一致的高精度。 MTP 是一种自定义矩张量势，在这些机制中保持良好的准确性，同时提供显着降低的计算成本、GPU 加速以及对数百万原子模拟的访问。作为应用级测试，两种电势在跨越短程排斥、瞬态无序和再结晶的位移级联中保持稳定。总体而言，MTP 对于 3C-SiC 的大规模和强非平衡模拟来说是一种特别强的精度和成本折衷方案。

English Abstract

Several machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have been developed for cubic silicon carbide (3C-SiC). Here, we benchmark seven existing MLIPs against a common set of structural, thermal, defect, radiation-damage, disordered-phase, and computational-performance tests. We also introduce two complementary MLIPs trained on DFT-labeled crystalline, liquid, amorphous, and defective configurations. TGAP, a Gaussian approximation potential based on the verb|soap_turbo| descriptor, provides consistently high accuracy across the sampled configurational space. MTP, a custom moment tensor potential, retains good accuracy across these regimes while offering substantially lower computational cost, GPU acceleration, and access to multi-million-atom simulations. As an application-level test, both potentials remain stable in displacement cascades spanning short-range repulsion, transient disorder, and recrystallization. Overall, MTP emerges as a particularly strong accuracy--cost compromise for large-scale and strongly non-equilibrium simulations of 3C-SiC.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

通过机器学习原子间势准确再现电子结构标签并不一定能确保可靠的凝聚相特性。我们研究了基组叠加误差 (BSSE) 和显式 Axilrod-Teller-Muto (ATM) 三体色散贡献对分子液体预测的影响。从 MACELES-OFF 开始，我们在 ωB97M-V/def2-TZVPD 水平上微调基线电位。一个单独的分支在基线冻结的情况下学习平衡 (CP) 能量和力校正，同时在分子动力学推理过程中添加 D4 参数化 ATM 项。对于来自 OMol25 的分析 SPICE2 配置，平均原子归一化 CP 和 ATM 贡献分别为 0.222 和 0.205 meV/原子。我们比较了 39 种有机液体的基线、仅 CP、仅 ATM 和 CP + ATM 变体。相对于基线，仅 CP、仅 ATM 和 CP + ATM 的平均系统密度变化分别为 -1.17%、-10.38% 和 -11.93%；相应的汽化焓变化为-2.79%、-14.74%和-18.32%。对于具有实验自扩散数据的11种液体，平均自扩散变化分别为+17.66%、+203.02%和+230.36%。因此，这两种修正都降低了蒸发的密度和焓，同时平均增加了分子迁移率。 ATM 在这些变化中占主导地位，而 CP 的贡献较小；它们的组合产生最大的平均偏移。这些结果表明，对分析的构型进行亚兆伏/原子平均值的校正可以显着重塑分子液体的预测结构、热力学和输运特性。

English Abstract

Accurate reproduction of electronic-structure labels by machine-learning interatomic potentials does not necessarily ensure reliable condensed-phase properties. We investigate the effects of basis-set superposition error (BSSE) and an explicit Axilrod-Teller-Muto (ATM) three-body dispersion contribution on molecular-liquid predictions. Starting from MACELES-OFF, we fine-tune a baseline potential at the ωB97M-V/def2-TZVPD level. A separate branch learns counterpoise (CP) energy and force corrections with the baseline frozen, while a D4-parametrized ATM term is added during molecular-dynamics inference. Mean atom-normalized CP and ATM contributions are 0.222 and 0.205 meV/atom, respectively, for the analyzed SPICE2 configurations from OMol25. We compare baseline, CP-only, ATM-only, and CP + ATM variants for 39 organic liquids. Relative to baseline, the mean system-wise density changes are -1.17%, -10.38%, and -11.93% for CP-only, ATM-only, and CP + ATM, respectively; the corresponding enthalpy-of-vaporization changes are -2.79%, -14.74%, and -18.32%. For the 11 liquids with experimental self-diffusion data, the mean self-diffusion changes are +17.66%, +203.02%, and +230.36%. Both corrections thus lower density and enthalpy of vaporization while increasing molecular mobility on average. ATM dominates these changes, while CP provides a smaller contribution; their combination yields the largest average shifts. These results show that corrections with sub-meV/atom mean values on the analyzed configurations can substantially reshape the predicted structural, thermodynamic, and transport properties of molecular liquids.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

目前导出超硬各向同性超材料的方法需要启发式或拓扑优化，但效率极低且受到人类输入的限制。为了解决这个问题，物理信息神经网络被应用于解析方程，以系统地求解闭孔晶格中的板方向。按照标准化程序，对板的方向进行建模并组装成类似于准随机泡沫的几何形状，以便通过有限元模拟进行验证。闭孔 6 板几何形状也使用 LAPIS 增材制造并进行了实验表征。结果表明，至少需要 6 个板才能构成超硬各向同性晶格，7 个板将返回已建立的板立方八位组结构，而 8 至 10 个板几何形状在性能上没有表现出显着差异。尽管由于次优板放置策略而引入了一些板弯曲，但 PINN 晶格的相对刚度紧密贴合于 Hashin Shtrikman 上限，在相对密度为 0.4 时达到 Hashin Shtrikman 上限的 90%，并且晶格保持接近各向同性的行为，在相对密度为 0.1 时实现了 0.95 的齐纳比。基准测试结果表明，PINN 晶格的比刚度高于文献中报道的大多数各向同性结构。

English Abstract

Current methods of deriving ultrastiff isotropic metamaterials require heuristics or topology optimization, which are highly inefficient and limited by human input. To circumvent this, Physics Informed Neural Networks are applied to analytical equations to systematically solve for plate orientations in closed cell lattices. Following a standardized procedure, the plate orientations were modelled and assembled into geometries resembling quasi random foams for validation via finite element simulations. Closed cell 6-plate geometries were also additively manufactured using LAPIS and experimentally characterized. The results indicate that a minimum of 6 plates is required to constitute an ultrastiff isotropic lattice, 7 plates would return the established plate Cubic Octet structure while 8 to 10 plate geometries did not show significant differences in properties. Despite the introduction of some plate bending due to sub-optimal plate placement strategy, relative stiffness of the PINNs lattices adheres closely to the Hashin Shtrikman upper bound, 90 percent of Hashin Shtrikman upper bound at a relative density of 0.4, and the lattices maintain near isotropic behaviour, achieving a Zener ratio of 0.95 at a relative density of 0.1. Benchmarking results show that specific stiffness of PINNs lattices is higher than the majority of isotropic architectures reported in the literature.

用于量子机器学习的电子结构通知多体编码#

原题：Electronic-Structure-Informed Many-Body Encoding for Quantum Machine Learning
作者：Liangnuo Zhang、Tanner Culpitt、Qiwei Yin、Linghua Zhu、Xiaosong Li。arXiv · physics，2026-11-02

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：数据编码是量子机器学习（QML）中的一个核心挑战，因为它决定了经典信息如何映射到量子态并影响参数化量子电路的表达能力和效率。在这里，我们引入了一种基于物理的量子编码策略，该策略利用电子系统固有的多体结构来建立输入描述符的体序与量子门的局域性之间的直接对应关系。具体来说，从单个平均场电子结构计算获得的 n 体电子描述符通过 n 量子位门进行编码，产生具有增加的多体表达能力的量子电路的系统层次结构。使用简化密度矩阵描述符，我们构建了包含一体…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

数据编码是量子机器学习（QML）中的一个核心挑战，因为它决定了经典信息如何映射到量子态并影响参数化量子电路的表达能力和效率。在这里，我们引入了一种基于物理的量子编码策略，该策略利用电子系统固有的多体结构来建立输入描述符的体序与量子门的局域性之间的直接对应关系。具体来说，从单个平均场电子结构计算获得的 n 体电子描述符通过 n 量子位门进行编码，产生具有增加的多体表达能力的量子电路的系统层次结构。使用简化密度矩阵描述符，我们构建了包含一体到四体编码的量子机器学习模型，并对其在预测沿键拉伸势能表面的电子相关能方面的性能进行了基准测试。我们证明，增加编码的主体顺序可以系统地提高针对完整配置交互限制的预测精度。值得注意的是，在关键情况下，由低阶电子信息构建的断开的多粒子密度所达到的精度可与显式相关的两个粒子密度相媲美，从而为更高体量子编码提供了一种计算成本低廉的途径。这些发现在经典数据的多体结构和量子特征图的设计之间建立了联系，为增强量子机器学习电路的表达能力和预测性能提供了系统的、物理可解释的框架。

English Abstract

Data encoding is a central challenge in quantum machine learning (QML), as it determines how classical information is mapped onto quantum states and influences the expressive power and efficiency of parameterized quantum circuits. Here, we introduce a physics-informed quantum encoding strategy that exploits the intrinsic many-body structure of electronic systems to establish a direct correspondence between the body order of input descriptors and the locality of quantum gates. Specifically, n-body electronic descriptors obtained from a single mean-field electronic-structure calculation are encoded through n-qubit gates, yielding a systematic hierarchy of quantum circuits with increasing many-body expressivity. Using reduced-density-matrix descriptors, we construct quantum machine-learning models incorporating one- through four-body encodings and benchmark their performance in predicting electron-correlation energies along bond-stretching potential energy surfaces. We demonstrate that increasing the body order of the encoding systematically improves prediction accuracy toward the full configuration interaction limit. Remarkably, disconnected many-particle densities constructed from lower-order electronic information achieve accuracy comparable to explicitly correlated two-particle densities in key cases, providing a computationally inexpensive route to higher-body quantum encodings. These findings establish a connection between the many-body structure of classical data and the design of quantum feature maps, offering a systematic, physically interpretable framework for enhancing the expressivity and predictive performance of quantum machine-learning circuits.

驱动非线性 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger 晶格中动态相的机器学习分类#

原题：Machine learning classification of dynamical phases in a driven nonlinear Su-Schrieffer-Heeger lattice
作者：Chukwunaedum Patrick Izediuno、Ghada H. Alharbi、Stephan Wong、Zeeshan Ahmad、Sunae So、Minkyung Kim 等 7 人。arXiv · physics，2026-11-06

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

驱动的非线性光子晶格可以进入稳定、对称破缺、周期性或混沌状态，并且映射每种状态在参数空间中出现的位置是器件设计的关键。暴力映射速度很慢，并且依赖于手工构建的标准，而机器学习分类器通常在不同的系统上进行测试，因此尚不清楚哪个适合新的网格。因此，我们将四个分类器应用于同一个相干驱动的克尔谐振器 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) 链：基于 Lyapunov 指数和谱诊断的手工构建的启发式分类器、在启发式标签上训练的有监督卷积神经网络 (CNN)、基于增强动态模式分解 (aDMD) 的无监督库构建方案，以及基于四个动态模型的无监督高斯混合模型 (GMM)。包括 aDMD 重建误差的不变量。使用收敛启发式图作为参考，CNN 达到 98.0% 的保留精度，而两种无标签方法的调整兰德指数 (ARI) 分别达到 0.916 和 0.947，而没有看到任何标签。我们表明，aDMD 库干净地解析了稳定相和双稳态相，并将对称周期性振荡与对称破缺周期性振荡分开，这是启发式方法所遗漏的，尽管它无法区分周期性切换与混沌切换。我们还发现，收敛性差的参考低估了无标签方法的准确性。

English Abstract

Driven nonlinear photonic lattices can settle into steady, symmetry-broken, periodic, or chaotic states, and mapping where each occurs in parameter space is key to device design. Brute-force mapping is slow and relies on hand-built criteria, while machine-learning classifiers are usually tested on different systems, so it is unclear which suits a new lattice. We therefore apply four classifiers to the same coherently driven Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chain of Kerr resonators: a hand-built heuristic classifier based on Lyapunov exponents and spectral diagnostics, a supervised convolutional neural network (CNN) trained on the heuristic labels, an unsupervised library-building scheme based on augmented dynamic mode decomposition (aDMD), and an unsupervised Gaussian mixture model (GMM) on four dynamical invariants that include an aDMD reconstruction error. Using a converged heuristic diagram as the reference, the CNN reaches 98.0% held-out accuracy, while the two label-free methods reach adjusted Rand indices (ARIs) of 0.916 and 0.947, respectively, without seeing any labels. We show that the aDMD library cleanly resolves the stable and bistable phases and separates symmetric from symmetry-broken periodic oscillations, which the heuristic misses, though it cannot distinguish periodic from chaotic switching. We also find that a poorly converged reference understates the label-free methods' accuracy.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

晶体结构预测（CSP）的生成模型扩大了新型材料的搜索空间并提高了筛选效率。然而，由于控制晶体的复杂结构和物理约束，同时生成稳定和新颖的结构仍然具有挑战性。为了应对这一挑战，我们提出了位点轨道掩模双分支变压器模型（ST2M），这是一种对称引导的离散连续扩散框架，结合了自由度掩模并以威科夫模式为条件。 ST2M 将晶体结构表示为独立占据的威科夫轨道序列，并采用双分支 Transformer 来联合对离散和连续变量进行去噪。在我们的实验中，ST2M 的结构有效性达到 96.37%。对于数据集中的已知模式，23.86% 的生成结构同时满足稳定性、唯一性和新颖性标准，比最佳性能基线相对提高了 68.15%。第一性原理计算在选定的具有低预测能量的候选结构中确定了总共 21 个先前未报道的稳定结构。这些结果表明，ST2M 可以在规定的晶体学约束下生成化学上可行的候选晶体，并推广到未知的威科夫模式，为加速材料发现提供了一条实用途径。

English Abstract

Generative models for crystal structure prediction (CSP) have expanded the search space for novel materials and improved screening efficiency. However, simultaneously generating stable and novel structures remains challenging because of the complex structural and physical constraints governing crystals. To address this challenge, we propose site-orbit masked twin-branch Transformer model (ST2M), a symmetry-guided discrete--continuous diffusion framework incorporating degree-of-freedom masks and conditioned on Wyckoff patterns. ST2M represents crystal structures as sequences of independent occupied Wyckoff orbits and employs a twin-branch Transformer to jointly denoise discrete and continuous variables. In our experiments, ST2M achieves a structural validity of 96.37%. For known patterns in dataset, 23.86% of generated structures simultaneously satisfy stability, uniqueness, and novelty criteria, representing a relative improvement of 68.15% over the best-performing baseline. First-principles calculations identify a total of 21 previously unreported stable structures among selected candidates with low predicted energies. These results demonstrate that ST2M can generate chemically viable crystal candidates under prescribed crystallographic constraints and generalize to unknown Wyckoff patterns, providing a practical route to accelerated materials discovery.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在多尺度系统中，空间分布的成分之间的非局域相互作用可以解锁集体的、潜在的突发行为，这些行为无法简化为个体成分的反应。除了表征之外，多尺度系统中的非局域性越来越多地被视为断裂力学、波物理学和无序系统的设计元素。现有的非定域性理论提供了严格的描述和机械洞察力，但通常需要规定的分析形式、范围、对称性或周期性。机器学习提供了一种数据驱动的途径来学习交互，但将网络设计与物理理论联系起来的原则性方法仍然处于萌芽状态。在这里，我们提出了域分解注意力神经算子（DANO），它仅从现场数据中学习由线性场方程控制的异构系统中的配置相关交互。通过大规模光学超表面的案例研究，我们展示了 DANO 在几何异质性和跨驱动条件下的全场预测、从小到大的域转移和多功能逆设计，并通过基于物理的询问验证了学习到的相互作用。作为一种人工智能科学工具，它支持非局部多尺度系统的交互发现和可扩展、可定制的设计，包括虚拟现实光学。

English Abstract

In multiscale systems, nonlocal interactions among spatially distributed constituents can unlock collective, potentially emergent behavior irreducible to individual constituent responses. Beyond characterization, nonlocality in multiscale systems is increasingly viewed as a design element across fracture mechanics, wave physics, and disordered systems. Existing nonlocality theories provide rigorous descriptions and mechanistic insight, but often require prescribed analytic forms, range, symmetries, or periodicity. Machine learning offers a data-driven route to learn interactions, yet principled approaches that tie the network design to physical theory remain nascent. Here we present domain-decomposition attention neural operator (DANO), which learns configuration-dependent interactions in heterogeneous systems governed by linear field equations from field data alone. Through a case study in large-scale optical metasurfaces, we demonstrate DANO's full-field prediction under geometric heterogeneity and across drive conditions, small-to-large domain transfer, and multifunctional inverse design, with learned interactions validated through physics-based interrogation. As an AI-for-Science tool, it supports interaction discovery and scalable, customizable design of nonlocal multiscale systems, including virtual-reality optics.

通过预训练实现机器学习原子间势的分布外不确定性量化#

原题：Towards Out-of-Distribution Uncertainty Quantification for Machine-Learned Interatomic Potentials via Pre-Training

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

信息不确定性量化（UQ）对于机器学习原子间势（MLIP）的实际部署至关重要，因为它可以深入了解其在化学空间中的可靠性。尽管最近提出了许多针对 MLIP 的事后不确定性估计方法，但大多数方法都是在低数据制度下进行训练的，这限制了它们对狭窄感兴趣领域的适用性。受基础 MLIP 出现的启发，我们提出在大型、多样化的数据集上预训练不确定性估计器，以实现更广泛的适用性。我们在大型材料科学数据集上预训练了几个 UQ 模型，以量化不同 MLIP 的不确定性，并评估它们在分布内和域外 (OOD) 吸附能预测方面的性能。我们发现，预先训练的不确定性估计器可以跨多个数据集提供可行的零样本不确定性估计，而即使在最具挑战性的数据集上，微调也能一致地产生预测不确定性和预测误差之间的正相关性。这些结果为 MLIP 的基础 UQ 模型奠定了基础，通过为用户提供有关模型可靠性和适用性的先验信息，最终促进其在实际环境中的部署。

English Abstract

Informative uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the practical deployment of machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs), as it provides insight into their reliability across chemical space. Although numerous post-hoc uncertainty estimation methods for MLIPs have recently been proposed, most have been trained in low-data regimes, restricting their applicability to narrow domains of interest. Inspired by the emergence of foundational MLIPs, we propose pre-training uncertainty estimators on large, diverse datasets to enable broader applicability. We pre-train several UQ models on large materials science datasets to quantify the uncertainty of different MLIPs, and evaluate their performance on both in-distribution and out-of-domain (OOD) adsorption-energy predictions. We find that pre-trained uncertainty estimators provide viable zero-shot uncertainty estimates across multiple datasets, while fine-tuning consistently yields positive correlations between predicted uncertainty and prediction error, even on the most challenging datasets. These results lay the groundwork for foundational UQ models for MLIPs, ultimately facilitating their deployment in practical settings by providing users with a priori information about model reliability and applicability.

在大数据和人工智能时代：我们还需要化学键吗？#

原题：In the age of big data and AI: do we still need chemical bonds?
作者：Mohammed Al-Fahdi、Aakash Naik、Katharina Ueltzen、Luc Walterbos、Tom Demeyere、Philipp Benner 等 7 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-03

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

化学键合是化学使材料结构、稳定性、性能和反应性合理化的语言。与此同时，现代深度学习模型使用主要基于组成和结构信息的表示来实现显着的预测性能，这就提出了显式键合概念是否仍有必要的问题。在这项工作中，我们认为人工智能并没有使化学键合过时，而是为评估和完善键合概念提供了一个新的框架。我们表明，通过线性探测， Allegro和MACE嵌入恢复与（ R ² > 0.95 ）的键合描述符，表明键合自然地从Allegro和MACE的潜在空间中出现。我们还表明，在我们的电子结构特征化MEGNet （ ESF-MEGNet ）模型中，键合描述符提供了超出键合距离的信息，将包括体积和剪切模量在内的属性的预测误差降低了15%。我们讨论了可解释机器学习、可合成性预测和生成材料设计的含义，并提出了学习更一般键合表示的联合嵌入方法。更广泛地说，我们提出化学键可以理解为电子结构的可学习表示。从这个角度来看，不同的键合模型代表了底层电子信息的替代压缩，可以通过其预测能力、维度和复杂性进行评估。机器学习的时代将重点从键合模型是否具有物理动机转移到它是否捕获解释和预测材料行为所需的信息。如果材料基因组存在，它应该可以通过化学键的表示进行编码。如果没有键合表示可以捕获这些信息，我们对化学键的理解可能需要修改。

English Abstract

Chemical bonding is the language through which chemistry rationalizes materials structure, stability, properties, and reactivity. Meanwhile, modern deep-learning models achieve remarkable predictive performance using representations based largely on compositional and structural information, raising the question of whether explicit bonding concepts remain necessary. In this work, we argue that artificial intelligence does not render chemical bonding obsolete but provides a new framework for evaluating and refining bonding concepts. We show that, through linear probing, Allegro and MACE embeddings recover bonding descriptors with (R² > 0.95), indicating that bonding emerges naturally from Allegro's and MACE's latent spaces. We also show that bonding descriptors provide information beyond bond distance in our electronic-structure-featurized MEGNet (ESF-MEGNet) model, reducing prediction error for properties including bulk and shear moduli by 15%. We discuss implications for interpretable machine learning, synthesizability prediction, and generative materials design, and suggest joint-embedding approaches for learning more general bonding representations. More broadly, we propose that chemical bonding can be understood as a learnable representation of electronic structure. From this perspective, different bonding models represent alternative compressions of the underlying electronic information and can be evaluated through their predictive power, dimensionality, and complexity. The age of machine learning shifts the focus from whether a bonding model is physically motivated to whether it captures the information required to explain and predict materials behavior. If a materials genome exists, it should be encodable through a representation of chemical bonding. If no bonding representation can capture this information, our understanding of the chemical bond may require revision.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

分子动力学根据原子力和轨迹积分预测分子运动。密度泛函理论 (DFT) 提供了第一原理力，但其 O(N3) 成本限制了对数千个原子的模拟；经验力场规模可达数百万，但错过了键断裂和电荷转移。等变消息传递 Transformer 以线性成本学习从头开始的潜力。堆叠层将信息传播到单个截止范围之外，使四层的感受野高达 23Å。在这个前沿，规模受到通信的限制：层在设备之间交换边界特征，力反向传播重复交换，移动原子改变相邻图和张量形状。我们推出了 Tessera，一种分布式运行时，可以修复重建之间的工作负载形状。它将沿着空间填充曲线的原子分类为相等数量的碎片，确保每个等级的原子数量和张量形状相同。对等方将比例设置为 K⁰.⁵，而不是 K。保守的邻居皮肤在更新过程中保留拓扑，使每个重建间隔能够作为一个 CUDA 图重播。融合内核、三级邻居搜索和重叠的 All-to-All 完成了它。在 512 个 GPU 上，Tessera 以 89.7% 的弱扩展效率维持 3070 万个原子（每个 GPU 的三个负载为 77--90%）；随着机器和问题增长 16 倍，每步时间增加 1.11--1.29 倍。图重用将全步时间缩短了 1.87 倍。设备增加 8 倍时，邻居搜索成本会增加 1.7 倍，而 Morton 阶则增加 3.0 倍。在实时 10.24M 原子盐溶液中，NVE 能量漂移为 0.42，μeV/atom/ps 超过 10 ps，并且结构与串行参考匹配。 1006 万原子诺如病毒衣壳在 256 个 A100 GPU 上以约 8 ps/天的速度维持 100 ps 的 NVT。超过 200,000 个步骤，它没有断裂的键或异常接近的对，并使用三种编译的变体，没有图形中断或急切的回退。

English Abstract

Molecular dynamics predicts molecular motion from atomic forces and trajectory integration. Density functional theory (DFT) provides first-principles forces, but its O(N³) cost limits simulations to thousands of atoms; empirical force fields scale to millions but miss bond breaking and charge transfer. Equivariant message-passing Transformers learn the ab initio potential at linear cost. Stacked layers propagate information beyond a single cutoff, giving four layers a receptive field up to 23Å. At this frontier, scale is limited by communication: layers exchange boundary features across devices, force backpropagation repeats the exchange, and moving atoms change neighbor graphs and tensor shapes. We present Tessera, a distributed runtime that fixes workload shapes between rebuilds. It sorts atoms along a space-filling curve into equal-count shards, ensuring identical atom counts and tensor shapes per rank. Peer sets scale as K⁰.⁵, not K. A conservative neighbor skin preserves topology across updates, enabling each rebuild interval to replay as one CUDA Graph. Fused kernels, three-level neighbor search, and overlapped All-to-All complete it. On 512 GPUs, Tessera sustains 30.7M atoms at 89.7% weak-scaling efficiency (77--90% across three per-GPU loads); per-step time rises 1.11--1.29x as machine and problem grow 16x. Graph reuse cuts full-step time 1.87×. Across an 8× device increase, neighbor-search cost grows 1.7×, versus 3.0× for Morton order. In a live 10.24M-atom salt solution, NVE energy drift is 0.42,μeV/atom/ps over 10 ps, and structure matches a serial reference. A 10.06M-atom Norovirus capsid sustains 100 ps of NVT at approx 8 ps/day on 256 A100 GPUs. Over 200,000 steps, it has no broken bonds or anomalously close pairs and uses three compiled variants, with no graph breaks or eager fallbacks.

正则化密度泛函的神经网络逼近#

原题：Neural network approximation of regularized density functionals
作者：Andre Laestadius、Mathias Oster。arXiv · cond-mat.supr-con，2026-11-03

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们研究巴拿赫空间设置中密度泛函的神经网络近似。由于精确的连续泛函在自然密度拓扑中是不连续的，因此我们应用 p-Moreau-Yosida 正则化。我们构建了保留凸性和非负性的有限秩 ReLU 网络，位于正则化函数之下，并在紧集上均匀地近似它。对于通过最小化紧凑密度集获得的能量，我们精确地考虑了正则化引起的能量转移，并通过这些紧凑集上的定位和网络逼近误差来限制剩余误差。我们将该框架分别应用于固定粒子数和固定平均粒子数的正则和大正则密度泛函理论。我们在范数紧类的外部势（包括分子库仑势）上获得了一致的能量收敛。我们通过简单模型系统的数值实验来说明我们的结果。

English Abstract

We study neural-network approximation of density functionals in a Banach-space setting. Since the exact continuum functionals are discontinuous in the natural density topology, we apply p-Moreau-Yosida regularization. We construct finite-rank ReLU networks that preserve convexity and nonnegativity, lie below the regularized functional, and approximate it uniformly on compact sets. For energies obtained by minimization over compact density sets, we account exactly for the energy shift caused by regularization and bound the remaining error by the localization on these compact sets and network approximation errors. We apply this framework to canonical and grand-canonical density-functional theory at fixed particle number and fixed mean particle number, respectively. We obtain uniform energy convergence on norm-compact classes of external potentials, including molecular Coulomb potentials. We illustrate our results through numerical experiments on a simple model system.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

gtrsim50 keV 范围内的明亮相干 X 射线非常适合材料科学、高能量密度物理学和核物理学的应用，但很难从现有的 X 射线自由电子激光 (XFEL) 设施中获得。基于等离子体的加力燃烧器提供了一种增加电子束能量的紧凑途径，但高变压器比 (TR) 操作容易受到软管不稳定的影响，这会严重降低见证光束质量。我们建议使用横向椭圆驱动光束来激发具有轴向变化聚焦梯度的椭圆非线性等离子体尾流，从而通过电子感应加速器退相干来抑制见证光束。三维粒子在单元模拟表明，对于3 kA驱动器和均方根长度为4 μm、光束间隔为94 μm（TR约3）的2.35 kA见证光束，该方案可以将8 GeV见证光束加速到sim30 GeV，同时近一半的电子满足XFEL要求。从头到尾的模拟表明，即使包含量子扩散，所得光束也可以驱动峰值功率为 sim10 GW、持续时间为 sim10 fs 的 gtrsim50 keV XFEL 辐射，从而提供了一条紧凑的路线，将现有的 XFEL 设施扩展到更高的光子能量。

English Abstract

Bright coherent X-rays in the gtrsim50 keV range are highly desirable for applications in materials science, high-energy-density physics, and nuclear physics, but are difficult to obtain from present X-ray free-electron laser (XFEL) facilities. Plasma-based afterburners offer a compact route to increasing the electron-beam energy, yet high transformer ratio (TR) operation is vulnerable to hosing instability, which can severely degrade the witness beam quality. We propose using a transversely elliptical driver beam to excite an elliptical nonlinear plasma wake with an axially varying focusing gradient, thereby suppressing the witness beam hosing through betatron decoherence. Three-dimensional particle-in-cell simulations show that, for a 3 kA driver and a 2.35 kA witness beam with an rms length of 4 μm and a beam separation of 94 μm (TR approx 3), this scheme can accelerate an 8 GeV witness beam to sim30 GeV while nearly half of the electrons meet the XFEL requirements. Start-to-end simulations show that the resulting beam can drive gtrsim50 keV XFEL radiation with peak powers of sim10 GW and durations of sim10 fs, even when quantum diffusion is included, offering a compact route to extend existing XFEL facilities to higher photon energies.

用于具有基础模型的分子设计的本地编排工具#

原题：A local orchestration harness for molecular design with foundation models
作者：Sakib Mostafa、Md Atik Ahamed、James Zou、Lei Xing、Md Tauhidul Islam。arXiv · physics，2026-11-04

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：分子设计需要协调生成、性质预测和迭代编辑，每个决定都与正确的化学结构和支持证据相关。在实践中整合这些功能提出了三个主要挑战：保留分子数据的本地控制、跨任务连接专门模型以及验证所提出的更改是否满足化学约束。在这里，我们介绍了 MolCrew，这是一个可本地部署的系统，它通过语言模型协调器和 RDKit 计算来协调四名单独训练的分子专家。专家根据描述生成结构，描述分子结构，预测生物终点并针对特定目标提出编辑建议。单独的模型权重允许在工作流程…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

分子设计需要协调生成、性质预测和迭代编辑，每个决定都与正确的化学结构和支持证据相关。在实践中整合这些功能提出了三个主要挑战：保留分子数据的本地控制、跨任务连接专门模型以及验证所提出的更改是否满足化学约束。在这里，我们介绍了 MolCrew，这是一个可本地部署的系统，它通过语言模型协调器和 RDKit 计算来协调四名单独训练的分子专家。专家根据描述生成结构，描述分子结构，预测生物终点并针对特定目标提出编辑建议。单独的模型权重允许在工作流程中独立评估和更新每个功能。 RDKit 计算检查结构有效性、量化描述符更改并评估指定的结构约束，同时返回的观察结果指导进一步的工具调用。我们评估了编排器对工具使用决策和故障恢复的低阶适应，同时保持专业权重不变。在 3,300 个 ChEBI-20 测试示例中，MolCrew 实现了 96.4% 的精确结构恢复率，而已发布的 Atomas-large 结果为 38.7%。在使用支架分割评估的八个 MoleculeNet 数据集中，受试者工作特征曲线下的平均面积为 0.851，而已发布的 Atomas 结果为 0.770。在使用未适应的协调器进行的记录评估中，1,699 个请求中有 1,554 个请求 (91.5%) 通过了每个预定义的工作流程检查。通过保留分子提议、模型输出和化学计算之间的可检查联系，MolCrew 提供了一个模块化框架，用于在本地数据控制下进行计算候选评估和细化。

English Abstract

Molecular design requires coordinated generation, property prediction and iterative editing, with each decision linked to the correct chemical structure and supporting evidence. Integrating these capabilities in practice presents three major challenges: retaining local control of molecular data, connecting specialized models across tasks and verifying that proposed changes satisfy chemical constraints. Here we present MolCrew, a locally deployable system that coordinates four separately trained molecular specialists with a language-model orchestrator and RDKit calculations. The specialists generate structures from descriptions, describe molecular structures, predict biological endpoints and propose edits towards specified goals. Separate model weights allow each capability to be evaluated and updated independently within the workflow. RDKit calculations check structural validity, quantify descriptor changes and assess specified structural constraints, while returned observations guide further tool calls. We evaluated low-rank adaptation of the orchestrator for tool-use decisions and failure recovery while leaving specialist weights unchanged. On 3,300 ChEBI-20 test examples, MolCrew achieved 96.4% exact structure recovery, compared with 38.7% for published Atomas-large results. Across eight MoleculeNet datasets evaluated using scaffold splits, mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve was 0.851, compared with 0.770 for published Atomas results. In a recorded evaluation using the unadapted orchestrator, 1,554 of 1,699 requests (91.5%) passed every predefined workflow check. By preserving inspectable links between molecular proposals, model outputs and chemical calculations, MolCrew provides a modular framework for computational candidate assessment and refinement under local data control.

薄膜铌酸锂中 Sb2S3 色散修整的物理替代物#

原题：Physics-Informed Surrogate for Sb2S3 Dispersion Trimming in Thin-Film Lithium Niobate
作者：Shoumik Debnath、Anup Kumar Modak、Sudipta Saha。arXiv · physics，2026-11-03

中文摘要

8 nm 的膜厚误差（在商用薄膜铌酸锂晶片的公差范围内）会使 1550 nm 处的群速度色散 β2 移动约 28 ps² km-1。我们通过数值研究分段非易失性相变包层是否可以在制造后纠正此错误。在 3012 几何形状的模式求解中，Sb2S₃ 在 40 nm 条带厚度下以中值 181 ps2 km-1 切换 β2，在采用的光学常数下没有材料吸收，而 Sb2Se3 在中值模态损耗为 5.6 dB cm-1 时达到了相当的对比度。由于相位沿着波导添加，四个独立切换的二进制段到达 (β2,β3) 平面的二维区域；本征模展开在连接处支持这种描述。 65 个芯片几何形状的全波求解表明，段状态随芯片误差而移动，因此从每个芯片模型中选择位模式：受无损麦克斯韦问题的精确恒等式约束的谱替代。它在训练域内的准确性与无约束基线的准确性相似；它使 β2 和 β₃ 保持一致，并且在未见薄膜厚度下的误差为 8.3，而不是 13.0 ps²·km⁻1。使用裸露的测试环和掩模上已带有晶体条的第二个环，96.8% 的芯片被修剪成 ±25 ps² km-1，在全波解决方案上进行评分。热模型给出了 532 nm 光束的候选写入窗口。

English Abstract

A film-thickness error of 8 nm, within the tolerance of commercial thin-film lithium niobate wafers, shifts the group-velocity dispersion β₂ at 1550 nm by about 28 ps² km⁻¹. We study numerically whether a segmented, non-volatile phase-change cladding can correct this error after fabrication. In mode solves of 3012 geometries, Sb₂S₃ switches β₂ by a median of 181 ps² km⁻¹ at a 40 nm strip thickness with no material absorption under the adopted optical constants, whereas Sb₂Se₃ reaches a comparable contrast at a median modal loss of 5.6 dB cm⁻¹. Because phase adds along the guide, four independently switched binary segments reach a two-dimensional region of the (β₂,β₃) plane; eigenmode expansion supports this description at the junctions. Full-wave solves at 65 die geometries show that the segment states move with the die error, so the bit pattern is chosen from a per-die model: a spectral surrogate constrained by exact identities of the lossless Maxwell problem. Its accuracy within the training domain is similar to that of unconstrained baselines; it keeps β₂ and β₃ consistent, and its error at an unseen film thickness is 8.3 rather than 13.0 ps² km⁻¹. With a bare test ring and a second ring carrying a crystalline strip already on the mask, 96.8% of the dies are trimmed into ±25 ps² km⁻¹, scored on full-wave solutions. A thermal model gives a candidate write window for a 532 nm beam.

PolyGPT：用于聚合物电解质设计的仅解码器变压器#

原题：PolyGPT: A Decoder-Only Transformer for Polymer Electrolyte Design

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

设计聚合物电解质需要控制材料特性和化学结构。在这里，我们展示了 PolyGPT，这是一个具有 3830 万个参数的 Transformer，它可以根据可选的属性目标、包含或排除特定化学特征的请求以及电解质域条件生成聚合物重复单元。使用通过验证损失选择的模型，我们将无条件生成与电解质相关结构、低玻璃化转变温度 (Tg) 或两者的要求进行了比较。无条件发电和仅电解质发电的结构有效性分别为 99.46% 和 99.59%。然而，电解质调节增加了重复性：有效输出的唯一性从 99.99% 下降到 51.74%。在 Tg 目标为 246.11 K 时，我们比较了有和没有电解质调节的发电情况，添加电解质条件将预测器估计的平均绝对误差从 26.97 K 略微降低到 26.07 K，但在目标 50 K 范围内产生不同有效聚合物的所有尝试的比例从 82.35% 下降到 32.31%。当我们还要求缺席培训目录时，这些收益率分别为 59.90% 和 19.71%。由于 25 K 耐受性更严格，产量仍然较低。域调节还削弱了对包含请求的控制：尝试生产含有所需化学特征的有效聚合物的平均比例从 94.27% 下降到 31.27%。在九个检查点中，早期模型有时会产生更独特的、目录中未见的聚合物，以满足 Tg 目标：10,000 个检查点模型进行电解质调节，30,000 个检查点模型不进行电解质调节。总之，这些结果表明了为什么单独的性能误差不足以评估条件聚合物的生成。必须将其与重复性、培训目录新颖性以及是否满足组合设计要求一起考虑。

English Abstract

Designing polymer electrolytes requires control over both material properties and chemical structure. Here, we present PolyGPT, a Transformer with 38.3 million parameters that generates polymer repeat units from optional property targets, requests to include or exclude a specified chemical feature, and an electrolyte-domain condition. Using the model selected by validation loss, we compared unconditional generation with requests for electrolyte-associated structures, a low glass-transition temperature (Tg), or both. Unconditional and electrolyte-only generation achieved structural validity of 99.46% and 99.59%, respectively. However, electrolyte conditioning increased repetition: uniqueness among valid outputs fell from 99.99% to 51.74%. At a Tg target of 246.11 K, we compared generation with and without electrolyte conditioning, and adding the electrolyte condition slightly reduced the predictor-estimated mean absolute error from 26.97 to 26.07 K, but the fraction of all attempts yielding distinct valid polymers within 50 K of the target fell from 82.35% to 32.31%. When we also required absence from the training catalog, these yields were 59.90% and 19.71%, respectively. The lower yield persisted with a tighter 25 K tolerance. Domain conditioning also weakened control of inclusion requests: the mean fraction of attempts producing valid polymers containing the requested chemical feature fell from 94.27% to 31.27%. Across nine checkpoints, earlier models sometimes produced more distinct, catalog-unseen polymers meeting the Tg target: 10,000 checkpoint model with electrolyte conditioning and 30,000 checkpoint model without it. Together, these results show why property error alone is insufficient for evaluating conditional polymer generation. It must be considered alongside repetition, training-catalog novelty, and whether combined design requests are satisfied.

使用 crystalALL 方法超越凸包的数据驱动候选结构集成生成#

原题：Data-driven candidate structure ensemble generation beyond the convex hull with crystALL approach
作者：Adam M. Krajewski、Ricardo Amaral、Jonathan W. Siegel、Luke A. Myers、Rushi Gong、Shun-Li Shang 等 7 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-03

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：真实材料在现实条件下由多种稳定或亚稳定状态组成，作为配置的统计遍历系综而不是理想晶体存在。然而，数据驱动的计算材料发现越来越倾向于仅通过基态 DFT 计算来寻找“新的稳定结构”。这限制了我们长期发现具有亚稳态特性的材料、解释实验观察结果或全面模拟热力学行为的能力。预测已知和假设固体的原子构型的遍历集是理论晶体学中的一个基本挑战，可以通过许多补充方法来实现。我们从概念上讨论了这个问题，并介绍了 crystalALL，这是一种实用且强大的数…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

真实材料在现实条件下由多种稳定或亚稳定状态组成，作为配置的统计遍历系综而不是理想晶体存在。然而，数据驱动的计算材料发现越来越倾向于仅通过基态 DFT 计算来寻找“新的稳定结构”。这限制了我们长期发现具有亚稳态特性的材料、解释实验观察结果或全面模拟热力学行为的能力。预测已知和假设固体的原子构型的遍历集是理论晶体学中的一个基本挑战，可以通过许多补充方法来实现。我们从概念上讨论了这个问题，并介绍了 crystalALL，这是一种实用且强大的数据驱动方法，用于发现凸包之外的集合，将结构多样性置于稳定性之上，从而在逃避传统方法的情况下实现稳健的实验匹配。 crystalALL 利用 OPTIMADE 和 MPDD 中的 500 万个松弛结构，通过以下方式快速生成全面的候选集：(1) 物种替换； (2) 使用 pySIPFENN 进行机器学习特征空间分析； (3)通过聚类和能量最小化或属性匹配来选择代表性结构； (4) 构建最终候选集合以进行实验或从头开始验证。我们还通过跨匿名成分范围而不是固定公式的新有效搜索，独特地解决了跨越许多整数化学计量的实验成分不确定性的常见挑战。总体而言，CrystALL 以集成为中心的理念比流行的凸包搜索范式提供了关键优势，后者通常无法解释关键的实验观察结果，并随着时间的推移增加 AI/ML 发现偏差 - 类似于在迭代生成的内容上重新训练的法学硕士中的模型崩溃。

English Abstract

Real materials consist of multiple stable or metastable states under real-world conditions, existing as statistical ergodic ensembles of configurations rather than ideal crystals. Yet, data-driven computational materials discovery increasingly biases itself toward finding "new stable structures" from ground-state DFT calculations alone. This limits our long-term ability to discover materials whose properties emerge from metastable phases, to explain experimental observations, or to model thermodynamic behavior comprehensively. Predicting ergodic sets of atomic configurations of known and hypothetical solids is a fundamental challenge in theoretical crystallography, approached via many complementary methods. We discuss this conceptually and introduce crystALL, a pragmatic and powerful data-driven approach to discovering ensembles beyond the convex hull, embracing structural diversity over stability alone, enabling robust experiment matching in cases evading traditional methods. crystALL leverages 5 million relaxed structures from OPTIMADE and MPDD to rapidly generate a comprehensive candidate set by: (1) species substitutions; (2) ML feature-space analysis with pySIPFENN; (3) representative structure selection via clustering and energy-minimization or property matching; and (4) construction of the final candidate ensemble for experimental or ab initio validation. We also uniquely address the common challenge of experimental composition uncertainties spanning many integer stoichiometries by new efficient searches across anonymized compositional ranges rather than fixed formulas. Overall, crystALL's ensemble-focused philosophy provides critical advantages over the prevailing convex-hull-searching paradigm, which often fails to account for key experimental observations and increases AI/ML discovery bias over time - akin to model collapse in LLMs retrained on iteratively generated content.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

随着 Gemini 和 DeepSeek 等通用大型语言模型 (LLM) 表现出越来越先进的推理能力，出现了一个关键问题：与特定领域的预训练材料模型相比，它们的属性预测能力如何？在这项研究中，我们评估了对 Ti、钢、高熵合金 (HEA) 和 Al 的预测，证明通用 LLM 的性能出人意料地优于 SteelBERT、MatSciBERT 和 SciBERT。为了进一步提高性能，我们引入了一种混合方法，利用先前模型（例如 CatBoost 或 SteelBERT）的数值先验来指导通用 LLM（Deepseek V3.1、Gemini-2.5-Flash）。通过输入先前模型的这些初始估计作为提示，法学硕士充当推理校正器，实现优于先前模型和“无指导模式”的准确性。此外，我们使用基于相似性的度量来研究数据策略的影响，揭示数据质量主导数量；仅使用训练集的 5% 高相似度参考数据作为输入，即可获得与使用 100% 训练集的结果相似的性能。此外，法学硕士展示了出色的不确定性校准，其中自我报告的“高”置信度与与真实情况的最小偏差密切相关。我们的研究结果表明，将通用法学硕士与先前的模型数值基线和高质量数据管理相结合，可以为材料发现创建一个具有成本效益且准确的范式，从而减少对昂贵的特定领域预训练的需求。

English Abstract

As general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs), such as Gemini and DeepSeek, exhibit increasingly advanced reasoning capabilities, a critical question arises: how do their property prediction abilities compare to those of domain-specific pretrained materials models? In this study, we evaluate predictions for Ti, steels, high-entropy alloy (HEA) and Al, demonstrating that general-purpose LLMs surprisingly outperform SteelBERT, MatSciBERT, and SciBERT. To further enhance performance, we introduce a hybrid approach that utilizes numerical priors from a prior model, such as CatBoost or SteelBERT, to guide the general LLMs (Deepseek V3.1, Gemini-2.5-Flash). By inputting these initial estimates from a prior model as prompts, the LLM acts as a reasoning corrector, achieving accuracy superior to the prior model and "unguided mode". Furthermore, we investigate the impact of data strategy using a similarity-based metric, revealing that data quality dominates quantity; using merely 5% of high-similarity reference data of training set as input yields performance similar to the results of using 100% of training set. Additionally, the LLMs demonstrate excellent uncertainty calibration, where self-reported "high" confidence strongly correlates with minimal deviation from ground truth. Our findings suggest that synergizing general-purpose LLMs with prior model numerical baselines and high-quality data curation creates a cost-effective and accurate paradigm for materials discovery, reducing the need for expensive domain-specific pretraining.

随机 Al-Cu 相场凝固的替代模型#

原题：Surrogate Models for Stochastic Al-Cu Phase-Field Solidification
作者：Simon Savukoski、Tatu Pinomaa。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-04

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

相场模型解决了合金快速凝固过程中的微观结构演化问题，但其计算成本限制了长轨迹、随机系综和跨工艺条件的研究。我们提出了一种用于二维 Al-Cu 凝固状态条件预测的保方差降噪扩散桥模型 (VP-DDBM)。给定当前相位、浓度和规定的温度场，该模型可以预测后续状态并以自回归方式应用。在两个保持的热梯度中，VP-DDBM 在域平均相位、域平均 Cu 浓度和前导尖端位置方面产生的长视野误差比确定性 U-Net 和 SI-ConvNeXt 基线更低。它还在长时间的部署过程中保留了精细的树突结构，而确定性的 U-Net 则逐渐平滑了形态。 VP-DDBM 预测相和浓度演化的速度比相场求解器快约 73 倍，显示出更快的工艺条件筛选和基于集合的微观结构研究的潜力。

English Abstract

Phase-field models resolve microstructural evolution during rapid alloy solidification, but their computational cost limits long trajectories, stochastic ensembles, and studies across process conditions. We present a variance-preserving denoising diffusion bridge model (VP-DDBM) for state-conditioned prediction of two-dimensional Al-Cu solidification. Given the current phase, concentration, and prescribed temperature fields, the model predicts the subsequent state and is applied autoregressively. Across two held-out thermal gradients, VP-DDBM produces lower long-horizon errors than deterministic U-Net and SI-ConvNeXt baselines in domain-mean phase, domain-mean Cu concentration, and leading-tip position. It also retains fine dendritic structure over long rollouts, whereas the deterministic U-Net progressively smooths the morphology. VP-DDBM predicts the phase and concentration evolution approximately 73× faster than the phase-field solver, showing potential for faster process-condition screening and ensemble-based microstructure studies.

跨 NVIDIA 和 Intel GPU 的 LAMMPS 中具有基础原子间势的等温-等压分子动力学#

原题：Isothermal-Isobaric Molecular Dynamics with a Foundation Interatomic Potential in LAMMPS across NVIDIA and Intel GPUs
作者：Xiaoli Yan、Eliu Huerta。arXiv · physics，2026-11-04
🤖 AI/机器学习

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：基础机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 为许多化学提供了一种模型，但它们的深层全局耦合架构不适合缩放经典和短程 MLIP 的空间分解。我们提出了pair_style uma，这是一种 LAMMPS 对样式，可通过 NVIDIA 和 Intel GPU 上的 LibTorch 从单一源树评估通用原子模型 (UMA)。它通过导出的 TorchScript 模块在 C++ 中重新实现了 FairChem 的边缘分片图形并行性，每个设备、NCCL…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

基础机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 为许多化学提供了一种模型，但它们的深层全局耦合架构不适合缩放经典和短程 MLIP 的空间分解。我们提出了pair_style uma，这是一种 LAMMPS 对样式，可通过 NVIDIA 和 Intel GPU 上的 LibTorch 从单一源树评估通用原子模型 (UMA)。它通过导出的 TorchScript 模块在 C++ 中重新实现了 FairChem 的边缘分片图形并行性，每个设备、NCCL 或 oneCCL 集合具有一个 MPI 等级，并且整个能量和力路径采用双精度。应变梯度维里通过导出的模块和跨设备缩减进行，以便修复 npt 在两个供应商的硬件上运行。我们测量了 1 个 4xA100-40GB 节点、1 个 8xH200 节点、1 个 12 块 Intel 节点以及最多 32 个 Intel 节点上的 NPT 容量和成本。如果没有激活检查点，一个节点可容纳约 8,000 个原子（A100，0.21 ns/天）、22,000 个原子（Intel，0.10 ns/天）和 47,000 个原子（H200，0.16 ns/天）的 NaCl 单元。图并行性使 H200 上单个设备的容量提高了 8.0 倍，A100 上提高了 4.6 倍。在各个节点上，32 个英特尔节点上的容量增长到约 779,000 个原子，速度较慢，为 0.007 纳秒/天。在 H200 和 A100 上，FairChem 的图并行预测器的 FLOP 计数给出 FP64 峰值的 31% 和 51% 下限。从一个 omat 检查点开始，在没有改装的情况下，九个 100 ps NPT 加热斜坡（NaCl、等原子 Pt-Ru 和等原子 (HfNbTaTiZr)N，在所有三种节点类型上）运行完成。

English Abstract

Foundation machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) offer one model for many chemistries, but their deep, globally coupled architectures do not fit the spatial decomposition that scales classical and short-ranged MLIPs. We present pair_style uma, a LAMMPS pair style that evaluates the Universal Model for Atoms (UMA) through LibTorch on NVIDIA and Intel GPUs from a single source tree. It reimplements FairChem's edge-sharded graph parallelism in C++ over exported TorchScript modules, with one MPI rank per device, NCCL or oneCCL collectives, and the whole energy and force path in double precision. The strain-gradient virial is carried through the exported modules and the cross-device reductions, so that fix npt runs on both vendors' hardware. We measure NPT capacity and cost on one 4xA100-40GB node, one 8xH200 node, and one 12-tile Intel node, and on up to 32 Intel nodes. Without activation checkpointing, one node holds NaCl cells of about 8,000 atoms (A100, 0.21 ns/day), 22,000 atoms (Intel, 0.10 ns/day), and 47,000 atoms (H200, 0.16 ns/day). Graph parallelism raises the capacity of a single device by 8.0x on H200 and 4.6x on A100. Across nodes, capacity grows to about 779,000 atoms on 32 Intel nodes, with a slower speed at 0.007 ns/day. On H200 and A100, a FLOP count of FairChem's graph-parallel predictor gives lower bounds of 31% and 51% of FP64 peak. From one omat checkpoint and with no refit, nine 100 ps NPT heating ramps (NaCl, equiatomic Pt-Ru, and equiatomic (HfNbTaTiZr)N, on all three node types) ran to completion.

原题：Anion Engineering Enables Concurrent Optimization of Phonon and Carrier Transport in AMg₂X₂ Zintl Thermoelectrics

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

同时抑制晶格热传输和增强载流子传输是高性能热电材料开发的核心。在这里，我们利用第一性原理计算、机器学习原子间势和玻尔兹曼输运理论研究了 CaAl2Si2 型 AMg2X2（A = Ca、Sr、Ba；X = P、As、Sb、Bi）Zintl 化合物中控制声子和载流子输运的微观机制。当X从P变为Bi时，声子带隙变宽限制了三声子散射通道并增加了四声子过程的相对重要性。在 CaMg2Bi2 和 BaMg2Bi2 中，四声子散射在 900 K 时主导低频载热模式的弛豫。尽管阳离子质量增加，Bi 基系列还表现出从 Ca 到 Ba 的晶格热导率异常增加。模式解析分析将 CaMg2Bi2 的较低热导率与较低的低频群速度和较强的非简声散射联系起来，而与 Ca 相关的中频振动提供了额外的四声子散射通道。包括颗粒状和共格贡献，其晶格热导率在 900 K 时达到 0.23 W·m-1K-1。同时，垂直 Mg-X 键共价特性的增加伴随着带边色散的增强和载流子有效质量的降低。这些电子变化与导带会聚一起，有利于 n 型输运，并在 CaMg2Bi2 中产生 900 K 时预测的峰值 ZT 为 3.68。这些结果阐明了热传输中声子谱结构和高阶非谐性的相互作用，以及对阴离子取代的独特电子响应。

English Abstract

The simultaneous suppression of lattice heat transport and enhancement of carrier transport is central to the development of high-performance thermoelectric materials. Here, we investigate the microscopic mechanisms governing phonon and carrier transport in CaAl₂Si₂-type AMg₂X₂ (A = Ca, Sr, Ba; X = P, As, Sb, Bi) Zintl compounds using first-principles calculations, machine-learning interatomic potentials, and Boltzmann transport theory. As X changes from P to Bi, the widening phonon band gap restricts three-phonon scattering channels and increases the relative importance of four-phonon processes. In CaMg₂Bi₂ and BaMg₂Bi₂, four-phonon scattering dominates the relaxation of low-frequency heat-carrying modes at 900 K. The Bi-based series also exhibits an anomalous increase in lattice thermal conductivity from Ca to Ba despite the increasing cation mass. Mode-resolved analysis links the lower thermal conductivity of CaMg₂Bi₂ to reduced low-frequency group velocities and stronger anharmonic scattering, with Ca-related mid-frequency vibrations providing additional four-phonon scattering channels. Including both particle-like and coherent contributions, its lattice thermal conductivity reaches 0.23 W·m⁻¹K⁻¹ at 900 K. Meanwhile, the increasing covalent character of the vertical Mg-X bonds is accompanied by enhanced band-edge dispersion and reduced carrier effective masses. These electronic changes, together with conduction-band convergence, favor n-type transport and yield a predicted peak ZT of 3.68 at 900 K in CaMg₂Bi₂. These results elucidate the interplay of phonon spectral structure and higher-order anharmonicity in thermal transport, alongside the distinct electronic response to anion substitution.

中文摘要

图形数据提供了许多化学和材料系统背后的互连结构和相互作用的自然表示。图神经网络（GNN）可以从这些数据中学习复杂的关系，但将这些表示转化为可解释的描述符和科学见解仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们介绍图符号蒸馏（GSD），一个用于将图数据解码为可解释的符号公式的框架。 GSD 识别与任务相关的消息传递架构，并提取其学习到的转换，同时保留图连接和聚合操作。在涵盖块体材料、分子和表面的三个案例研究中，GSD 不仅从基本节点和边缘特征中恢复了专家定义的描述符，而且还揭示了复杂图中它们的组织中出现的关系：γ-CsPbI₃ 中带隙变化的极值局部几何描述符、水合自由能基础上的局部极性-尺寸关系，以及多齿吸附中的位点特异性电子连接耦合。因此，GSD 提供了从图形数据到可解释的描述符和新的、可测试的科学见解的一般途径。

English Abstract

Graph data provide a natural representation of the interconnected structures and interactions underlying many chemical and materials systems. Graph neural networks (GNNs) can learn complex relationships from such data, yet translating these representations into interpretable descriptors and scientific insights remains challenging. Here we introduce graph symbolic distillation (GSD), a framework for decoding graph data into interpretable symbolic formulas. GSD identifies task-relevant message-passing architectures and distills their learned transformations while preserving graph connectivity and aggregation operations. Across three case studies spanning bulk materials, molecules, and surfaces, GSD not only recovers expert-defined descriptors from elementary node and edge features, but also reveals relationships emerging from their organization in complex graphs: extremal local geometric descriptors for band gap variations in γ-CsPbI₃, a local polarity--size relationship underlying hydration free energy, and site-specific electronic--connectivity coupling in multidentate adsorption. GSD thus provides a general route from graph data to interpretable descriptors and new, testable scientific insights.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习原子间势（MLIP）的快速发展，特别是材料化学的基础模型（例如 MACE），显着增强了我们通过统计采样研究材料复杂动力学的能力。在这里，通过使用微调的 MACE 模型执行分子动力学，并进一步与基本动力学分析（生物）分子模拟中常用的工具相结合，我们检查了原型无机固体超离子导体（即银汞矿 Li₆PS₅Cl）中温度依赖性离子动力学。我们的分析表明，500 K 以下的 Li⁺ 离子具有清晰的局域声子动力学，其轨迹位于高度非线性的低维流形中，这与高温 (sim800 K) 端的情况相反。在 500-700 K 之间，该系统在 Li⁺ 和骨架离子之间的动力学方面表现出强烈的竞争，这反映在特征谱和表征这些动力学的投影低维嵌入中，没有表现出对温度升高的简单单调依赖性，并且由于 MLIP 提供的计算效率，采用朗之万动力学的研究进一步支持了该研究，系统地检查了骨架运动阻尼如何影响 Li⁺ 离子的动力学。因此，这项工作有助于建立更广泛的理论和数据驱动框架来研究固体晶格动力学。

English Abstract

The rapid growth of machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIP), particularly the foundational models for materials chemistry, such as MACE, has significantly strengthened our capabilities in studying the complex dynamics of materials through statistical sampling. Here, by performing molecular dynamics with a fine-tuned MACE model, that are further combined with essential dynamics analysis, a tool that is commonly applied in (bio)molecular simulations, we examine the temperature-dependent ion dynamics in a prototypical inorganic solid superionic conductor, namely argyrodite Li₆PS₅Cl. Our analyses show clear localised phonon-like dynamics for Li⁺ ions below 500 K with trajectories lying in a low-dimensional manifold that is highly nonlinear, which is opposite to the case in the high-temperature (sim800 K) end. Between 500-700 K, the system exhibit strong competition in the dynamics between the Li⁺ and framework ions, that are reflected in both the eigenspectrum and the projected low-dimensional embeddings characterising these dynamics not showing a simple monotonic dependency upon rising temperatures, and is further supported by investigations employing Langevin dynamics that systematically examine how damping the framework motions affects the dynamics of the Li⁺ ions, thanks to the computational efficiency provided by MLIP. This work thus contributes towards the broader theoretical and data-driven framework for studying the lattice dynamics of solids.

机器学习潜力何时需要依赖于配置的内核？学习与傅立叶神经算子的远程交互#

原题：When do machine-learning potentials need a configuration-dependent kernel? Learning long-range interactions with Fourier neural operators

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习原子间势中的长程相互作用通常通过通过规定的静电核或学习但与配置无关的核耦合学习源来处理。这样的内核是否足够取决于底层的屏蔽交互。我们通过使用傅里叶神经算子 (FNO) 来解决这个问题，以了解根据本地骨干网（此处为 MACE）的描述符构建的潜在源场如何在模拟单元中相互作用。对于具有可调谐屏蔽的合成离子系统，规定的库仑核在强屏蔽下将长波长电荷波动低估了 43%，而 FNO 在 3% 内再现了它们，并恢复了本地主干留下的残差方差的 95-99%。学习独立于配置的内核的线性 FNO 在同质情况下表现同样出色。在非均匀筛选下，重新排列远程介质会调节远程离子之间的耦合，这是任何与配置无关的内核都无法表示的依赖性。在测试的模型中，只有非线性FNO响应这种调制，其力误差比与配置无关的模型低27-41%。在液态水、锆酸锂镧和 Au2/MgO(001) 的第一性原理基准测试中，MACE-FNO 与体系统的规定内核模型相当，并且界面更准确。它进一步捕获了由远程 Al 掺杂剂引起的 Au2 润湿偏好的逆转，与密度泛函理论相比，每个细胞的偏差低于 5 meV。这些结果表明，一旦屏蔽介质发生变化，即使固定介质的能量仍然是电荷的二次方，FNO 所学到的依赖于配置的内核就变得必要。

English Abstract

Long-range interactions in machine-learning interatomic potentials are commonly treated by coupling learned sources through either a prescribed electrostatic kernel or a learned but configuration-independent one. Whether such a kernel suffices depends on the underlying screened interaction. We address this question by using Fourier neural operators (FNOs) to learn how latent source fields, built from the descriptors of a local backbone (here MACE), interact across the simulation cell. For a synthetic ionic system with tunable screening, a prescribed Coulomb kernel underestimates the long-wavelength charge fluctuations by 43% under strong screening, whereas the FNO reproduces them within 3% and recovers 95-99% of the variance of the residual left by the local backbone. A linear FNO, which learns a configuration-independent kernel, performs equally well in the homogeneous case. Under inhomogeneous screening, rearranging the remote medium modulates the coupling between distant ions, a dependence that no configuration-independent kernel can represent. Among the models tested, only the nonlinear FNO responds to this modulation, and its force errors are 27-41% lower than those of configuration-independent models. In first-principles benchmarks of liquid water, lithium lanthanum zirconate, and Au2/MgO(001), MACE-FNO is comparable to a prescribed-kernel model for the bulk systems and more accurate for the interface. It further captures the reversal of the Au2 wetting preference induced by remote Al dopants, with deviations below 5 meV per cell from density functional theory. These results indicate that a configuration-dependent kernel, as learned by FNOs, becomes necessary once the screening medium varies, even though the energy remains quadratic in the charges for a fixed medium.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

提出了一种使用玻尔兹曼构象异构体作为输入的化学空间机器学习的计算高效协议；该方法基于根据结构化正交随机特征重写核岭回归表达式，产生物理驱动的三角神经网络。为了评估该方法在材料发现方面的实用性，我们在与电池电解质设计相关的两个量（即乙腈中的氧化电位和水合能）的实验数据集上对其进行了测试，并使用几种基于几何和轨道的分子表示来证明该方法的灵活性。尽管仅使用计算成本低的力场计算来生成构象异构体并通过 GFN2-xTB 计算生成轨道表示，但我们观察到在所有情况下随着训练集大小的增加，预测误差都会系统性减少，并且在对数百个分子进行训练后达到了实验精度。

English Abstract

A computationally efficient protocol for machine learning in chemical space using Boltzmann ensembles of conformers as input is proposed; the method is based on rewriting Kernel Ridge Regression expressions in terms of Structured Orthogonal Random Features, yielding physics-motivated trigonometric neural networks. To evaluate the method's utility for materials discovery, we test it on experimental datasets of two quantities related to battery electrolyte design, namely oxidation potentials in acetonitrile and hydration energies, using several geometry- and orbital-based molecular representations to demonstrate the method's flexibility. Despite only using computationally cheap force field calculations for conformer generation and generating orbital representations with GFN2-xTB calculations, we observe systematic decrease of prediction errors with increased training set size in all cases, with experimental accuracy reached after training on hundreds of molecules.
